=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\BattleDebugInspectors.cs ===
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(BattleSession))]
    public sealed class BattleSessionInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        public override bool RequiresConstantRepaint() => Application.isPlaying;
        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            DrawDefaultInspector();
            var session = (BattleSession)target;
            if (GUILayout.Button("Validate Scene"))
            {
                var errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
                if (errors.Count == 0) Debug.Log("Battle Scene is valid.", session);
                else Debug.LogError(string.Join("\n", errors), session);
            }
            if (Application.isPlaying && GUILayout.Button("Start / Restart Battle")) session.StartBattle();
            var simulation = session.Simulation;
            if (simulation == null) return;
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"{simulation.Outcome} | {simulation.ElapsedTime:F2}s | Lives {simulation.LivesRemaining}");
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Spawned {simulation.SpawnedCount}  Alive {simulation.AliveCount}  Dead {simulation.KilledCount}  Goal {simulation.ReachedGoalCount}");
            foreach (var tower in simulation.Towers)
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Tower {tower.Id}: HP {tower.Hp:F1}, cooldown {tower.CooldownRemaining:F2}, target {tower.TargetId?.ToString() ?? "-"}, {tower.Position}");
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"#{unit.MemberId} {unit.Role} {unit.LifeState} | HP {unit.Hp:F1}/{unit.MaxHp:F1} | {unit.Position} | Squad {unit.SquadId} Slot {unit.SlotId} Cell {unit.OccupiedCell}");
        }
    }

    [CustomEditor(typeof(BattleTower))]
    public sealed class BattleTowerInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        public override bool RequiresConstantRepaint() => Application.isPlaying;
        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            DrawDefaultInspector();
            var state = ((BattleTower)target).RuntimeState;
            if (state != null) EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Cooldown {state.CooldownRemaining:F2} | Target {state.TargetId?.ToString() ?? "-"}");
        }
    }

    public static class BattleDebugLabels
    {
        [DrawGizmo(GizmoType.Selected | GizmoType.NonSelected)]
        private static void DrawLabels(BattleDebugGizmos view, GizmoType type)
        {
            if (!view.enabled || view.Session == null) return;
            var session = view.Session;
            if (session.Route != null)
            {
                for (var i = 0; i < session.Route.Cells.Count; i++)
                {
                    var cell = session.Route.Cells[i];
                    if (cell != null) Handles.Label(cell.TopCenter + Vector3.up * 0.1f, $"{i}: {cell.Kind}");
                }
            }
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
                if (tower != null) Handles.Label(tower.transform.position + Vector3.up, $"{tower.name} R={tower.Range:F1} CD={tower.CurrentCooldown:F1}");
            if (session.Simulation == null) return;
            foreach (var unit in session.Simulation.Units)
                if (unit.IsAlive) Handles.Label(unit.Position + Vector3.up * 0.6f, $"#{unit.Id} HP {unit.Hp:F0}");
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\BattleGridInspector.cs ===
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(BattleGrid))]
    public sealed class BattleGridInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        private Vector3Int macro;
        public override bool RequiresConstantRepaint() => Application.isPlaying;
        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            DrawDefaultInspector();
            var grid = (BattleGrid)target;
            macro = EditorGUILayout.Vector3IntField("Macro (X, layer, Z)", macro);
            var errors = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();
            grid.RebuildIndex(errors);
            var session = Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleSession>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).FirstOrDefault(value => value.Grid == grid);
            var cells = grid.GetMacroCells(macro);
            var occupancy = session?.Simulation == null ? null : grid.GetMacroOccupancy(macro, session.Simulation);
            for (var i = 0; i < cells.Length; i++)
            {
                EditorGUILayout.ObjectField($"Small ({i % 3},{i / 3})", cells[i], typeof(BattleCell), true);
                if (occupancy != null)
                    EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Towers: {string.Join(",", occupancy[i].TowerIds)}  Units: {string.Join(",", occupancy[i].UnitIds)}");
            }
            foreach (var error in errors) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(error, MessageType.Error);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\DecorativeBorderSetup.cs ===
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    // Edit-time authoring only. Border meshes are siblings of BattleGrid, with no combat components.
    public static class DecorativeBorderSetup
    {
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Add Decorative Border Tiles")]
        public static void AddThreeRings()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying || SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path != PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Open GridBattlePrototype.unity in Edit Mode first.");
            var session = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var before = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var cells = session.Grid.SceneCells;
            var lowestLayer = cells.Min(cell => cell.Occupancy.y);
            var baseCells = cells.Where(cell => cell.Occupancy.y == lowestLayer).ToArray();
            var minX = baseCells.Min(cell => cell.Occupancy.x);
            var maxX = baseCells.Max(cell => cell.Occupancy.x);
            var minZ = baseCells.Min(cell => cell.Occupancy.z);
            var maxZ = baseCells.Max(cell => cell.Occupancy.z);
            var existingRoot = session.Grid.transform.parent.Find("Decorative Border Tiles");
            var root = existingRoot != null ? existingRoot.gameObject : new GameObject("Decorative Border Tiles");
            root.transform.SetParent(session.Grid.transform.parent, true);
            root.layer = 2; // Ignore Raycast, in addition to having no colliders.
            var material = BorderMaterial();
            for (var z = minZ - 3; z <= maxZ + 3; z++)
            for (var x = minX - 3; x <= maxX + 3; x++)
            {
                if (x >= minX && x <= maxX && z >= minZ && z <= maxZ) continue;
                var name = $"Border Tile ({x},{z})";
                var existing = root.transform.Find(name);
                var tile = existing != null ? existing.gameObject : GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
                tile.name = name;
                tile.transform.SetParent(root.transform, false);
                tile.transform.position = new Vector3(x, lowestLayer * BattleCell.LayerHeight, z);
                tile.transform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
                tile.transform.localScale = BattleCell.Size;
                tile.layer = 2;
                foreach (var collider in tile.GetComponents<Collider>()) UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(collider);
                var renderer = tile.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();
                renderer.sharedMaterial = material;
                renderer.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
                renderer.receiveShadows = false;
            }
            // Validate isolation before saving: no new grid occupancy or changed route/tower configuration.
            var after = session.CaptureDefinition();
            if (session.Grid.SceneCells.Length != cells.Length || !before.Waypoints.SequenceEqual(after.Waypoints) ||
                !before.Towers.Select(tower => tower.Position).SequenceEqual(after.Towers.Select(tower => tower.Position)) ||
                root.GetComponentsInChildren<BattleCell>(true).Length != 0 || root.GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>(true).Length != 0)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Decorative border must remain isolated from battle entities and physics.");
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            EditorSceneManager.MarkSceneDirty(session.gameObject.scene);
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(session.gameObject.scene);
            Debug.Log($"Saved {root.transform.childCount} Decorative Border Tiles: three rings, no BattleCell or Collider; battle grid remains {cells.Length} cells.");
        }

        private static Material BorderMaterial()
        {
            const string path = MinimalPresentationSetup.MaterialFolder + "/DecorativeBorder.mat";
            var material = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Material>(path);
            if (material != null) return material;
            Directory.CreateDirectory(MinimalPresentationSetup.MaterialFolder);
            material = new Material(Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit")) { name = "Decorative Border" };
            material.SetColor("_BaseColor", new Color(0.28f, 0.37f, 0.29f));
            material.SetFloat("_Smoothness", 0.15f);
            AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(material, path);
            return material;
        }
    }
}

using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    public static class GameMenuScenes
    {
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Start Game")]
        public static void OpenStart() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open End Game")]
        public static void OpenEnd() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.EndScene);
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Upgrade Scene")]
        public static void OpenUpgrade() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 2 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelTwo() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 3 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelThree() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelThreeScene);
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 4 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelFour() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelFourScene);
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level Settlement")]
        public static void OpenSettlement() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
        private static void Open(string path)
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying) { Debug.LogWarning("Exit Play Mode before opening a scene."); return; }
            if (EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(path);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\MacroPrototypeSceneBuilder.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.Rendering.Universal;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    // One-time Edit Mode scene authoring. Existing source and expanded scenes are never overwritten.
    public static class MacroPrototypeSceneBuilder
    {
        public const string ScenePath = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/GridBattleMacroPrototype.unity";

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Create Macro Grid Prototype")]
        public static void Create()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying) throw new InvalidOperationException("Exit Play Mode first.");
            if (File.Exists(ScenePath)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Expanded scene already exists; edit that Scene directly.");
            if (SceneManager.GetActiveScene().isDirty) throw new InvalidOperationException("Save the current Scene before authoring a separate expanded scene.");
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath);
            var source = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var errors = source.ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count != 0) throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("\n", errors));
            var cells = source.Grid.SceneCells.Select(cell => new
            {
                Key = cell.Occupancy, cell.Kind, Material = cell.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial
            }).ToArray();
            var routeKeys = source.Route.Cells.Select(cell => cell.Occupancy).ToArray();
            var towers = source.Towers.Select((tower, i) => new
            {
                Definition = tower.CaptureDefinition(i + 1), Material = tower.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial
            }).ToArray();
            var borderRoot = source.Grid.transform.parent.Find("Decorative Border Tiles");
            var decoration = borderRoot == null ? Array.Empty<(Vector3 position, Material material)>() :
                borderRoot.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>().Select(mesh => (position: mesh.transform.position, material: mesh.sharedMaterial)).ToArray();
            var prefabPath = AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattlePresentation>().UnitPrefab);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(prefabPath)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Source scene needs a saved UnitView prefab asset.");
            var sourceCamera = source.transform.parent.GetComponentInChildren<Camera>();
            var cameraRotation = sourceCamera.transform.rotation;
            var cameraFov = sourceCamera.fieldOfView;
            var cameraBackground = sourceCamera.backgroundColor;

            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var root = new GameObject("Macro Grid Battle Prototype");
            var grid = Child(root, "GameplayGrid").AddComponent<BattleGrid>();
            var lookup = new Dictionary<Vector3Int, BattleCell>();
            foreach (var old in cells)
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 3; x++)
            {
                var key = new Vector3Int(old.Key.x * 3 + x, old.Key.y, old.Key.z * 3 + z);
                var cell = Child(grid.gameObject, $"Small Cell ({key.x},{key.y},{key.z}) {old.Kind}").AddComponent<BattleCell>();
                cell.transform.position = new Vector3(key.x, key.y * BattleCell.LayerHeight, key.z);
                Set(cell, "kind", (int)old.Kind);
                Mesh(cell.gameObject, "Cell Mesh", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.zero, BattleCell.Size, old.Material);
                lookup.Add(key, cell);
            }

            // Each stored waypoint is a real Small Cell reference, including intermediate centerline cells.
            var centers = routeKeys.Select(key => new Vector3Int(key.x * 3 + 1, key.y, key.z * 3 + 1)).ToArray();
            var routeCells = new List<BattleCell> { lookup[centers[0]] };
            for (var i = 1; i < centers.Length; i++)
            {
                var cursor = centers[i - 1];
                var delta = centers[i] - cursor;
                var direction = new Vector3Int(Math.Sign(delta.x), 0, Math.Sign(delta.z));
                while (cursor != centers[i]) { cursor += direction; routeCells.Add(lookup[cursor]); }
            }
            var route = Child(root, "SceneRoute (3 Wide)").AddComponent<SceneRoute>();
            SetReferences(route, "cells", routeCells.Cast<UnityEngine.Object>().ToArray());
            Set(route, "requiredWidth", 3);
            var towerRoot = Child(root, "Scene Towers (3x3 Footprints)");
            var towerList = new List<BattleTower>();
            foreach (var old in towers)
            {
                var definition = old.Definition;
                var tower = Child(towerRoot, $"Tower {definition.Id} (3x3)").AddComponent<BattleTower>();
                tower.transform.position = new Vector3(Mathf.RoundToInt(definition.Position.x) * 3 + 1,
                    definition.Position.y, Mathf.RoundToInt(definition.Position.z) * 3 + 1);
                Set(tower, "usesMacroFootprint", true);
                // World-distance rebalance: 4.5, deliberately not the old range times three (7.2).
                Set(tower, "range", 4.5f);
                Set(tower, "damage", definition.Damage);
                Set(tower, "attackInterval", definition.AttackInterval);
                Set(tower, "targetRule", (int)definition.TargetRule);
                Mesh(tower.gameObject, "Tower Mesh", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.6f, new Vector3(2.8f, 1.2f, 2.8f), old.Material);
                towerList.Add(tower);
            }
            var decorativeRoot = Child(root, "Decorative Border Tiles");
            decorativeRoot.layer = 2;
            foreach (var old in decoration)
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 3; x++)
            {
                var position = new Vector3(old.position.x * 3 + x, old.position.y, old.position.z * 3 + z);
                var mesh = Mesh(decorativeRoot, $"Border Tile ({position.x},{position.z})", PrimitiveType.Cube,
                    position, BattleCell.Size, old.material);
                mesh.layer = 2;
            }
            var session = Child(root, "BattleSession (Nine Independent Units)").AddComponent<BattleSession>();
            Set(session, "grid", grid);
            Set(session, "route", route);
            Set(session, "unitCount", 9);
            Set(session, "formationSize", 3);
            Set(session, "unitSpeed", 2.4f);
            Set(session, "spawnInterval", 3f);
            Set(session, "goalLives", 9);
            SetReferences(session, "towers", towerList.Cast<UnityEngine.Object>().ToArray());
            var presentation = Child(root, "Minimal Presentation (Runtime Unit Views)").AddComponent<BattlePresentation>();
            Set(presentation, "session", session);
            // Resolve the asset again after switching scenes; do not retain the old scene's loaded component reference.
            var prefabObject = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(prefabPath);
            var prefab = prefabObject != null ? prefabObject.GetComponent<UnitView>() : null;
            if (prefab == null) throw new InvalidOperationException($"UnitView prefab could not be loaded: {prefabPath}");
            Set(presentation, "unitPrefab", prefab);
            var gizmos = Child(root, "Optional Debug Gizmos").AddComponent<BattleDebugGizmos>();
            Set(gizmos, "session", session);
            var light = Child(root, "Presentation Light").AddComponent<Light>();
            light.type = LightType.Directional;
            light.intensity = 1.2f;
            light.shadows = LightShadows.None;
            light.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(50, -35, 0);
            var camera = Child(root, "Battle Camera (Perspective 16x9)").AddComponent<Camera>();
            camera.tag = "MainCamera";
            camera.orthographic = false;
            camera.fieldOfView = cameraFov;
            camera.transform.rotation = cameraRotation;
            camera.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
            camera.backgroundColor = cameraBackground;
            camera.GetUniversalAdditionalCameraData().SetRenderer(-1);
            FitCamera(camera, root);
            errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count != 0) throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("\n", errors));
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, ScenePath)) throw new IOException("Failed to save macro scene.");
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            var buildScenes = EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Where(entry => entry.path != ScenePath).ToList();
            buildScenes.Insert(0, new EditorBuildSettingsScene(ScenePath, true));
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = buildScenes.ToArray();
            Debug.Log($"Saved {ScenePath}: {lookup.Count} Small Cells, {routeCells.Count} real route references, 3 macro towers, 9-unit formation, {decoration.Length * 9} decorative tiles.");
        }

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Macro Grid Prototype")]
        public static void Open()
        {
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) return;
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(ScenePath);
        }

        private static void FitCamera(Camera camera, GameObject root)
        {
            var renderers = root.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>();
            var bounds = renderers[0].bounds;
            foreach (var renderer in renderers) bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
            var tanVertical = Mathf.Tan(camera.fieldOfView * 0.5f * Mathf.Deg2Rad);
            var tanHorizontal = tanVertical * (16f / 9f);
            var distance = 0f;
            for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
            for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
            {
                var offset = Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z));
                var depth = Vector3.Dot(offset, camera.transform.forward);
                distance = Mathf.Max(distance, Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(offset, camera.transform.right)) * 1.08f / tanHorizontal - depth,
                    Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(offset, camera.transform.up)) * 1.08f / tanVertical - depth);
            }
            camera.transform.position = bounds.center - camera.transform.forward * (distance + 2);
            camera.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
            camera.farClipPlane = distance + bounds.size.magnitude + 20;
        }

        private static GameObject Child(GameObject parent, string name)
        {
            var child = new GameObject(name);
            child.transform.SetParent(parent.transform, false);
            return child;
        }
        private static GameObject Mesh(GameObject parent, string name, PrimitiveType type, Vector3 position, Vector3 size, Material material)
        {
            var child = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(type);
            child.name = name;
            child.transform.SetParent(parent.transform, false);
            child.transform.localPosition = position;
            child.transform.localScale = size;
            UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(child.GetComponent<Collider>());
            var renderer = child.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();
            renderer.sharedMaterial = material;
            renderer.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
            renderer.receiveShadows = false;
            return child;
        }
        private static void Set(UnityEngine.Object target, string field, object value)
        {
            var data = new SerializedObject(target);
            var property = data.FindProperty(field);
            if (value is bool boolean) property.boolValue = boolean;
            else if (value is int integer) property.intValue = integer;
            else if (value is float number) property.floatValue = number;
            else property.objectReferenceValue = (UnityEngine.Object)value;
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
        }
        private static void SetReferences(UnityEngine.Object target, string field, UnityEngine.Object[] values)
        {
            var data = new SerializedObject(target);
            var property = data.FindProperty(field);
            property.arraySize = values.Length;
            for (var i = 0; i < values.Length; i++) property.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).objectReferenceValue = values[i];
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\MinimalPresentationSetup.cs ===
using System;
using System.IO;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.Rendering.Universal;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    // Editor-only authoring: add mesh children to the existing Scene entities, never clone the map.
    public static class MinimalPresentationSetup
    {
        public const string UnitPrefabPath = "Assets/Rebuild/Prefabs/MinimalUnitView.prefab";
        public const string MaterialFolder = "Assets/Rebuild/Materials/Minimal";

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Apply Minimal Presentation")]
        public static void ApplyToOpenPrototype()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying) throw new InvalidOperationException("Exit Play Mode before authoring the Scene.");
            if (UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path != PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Open GridBattlePrototype.unity before applying presentation.");
            Apply(UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>());
        }

        public static void ApplyToSavedPrototype()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath);
            Apply(UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>());
        }

        private static void Apply(BattleSession session)
        {
            if (session == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("No BattleSession in prototype Scene.");
            var errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count > 0) throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("\n", errors));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(MaterialFolder);
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Assets/Rebuild/Prefabs");
            AssetDatabase.Refresh();
            var materials = new Material[6];
            foreach (CellKind kind in Enum.GetValues(typeof(CellKind)))
                materials[(int)kind] = MaterialAsset(kind.ToString(), CellColor(kind));
            var towerMaterial = MaterialAsset("Tower", new Color(0.24f, 0.48f, 0.9f));
            foreach (var cell in session.Grid.SceneCells)
                MeshChild(cell.transform, "Cell Mesh", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.zero, BattleCell.Size, materials[(int)cell.Kind]);
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
                MeshChild(tower.transform, "Tower Mesh", PrimitiveType.Cylinder, Vector3.up * 0.5f, new Vector3(0.55f, 0.5f, 0.55f), towerMaterial);

            var prefab = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<UnitView>(UnitPrefabPath);
            if (prefab == null)
            {
                UpdateSquareUnitPrefab();
                prefab = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<UnitView>(UnitPrefabPath);
            }
            var presentation = session.transform.parent.GetComponentInChildren<BattlePresentation>(true);
            if (presentation == null)
            {
                var root = new GameObject("Minimal Presentation (Runtime Unit Views)");
                root.transform.SetParent(session.transform.parent, false);
                presentation = root.AddComponent<BattlePresentation>();
            }
            var data = new SerializedObject(presentation);
            data.FindProperty("session").objectReferenceValue = session;
            data.FindProperty("unitPrefab").objectReferenceValue = prefab;
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();

            var camera = session.transform.parent.GetComponentInChildren<Camera>(true);
            camera.name = "Battle Camera";
            camera.transform.position = new Vector3(5.5f, 15, -7.5f);
            camera.transform.LookAt(new Vector3(5.5f, 0, 3.5f));
            camera.orthographic = true;
            camera.orthographicSize = 6.6f;
            camera.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
            camera.farClipPlane = 100;
            camera.GetUniversalAdditionalCameraData().SetRenderer(-1); // Existing URP Universal 3D Renderer.
            camera.GetUniversalAdditionalCameraData().renderPostProcessing = false;
            var lightTransform = session.transform.parent.Find("Presentation Light");
            var lightObject = lightTransform != null ? lightTransform.gameObject : new GameObject("Presentation Light");
            lightObject.transform.SetParent(session.transform.parent, false);
            lightObject.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(50, -35, 0);
            var light = lightObject.GetComponent<Light>();
            if (light == null) light = lightObject.AddComponent<Light>();
            light.type = LightType.Directional;
            light.intensity = 1.2f;
            light.shadows = LightShadows.None;
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            EditorSceneManager.MarkSceneDirty(session.gameObject.scene);
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(session.gameObject.scene);
            Debug.Log("Minimal Presentation saved: static mesh children and square unit view prefab are ready.");
        }

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Update Square Unit View")]
        public static void UpdateSquareUnitPrefab()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying) throw new InvalidOperationException("Exit Play Mode before editing the unit prefab.");
            Directory.CreateDirectory(MaterialFolder);
            var materialPath = MaterialFolder + "/UnitSquare.mat";
            var material = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Material>(materialPath);
            if (material == null)
            {
                material = new Material(Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit")) { name = "UnitSquare" };
                material.SetColor("_BaseColor", Color.white);
                AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(material, materialPath);
            }
            var exists = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(UnitPrefabPath) != null;
            var root = exists ? PrefabUtility.LoadPrefabContents(UnitPrefabPath) : new GameObject("MinimalUnitView");
            try
            {
                if (root.GetComponent<UnitView>() == null) root.AddComponent<UnitView>();
                // Preserve the prefab root/GUID and original child IDs, so all Scene references survive.
                var capsule = root.transform.Find("Capsule Mesh");
                if (capsule != null)
                {
                    capsule.name = "Outline";
                    var cube = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
                    capsule.GetComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh = cube.GetComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh;
                    UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(cube);
                }
                MeshChild(root.transform, "Outline", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.13f, new Vector3(0.8f, 0.26f, 0.8f), material);
                MeshChild(root.transform, "Body", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.26f, new Vector3(0.68f, 0.008f, 0.68f), material);
                MeshChild(root.transform, "Mark Diamond", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.268f, new Vector3(0.26f, 0.004f, 0.26f), material);
                root.transform.Find("Mark Diamond").localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 45, 0);
                MeshChild(root.transform, "Mark Plate", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.268f, new Vector3(0.32f, 0.004f, 0.32f), material);
                MeshChild(root.transform, "Mark Across", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.268f, new Vector3(0.44f, 0.004f, 0.14f), material);
                MeshChild(root.transform, "Mark Upright", PrimitiveType.Cube, Vector3.up * 0.269f, new Vector3(0.14f, 0.004f, 0.44f), material);
                root.GetComponent<UnitView>().ApplyAppearance(Team5.Rebuild.Simulation.UnitRole.Attack);
                PrefabUtility.SaveAsPrefabAsset(root, UnitPrefabPath);
            }
            finally
            {
                if (exists) PrefabUtility.UnloadPrefabContents(root); else UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(root);
            }
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            Debug.Log("Square unit view prefab saved.");
        }

        private static void MeshChild(Transform parent, string name, PrimitiveType primitive, Vector3 position, Vector3 scale, Material material)
        {
            var existing = parent.Find(name);
            var mesh = existing != null ? existing.gameObject : GameObject.CreatePrimitive(primitive);
            mesh.name = name;
            mesh.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            mesh.transform.localPosition = position;
            mesh.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            mesh.transform.localScale = scale;
            // Views never interact with physics or decide collision/combat outcomes.
            foreach (var collider in mesh.GetComponents<Collider>()) UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(collider);
            var renderer = mesh.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();
            renderer.sharedMaterial = material;
            renderer.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
            renderer.receiveShadows = false;
        }

        private static Material MaterialAsset(string name, Color color)
        {
            var path = $"{MaterialFolder}/{name}.mat";
            var material = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<Material>(path);
            if (material != null) return material;
            var shader = Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit");
            if (shader == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("The existing URP Lit shader is unavailable.");
            material = new Material(shader) { name = name };
            material.SetColor("_BaseColor", color);
            material.SetFloat("_Smoothness", 0.15f);
            AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(material, path);
            return material;
        }

        private static Color CellColor(CellKind kind)
        {
            switch (kind)
            {
                case CellKind.Road: return new Color(0.65f, 0.54f, 0.35f);
                case CellKind.Spawn: return new Color(0.9f, 0.25f, 0.25f);
                case CellKind.Goal: return new Color(0.2f, 0.75f, 0.9f);
                case CellKind.TowerPlatform: return new Color(0.35f, 0.4f, 0.65f);
                case CellKind.Obstacle: return new Color(0.35f, 0.32f, 0.3f);
                default: return new Color(0.38f, 0.53f, 0.39f);
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\PrototypeSceneBuilder.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    // Authoring utility only. Never called by Play Mode or a runtime assembly.
    public static class PrototypeSceneBuilder
    {
        public const string ScenePath = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/GridBattlePrototype.unity";

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Grid Battle Prototype")]
        public static void OpenPrototype()
        {
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) return;
            if (!File.Exists(ScenePath)) CreatePrototype();
            else EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(ScenePath);
            FrameScene();
        }

        // Used by the initial batch authoring run. Existing work is never regenerated.
        public static void CreatePrototype()
        {
            if (File.Exists(ScenePath)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Prototype already exists; edit its Scene directly.");
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes");
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Assets/Rebuild/Prefabs");
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            var root = new GameObject("Grid Battle Prototype");
            var grid = Child(root, "BattleGrid").AddComponent<BattleGrid>();
            var map = new Dictionary<Vector2Int, BattleCell>();
            var routeCoordinates = new List<Vector2Int>();
            for (var x = 0; x <= 4; x++) routeCoordinates.Add(new Vector2Int(x, 2));
            for (var z = 3; z <= 5; z++) routeCoordinates.Add(new Vector2Int(4, z));
            for (var x = 5; x <= 8; x++) routeCoordinates.Add(new Vector2Int(x, 5));
            for (var z = 4; z >= 2; z--) routeCoordinates.Add(new Vector2Int(8, z));
            for (var x = 9; x <= 11; x++) routeCoordinates.Add(new Vector2Int(x, 2));
            var platforms = new[] { new Vector2Int(2, 3), new Vector2Int(6, 4), new Vector2Int(10, 3), new Vector2Int(1, 5) };
            for (var z = 0; z < 8; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 12; x++)
            {
                var coordinate = new Vector2Int(x, z);
                var kind = routeCoordinates.Contains(coordinate) ? CellKind.Road : CellKind.Ground;
                if (coordinate == routeCoordinates[0]) kind = CellKind.Spawn;
                if (coordinate == routeCoordinates[routeCoordinates.Count - 1]) kind = CellKind.Goal;
                if (platforms.Contains(coordinate)) kind = CellKind.TowerPlatform;
                map.Add(coordinate, CreateCell(grid.gameObject, x, 0, z, kind));
            }
            CreateCell(grid.gameObject, 2, 1, 6, CellKind.Ground);
            CreateCell(grid.gameObject, 2, 2, 6, CellKind.Obstacle);
            CreateCell(grid.gameObject, 3, 1, 6, CellKind.Obstacle);
            CreateCell(grid.gameObject, 10, 1, 6, CellKind.Obstacle);
            var route = Child(root, "SceneRoute").AddComponent<SceneRoute>();
            SetReferences(route, "cells", routeCoordinates.Select(value => (UnityEngine.Object)map[value]).ToArray());
            var towerRoot = Child(root, "Scene Towers");
            var towerList = new List<BattleTower>();
            for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                var tower = Child(towerRoot, $"Tower {i + 1}").AddComponent<BattleTower>();
                tower.transform.position = map[platforms[i]].TopCenter;
                towerList.Add(tower);
            }
            var session = Child(root, "BattleSession").AddComponent<BattleSession>();
            var sessionData = new SerializedObject(session);
            sessionData.FindProperty("grid").objectReferenceValue = grid;
            sessionData.FindProperty("route").objectReferenceValue = route;
            sessionData.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            SetReferences(session, "towers", towerList.Cast<UnityEngine.Object>().ToArray());
            var debug = Child(root, "Optional Debug Gizmos").AddComponent<BattleDebugGizmos>();
            var debugData = new SerializedObject(debug);
            debugData.FindProperty("session").objectReferenceValue = session;
            debugData.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            // A plain camera makes Game View Gizmos usable; combat does not reference it.
            var camera = Child(root, "Debug Camera").AddComponent<Camera>();
            camera.tag = "MainCamera";
            camera.transform.position = new Vector3(5.5f, 15, -7.5f);
            camera.transform.LookAt(new Vector3(5.5f, 0, 3.5f));
            camera.orthographic = true;
            camera.orthographicSize = 7.8f;
            camera.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
            camera.backgroundColor = new Color(0.08f, 0.1f, 0.14f);
            camera.farClipPlane = 100;
            var errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count > 0) throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("\n", errors));
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, ScenePath)) throw new IOException("Failed to save prototype Scene.");
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            var buildScenes = EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Where(value => value.path != ScenePath).ToList();
            buildScenes.Insert(0, new EditorBuildSettingsScene(ScenePath, true));
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = buildScenes.ToArray();
            Debug.Log($"Created {ScenePath}: {grid.SceneCells.Length} static cells, {route.Cells.Count} route references, {towerList.Count} towers.");
            VerifySavedScene();
        }

        public static void VerifySavedScene()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(ScenePath, OpenSceneMode.Single);
            var session = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count > 0) throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("\n", errors));
            if (session.Grid.SceneCells.Length != 100 || session.Towers.Count != 3)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unexpected saved Scene contents.");
            Debug.Log("Saved Scene reopened and validated successfully.");
        }

        private static void FrameScene()
        {
            SceneView.lastActiveSceneView?.LookAt(new Vector3(5.5f, 0, 3.5f), Quaternion.Euler(55, 0, 0), 12);
        }
        private static GameObject Child(GameObject parent, string name)
        {
            var child = new GameObject(name);
            child.transform.SetParent(parent.transform, false);
            return child;
        }
        private static BattleCell CreateCell(GameObject parent, int x, int layer, int z, CellKind kind)
        {
            var cell = Child(parent, $"Cell ({x},{layer},{z}) {kind}").AddComponent<BattleCell>();
            cell.transform.position = new Vector3(x, layer * BattleCell.LayerHeight, z);
            var data = new SerializedObject(cell);
            data.FindProperty("kind").enumValueIndex = (int)kind;
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            return cell;
        }
        private static void SetReferences(UnityEngine.Object target, string field, UnityEngine.Object[] values)
        {
            var data = new SerializedObject(target);
            var list = data.FindProperty(field);
            list.arraySize = values.Length;
            for (var i = 0; i < values.Length; i++) list.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).objectReferenceValue = values[i];
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\PrototypeValidation.cs ===
using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.TestTools.TestRunner.Api;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    // A normal Unity Test Framework entry point for an already-open Editor.
    [InitializeOnLoad]
    public static class PrototypeValidation
    {
        private const string PendingKey = "Team5.Rebuild.ValidationPending";
        private static readonly TestRunnerApi Runner;
        static PrototypeValidation()
        {
            Runner = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<TestRunnerApi>();
            Runner.RegisterCallbacks(new Results());
        }

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Run Prototype EditMode Tests")]
        public static void RunEditMode() => Run(TestMode.EditMode);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Run Prototype PlayMode Tests")]
        public static void RunPlayMode() => Run(TestMode.PlayMode);

        private static void Run(TestMode mode)
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying) throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Exit Play Mode before running tests.");
            SessionState.SetBool(PendingKey, true);
            SessionState.SetString(PendingKey + ".Report", $"Logs/MinimalPresentation{mode}Results.xml");
            Runner.Execute(new ExecutionSettings(new Filter
            {
                testMode = mode,
                assemblyNames = new[] { "Team5.Rebuild.EditModeTests", "Team5.Rebuild.PlayModeTests" }
            }));
        }

        private sealed class Results : ICallbacks
        {
            public void RunStarted(ITestAdaptor testsToRun) { }
            public void TestStarted(ITestAdaptor test) { }
            public void TestFinished(ITestResultAdaptor result) { }
            public void RunFinished(ITestResultAdaptor result)
            {
                if (!SessionState.GetBool(PendingKey, false)) return;
                Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs");
                TestRunnerApi.SaveResultToFile(result, SessionState.GetString(PendingKey + ".Report", "Logs/MinimalPresentationResults.xml"));
                SessionState.SetBool(PendingKey, false);
                Debug.Log($"Prototype validation: {result.ResultState}, passed {result.PassCount}, failed {result.FailCount}.");
            }
        }
    }
}
