=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\BattleDebugGizmos.cs ===
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    // Optional observer only. Removing this entire component cannot change combat.
    public sealed class BattleDebugGizmos : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleSession session;
        [SerializeField] private bool showRanges = true;
        public BattleSession Session => session;

        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (!enabled || session == null || session.Grid == null) return;
            foreach (var cell in session.Grid.SceneCells)
            {
                Gizmos.color = CellColor(cell.Kind);
                Gizmos.DrawCube(cell.transform.position, BattleCell.Size * 0.96f);
                Gizmos.color = new Color(0, 0, 0, 0.6f);
                Gizmos.DrawWireCube(cell.transform.position, BattleCell.Size);
            }
            if (session.Route != null)
            {
                var cells = session.Route.Cells;
                Gizmos.color = Color.cyan;
                for (var i = 1; i < cells.Count; i++)
                    if (cells[i - 1] != null && cells[i] != null)
                        Gizmos.DrawLine(cells[i - 1].TopCenter + Vector3.up * 0.04f, cells[i].TopCenter + Vector3.up * 0.04f);
            }
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
            {
                if (tower == null) continue;
                Gizmos.color = new Color(0.3f, 0.6f, 1);
                Gizmos.DrawCube(tower.transform.position + Vector3.up * 0.4f, new Vector3(0.4f, 0.8f, 0.4f));
                Gizmos.DrawWireCube(tower.transform.position + Vector3.up * 0.03f,
                    new Vector3(tower.FootprintSize, 0.06f, tower.FootprintSize));
                if (showRanges)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(0.3f, 0.6f, 1, 0.3f);
                    var definition = tower.RuntimeState?.Definition ?? tower.CaptureDefinition(0);
                    var bounds = SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(tower.RuntimeState?.Position ?? tower.transform.position,
                        definition.Area, definition.Range, definition.AttackWidth);
                    Gizmos.DrawWireCube(bounds.center + Vector3.up * 0.035f, bounds.size);
                }
            }
            var simulation = session.Simulation;
            if (simulation == null) return;
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
            {
                if (!unit.IsAlive) continue;
                Gizmos.color = Color.Lerp(Color.red, Color.green, unit.Hp / unit.MaxHp);
                Gizmos.DrawSphere(unit.Position + Vector3.up * 0.22f, 0.19f);
                Gizmos.DrawLine(unit.Position, unit.Position + Vector3.up * 0.55f);
            }
        }

        private static Color CellColor(CellKind kind)
        {
            switch (kind)
            {
                case CellKind.Road: return new Color(0.65f, 0.54f, 0.35f);
                case CellKind.Spawn: return new Color(0.9f, 0.25f, 0.25f);
                case CellKind.Goal: return new Color(0.2f, 0.75f, 0.9f);
                case CellKind.TowerPlatform: return new Color(0.35f, 0.4f, 0.65f);
                case CellKind.Obstacle: return new Color(0.35f, 0.32f, 0.3f);
                default: return new Color(0.38f, 0.53f, 0.39f);
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\BattleCombatControls.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    // Disposable prototype observer/input adapter. No gameplay is computed here.
    [RequireComponent(typeof(BattleSession))]
    public sealed class BattleCombatControls : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        private BattleSimulation bound;
        private readonly int[] draft = new int[9];
        private int selected = -1;
        private bool editing;
        private bool showDetails;
        private Vector2 detailsScroll;
        private string message;
        private readonly Dictionary<int, Transform> shots = new Dictionary<int, Transform>();
        private readonly Dictionary<Renderer, MaterialPropertyBlock> originalColors = new Dictionary<Renderer, MaterialPropertyBlock>();
        private MaterialPropertyBlock color;
        private Material shotMaterial;
        public bool ShowDebugPanel { get; set; } = true;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            session = GetComponent<BattleSession>();
            color = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
        }
        private void ResetDraft()
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < 9; i++) draft[i] = i;
            selected = -1; message = null;
        }
        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            var sim = session.Simulation;
            if (sim != bound)
            {
                ClearVisuals(); bound = sim; editing = false; ResetDraft();
            }
            if (sim == null || sim.Objective != BattleObjective.ReachGoal) return;
            foreach (var shot in sim.Projectiles)
            {
                if (!shot.IsActive)
                {
                    if (shots.TryGetValue(shot.Id, out var old)) { Destroy(old.gameObject); shots.Remove(shot.Id); }
                    continue;
                }
                if (!shots.TryGetValue(shot.Id, out var view))
                {
                    var model = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Sphere);
                    model.name = $"Projectile View {shot.Id}";
                    model.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                    model.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * 0.18f;
                    Destroy(model.GetComponent<Collider>());
                    if (shotMaterial == null) shotMaterial = new Material(Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit")) { color = Color.white };
                    var renderer = model.GetComponent<Renderer>();
                    renderer.sharedMaterial = shotMaterial;
                    color.SetColor("_BaseColor", shot.TargetKind == ProjectileTargetKind.Tower ? Color.yellow : Color.magenta);
                    renderer.SetPropertyBlock(color);
                    view = model.transform; shots.Add(shot.Id, view);
                }
                view.position = shot.Position + Vector3.up * 0.5f;
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < session.Towers.Count; i++)
            {
                var tower = session.Towers[i];
                if (tower == null || tower.RuntimeState == null) continue;
                foreach (var renderer in tower.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
                {
                    if (!originalColors.ContainsKey(renderer))
                    {
                        var original = new MaterialPropertyBlock(); renderer.GetPropertyBlock(original); originalColors.Add(renderer, original);
                    }
                    if (tower.RuntimeState.IsAlive) renderer.SetPropertyBlock(originalColors[renderer]);
                    else { color.SetColor("_BaseColor", Color.gray); renderer.SetPropertyBlock(color); }
                }
            }
        }

        private void OnGUI()
        {
            if (!ShowDebugPanel) return;
            var sim = session != null ? session.Simulation : null;
            if (sim == null || sim.Objective != BattleObjective.ReachGoal) return;
            var panelHeight = Mathf.Min(Screen.height - 20, editing ? 430 : showDetails ? 480 : 165);
            GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(10, 10, 300, panelHeight), GUI.skin.box);
            GUILayout.Label($"Squad Battle | {sim.Outcome} | {sim.ElapsedTime:F1}s");
            GUILayout.Label($"Alive {sim.AliveCount}  Pending {sim.PendingSpawnCount}");
            if (sim.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running)
            {
                if (GUILayout.Button(sim.IsPaused ? "Resume" : "Pause"))
                {
                    sim.SetPaused(!sim.IsPaused); editing = false;
                }
                GUI.enabled = sim.PendingSpawnCount == 0;
                if (GUILayout.Button("Edit Formation"))
                {
                    sim.SetPaused(true); editing = true; ResetDraft();
                }
                GUI.enabled = true;
            }
            if (editing && sim.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running)
            {
                GUILayout.Label("Select two slots to swap. Apply to confirm.");
                // Row 2 is +Z (top in the existing battle view); no legacy XY mapping.
                for (var row = 2; row >= 0; row--)
                {
                    GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
                    for (var column = 0; column < 3; column++)
                    {
                        var slot = row * 3 + column;
                        var member = sim.GetMemberAtSlot(draft[slot]);
                        var label = $"{slot}: " + (member == null ? "Empty" : $"{member.Role}\n#{member.MemberId} {member.Hp:F1}");
                        if (GUILayout.Button((selected == slot ? "* " : "") + label, GUILayout.Width(91), GUILayout.Height(48)))
                        {
                            if (selected < 0) selected = slot;
                            else { var previous = draft[selected]; draft[selected] = draft[slot]; draft[slot] = previous; selected = -1; }
                        }
                    }
                    GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
                }
                if (GUILayout.Button("Apply & Resume"))
                {
                    if (sim.TryApplyFormation(draft)) { editing = false; sim.SetPaused(false); }
                    else message = "Formation rejected; no state was changed.";
                }
                if (GUILayout.Button("Cancel")) { editing = false; ResetDraft(); sim.SetPaused(false); }
                if (message != null) GUILayout.Label(message);
            }
            if (!editing)
            {
                if (GUILayout.Button(showDetails ? "Hide Details" : "Show Details")) showDetails = !showDetails;
                if (showDetails)
                {
                    detailsScroll = GUILayout.BeginScrollView(detailsScroll, GUILayout.Height(Mathf.Min(280, Screen.height * 0.4f)));
                    foreach (var unit in sim.Units)
                        GUILayout.Label($"#{unit.MemberId} {unit.Role} | Slot {unit.SlotId} | {unit.Hp:F1}/{unit.MaxHp} | {unit.LifeState}");
                    foreach (var tower in sim.Towers)
                        GUILayout.Label($"Tower {tower.Id} | {tower.Hp:F1}/{tower.Definition.MaxHp} | {(tower.IsAlive ? "Alive" : "Destroyed")}");
                    GUILayout.EndScrollView();
                }
            }
            if (GUILayout.Button("Restart Battle")) session.StartBattle();
            GUILayout.EndArea();
        }

        private void ClearVisuals()
        {
            foreach (var view in shots.Values) if (view != null) Destroy(view.gameObject);
            shots.Clear();
            foreach (var pair in originalColors) if (pair.Key != null) pair.Key.SetPropertyBlock(pair.Value);
            originalColors.Clear();
        }
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            ClearVisuals(); bound = null;
            if (shotMaterial != null) Destroy(shotMaterial);
            // Disabling this component never resumes, resets or otherwise changes Simulation.
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\BattlePresentation.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattlePresentation : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleSession session;
        [SerializeField] private UnitView unitPrefab;
        [SerializeField] private bool showTowerRanges = true;
        private readonly Dictionary<int, UnitView> views = new Dictionary<int, UnitView>();
        private readonly List<TowerRangeView> ranges = new List<TowerRangeView>();
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private BattleUnitInspection inspection;
        public BattleSession Session => session;
        public UnitView UnitPrefab => unitPrefab;
        public int ViewCount => views.Count;
        public bool ShowTowerRanges
        {
            get => showTowerRanges;
            set { showTowerRanges = value; foreach (var range in ranges) if (range != null) range.SetVisible(value); }
        }
        public bool TryGetView(int unitId, out UnitView view) => views.TryGetValue(unitId, out view);
        private void OnValidate()
        {
            foreach (var range in ranges) if (range != null) range.SetVisible(showTowerRanges);
        }

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            if (session == null || unitPrefab == null)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("BattlePresentation needs a session and UnitView prefab; combat can still run.", this);
                return;
            }
            session.SimulationCreated += Bind;
            inspection = GetComponent<BattleUnitInspection>();
            if (inspection == null) inspection = gameObject.AddComponent<BattleUnitInspection>();
            inspection.Initialize(this);
            inspection.enabled = true;
            Bind(session.Simulation);
        }

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (inspection != null) inspection.enabled = false;
            if (session != null) session.SimulationCreated -= Bind;
            Unbind();
            ClearViews();
            ClearRanges();
        }

        private void Bind(BattleSimulation next)
        {
            if (inspection != null) inspection.CloseImmediately();
            Unbind();
            ClearViews();
            ClearRanges();
            simulation = next;
            if (simulation == null) return;
            foreach (var tower in simulation.Towers)
            {
                var range = new GameObject($"Tower Range {tower.Id}").AddComponent<TowerRangeView>();
                range.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                range.Bind(tower);
                range.SetVisible(showTowerRanges);
                ranges.Add(range);
            }
            simulation.UnitSpawned += OnSpawned;
            simulation.UnitMoved += OnMoved;
            simulation.UnitDied += OnUnitEnded;
            simulation.UnitReachedGoal += OnUnitEnded;
            simulation.FormationChanged += OnFormationChanged;
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units) if (unit.IsAlive) CreateView(unit);
        }

        private void Unbind()
        {
            if (simulation == null) return;
            simulation.UnitSpawned -= OnSpawned;
            simulation.UnitMoved -= OnMoved;
            simulation.UnitDied -= OnUnitEnded;
            simulation.UnitReachedGoal -= OnUnitEnded;
            simulation.FormationChanged -= OnFormationChanged;
            simulation = null;
        }

        private void OnSpawned(UnitSpawnedEvent value)
        {
            // Look up the read-only state by ID, rather than giving the View simulation commands.
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
                if (unit.Id == value.UnitId) { CreateView(unit); break; }
        }

        private void CreateView(UnitState unit)
        {
            if (views.ContainsKey(unit.Id)) return;
            var view = Instantiate(unitPrefab, transform);
            view.name = $"Unit View {unit.Id}";
            view.Bind(unit);
            views.Add(unit.Id, view);
        }

        private void OnMoved(UnitMovedEvent value)
        {
            if (views.TryGetValue(value.UnitId, out var view)) view.CaptureState();
        }

        private void OnFormationChanged()
        {
            foreach (var view in views.Values) view.SnapToState();
        }

        private void OnUnitEnded(UnitEndedEvent value)
        {
            if (!views.TryGetValue(value.UnitId, out var view)) return;
            views.Remove(value.UnitId);
            RemoveView(view);
        }

        private static void RemoveView(UnitView view)
        {
            if (view == null) return;
            view.gameObject.SetActive(false); // Hide immediately; Destroy completes at the frame boundary.
            Destroy(view.gameObject);
        }

        private void ClearViews()
        {
            foreach (var view in views.Values) RemoveView(view);
            views.Clear();
        }

        private void ClearRanges()
        {
            foreach (var range in ranges)
            {
                if (range == null) continue;
                range.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                Destroy(range.gameObject);
            }
            ranges.Clear();
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\BattleUnitInspection.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem.UI;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent, DefaultExecutionOrder(200)]
    public sealed class BattleUnitInspection : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private BattlePresentation owner;
        private BattleSimulation selectedSimulation;
        private BattleSession inspectionSession;
        private UnitState selected;
        private UnitView selectedView;
        private Camera focusCamera;
        private Vector3 savedPosition, focusPoint;
        private Quaternion savedRotation;
        private float savedOrthoSize, focusDepth, focusOrthoSize;
        private bool squadSelected;
        private LegacyFormationEditor squadEditor;
        private UnitView highlightedCaptain;
        private Renderer[] highlightedRenderers;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock[] savedCaptainProperties;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock captainGlow;
        private bool hasCameraSnapshot, restoring;
        private GameObject uiRoot, ownedEventSystem;
        private RectTransform panel;
        private TMP_Text details, heading, hint;
        private LegacyUnitIconUI icon;
        private RectTransform unitSkills;
        private readonly Button[] skillButtons = new Button[3];
        private readonly TMP_Text[] skillLabels = new TMP_Text[3];
        private TMP_Text skillFeedback;
        private LegacyBattleHud battleHud;
        private readonly List<RaycastResult> uiHits = new List<RaycastResult>();
        public UnitState SelectedUnit => selected;
        public TMP_Text DetailsLabel => details;
        public RectTransform Panel => panel;
        public bool IsSquadSelected => squadSelected;
        public bool IsInputSuppressed { get; set; }
        public UnitView HighlightedCaptain => highlightedCaptain;

        public void Initialize(BattlePresentation presentation) => owner = presentation;

        private void Update()
        {
            if (owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled || IsInputSuppressed) return;
            if (Keyboard.current != null && Keyboard.current.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame) CloseInspection();
            if (Mouse.current == null || !Mouse.current.leftButton.wasPressedThisFrame) return;
            var point = Mouse.current.position.ReadValue();
            if (PointerOverUI(point)) return;
            if (!TrySelectAt(point)) CloseInspection();
        }

        private bool PointerOverUI(Vector2 point)
        {
            if (EventSystem.current == null) return false;
            uiHits.Clear();
            EventSystem.current.RaycastAll(new PointerEventData(EventSystem.current) { position = point }, uiHits);
            return uiHits.Count > 0;
        }

        public bool TrySelectAt(Vector2 screenPoint)
        {
            var camera = Camera.main;
            var simulation = owner != null && owner.Session != null ? owner.Session.Simulation : null;
            if (camera == null || simulation == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled) return false;
            var ray = camera.ScreenPointToRay(screenPoint);
            var nearest = float.PositiveInfinity;
            var id = 0;
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
            {
                if (!unit.IsAlive || !owner.TryGetView(unit.Id, out var view) || view == null || !view.TryGetVisibleBounds(out var bounds)) continue;
                if (!bounds.IntersectRay(ray, out var distance) || distance >= nearest) continue;
                nearest = distance; id = unit.Id;
            }
            return id != 0 && SelectUnit(id);
        }

        public bool SelectUnit(int memberId)
        {
            var camera = Camera.main;
            if (owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled || owner.Session == null || owner.Session.Simulation == null || camera == null ||
                !owner.TryGetView(memberId, out var view) || view == null || view.State == null || !view.State.IsAlive ||
                !view.TryGetVisibleBounds(out var bounds)) return false;
            EndSquadEdit();
            if (hasCameraSnapshot && focusCamera != camera) CloseImmediately();
            if (!hasCameraSnapshot)
            {
                focusCamera = camera;
                savedPosition = camera.transform.position;
                savedRotation = camera.transform.rotation;
                savedOrthoSize = camera.orthographicSize;
                hasCameraSnapshot = true;
            }
            selected = view.State; selectedView = view; selectedSimulation = owner.Session.Simulation;
            focusPoint = bounds.center;
            var originalDepth = Vector3.Dot(focusPoint - savedPosition, savedRotation * Vector3.forward);
            focusDepth = camera.orthographic ? Mathf.Max(originalDepth, camera.nearClipPlane * 2) :
                Mathf.Max(Mathf.Clamp(originalDepth * 0.35f, 4, 8) * 4f, camera.nearClipPlane * 2);
            focusOrthoSize = Mathf.Max(1.5f, savedOrthoSize * 0.4f) * 2f;
            restoring = false;
            EnsurePanel();
            skillFeedback.text = string.Empty;
            uiRoot.SetActive(true);
            details.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            hint.text = "Battle speed: 0.1x  |  Esc / X to close";
            icon.SetType(selected.Role);
            RefreshText();
            if (inspectionSession != null && inspectionSession != owner.Session) inspectionSession.SetUnitInspectionActive(false);
            inspectionSession = owner.Session;
            inspectionSession.SetUnitInspectionActive(true);
            return true;
        }

        public bool SelectSquad(LegacyBattleHud hud)
        {
            var camera = Camera.main;
            if (squadSelected) return true;
            if (owner == null || !owner.isActiveAndEnabled || owner.Session == null || hud == null || hud.Session != owner.Session ||
                !hud.CanEditFormation || camera == null || !TryGetSquadBounds(out var bounds)) return false;
            if (hasCameraSnapshot && focusCamera != camera) CloseImmediately();
            if (!hasCameraSnapshot)
            {
                focusCamera = camera; savedPosition = camera.transform.position; savedRotation = camera.transform.rotation;
                savedOrthoSize = camera.orthographicSize; hasCameraSnapshot = true;
            }
            selected = null; selectedView = null; selectedSimulation = owner.Session.Simulation;
            squadSelected = true; restoring = false; battleHud = hud;
            EnsurePanel(); uiRoot.SetActive(true);
            heading.text = "SQUAD 1";
            details.gameObject.SetActive(false); unitSkills.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            hint.text = "Battle paused  |  Esc / X to cancel";
            UnitState captain = null;
            foreach (var unit in selectedSimulation.Units)
            { if (captain == null) captain = unit; if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain) { captain = unit; break; } }
            icon.SetType(captain?.Role ?? UnitRole.Test);
            SetSquadFocus(bounds);
            UpdateCaptainHighlight();
            inspectionSession = owner.Session; inspectionSession.SetUnitInspectionActive(true);
            squadEditor = hud.OpenSquadEditorInPanel(panel);
            if (squadEditor != null) return true;
            CloseInspection(); return false;
        }

        public bool TryGetSquadBounds(out Bounds bounds)
        {
            bounds = default;
            if (owner == null || owner.Session == null || owner.Session.Simulation == null) return false;
            foreach (var unit in owner.Session.Simulation.Units)
            {
                if (!unit.IsAlive) continue;
                var anchor = unit.AnchorPosition;
                if (owner.TryGetView(unit.Id, out var view) && view != null) anchor = view.transform.position - unit.FormationOffset;
                // Include all nine slots, including empty/dead slots. The center is the formation anchor.
                bounds = new Bounds(anchor, new Vector3(3, 0.56f, 3));
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        private void UpdateCaptainHighlight()
        {
            UnitView captain = null;
            if (owner != null && selectedSimulation != null)
                foreach (var unit in selectedSimulation.Units)
                    if (unit.IsAlive && unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain)
                    { owner.TryGetView(unit.Id, out captain); break; }
            if (captain != highlightedCaptain)
            {
                ClearCaptainHighlight();
                if (captain == null) return;
                highlightedCaptain = captain;
                var bodyParts = new List<Renderer>();
                foreach (var renderer in captain.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
                    if (renderer.name == "Body" || renderer.name == "Outline") bodyParts.Add(renderer);
                highlightedRenderers = bodyParts.ToArray();
                savedCaptainProperties = new MaterialPropertyBlock[highlightedRenderers.Length];
                for (var i = 0; i < highlightedRenderers.Length; i++)
                {
                    savedCaptainProperties[i] = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
                    highlightedRenderers[i].GetPropertyBlock(savedCaptainProperties[i]);
                }
            }
            if (captain == null) return;
            if (captainGlow == null) captainGlow = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            // Bright gold pulses also work with the prototype's Unlit material, without Bloom.
            var pulse = (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4f) + 1f) * 0.5f;
            var gold = Color.Lerp(new Color(1f, 0.64f, 0.08f), new Color(1f, 0.94f, 0.48f), pulse);
            foreach (var renderer in highlightedRenderers)
            {
                if (renderer == null) continue;
                renderer.GetPropertyBlock(captainGlow);
                captainGlow.SetColor("_BaseColor", gold);
                captainGlow.SetColor("_Color", gold);
                renderer.SetPropertyBlock(captainGlow);
            }
        }

        private void ClearCaptainHighlight()
        {
            if (highlightedRenderers != null)
                for (var i = 0; i < highlightedRenderers.Length; i++)
                    if (highlightedRenderers[i] != null) highlightedRenderers[i].SetPropertyBlock(savedCaptainProperties[i]);
            highlightedCaptain = null; highlightedRenderers = null; savedCaptainProperties = null;
        }

        private void SetSquadFocus(Bounds bounds)
        {
            focusPoint = bounds.center;
            var extent = Vector3.zero;
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
            for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
            for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
            {
                var corner = Quaternion.Inverse(savedRotation) * Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z));
                extent = Vector3.Max(extent, new Vector3(Mathf.Abs(corner.x), Mathf.Abs(corner.y), Mathf.Abs(corner.z)));
            }
            var halfHeight = Mathf.Max(extent.y / 0.85f, extent.x / (focusCamera.aspect * 0.42f));
            focusOrthoSize = Mathf.Max(2, halfHeight);
            focusDepth = focusCamera.orthographic
                ? Mathf.Max(Vector3.Dot(focusPoint - savedPosition, savedRotation * Vector3.forward), focusCamera.nearClipPlane + extent.z + 1)
                : Mathf.Max(6, halfHeight / Mathf.Tan(focusCamera.fieldOfView * Mathf.Deg2Rad / 2) + extent.z,
                    focusCamera.nearClipPlane + extent.z + 1);
        }

        private void EndSquadEdit()
        {
            ClearCaptainHighlight();
            if (squadEditor != null && squadEditor.gameObject.activeSelf) squadEditor.CancelAndClose();
            squadEditor = null; squadSelected = false;
        }

        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            if (selected != null || squadSelected)
            {
                if (owner == null || owner.Session == null || owner.Session.Simulation != selectedSimulation || focusCamera == null)
                { CloseImmediately(); return; }
                if (squadSelected)
                {
                    if (squadEditor == null || !squadEditor.gameObject.activeSelf || !TryGetSquadBounds(out var squadBounds))
                    { CloseInspection(); return; }
                    SetSquadFocus(squadBounds);
                    UpdateCaptainHighlight();
                }
                else
                {
                    if (selectedView != null && selectedView.TryGetVisibleBounds(out var bounds)) focusPoint = bounds.center;
                    RefreshText();
                }
                var alpha = 1 - Mathf.Exp(-12 * Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
                if (focusCamera.orthographic)
                    focusCamera.orthographicSize = Mathf.Lerp(focusCamera.orthographicSize, focusOrthoSize, alpha);
                // Place the model at the center of the right half of the actual Game View.
                var viewport = focusCamera.ScreenToViewportPoint(new Vector3(Screen.width * 0.75f, Screen.height * 0.5f, 0));
                var offset = focusCamera.ViewportToWorldPoint(new Vector3(viewport.x, viewport.y, focusDepth)) - focusCamera.transform.position;
                focusCamera.transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(focusCamera.transform.position, focusPoint - offset, alpha);
            }
            else if (restoring && focusCamera != null)
            {
                var alpha = 1 - Mathf.Exp(-12 * Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
                focusCamera.transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(focusCamera.transform.position, savedPosition, alpha);
                focusCamera.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Slerp(focusCamera.transform.rotation, savedRotation, alpha);
                focusCamera.orthographicSize = Mathf.Lerp(focusCamera.orthographicSize, savedOrthoSize, alpha);
                if (Vector3.Distance(focusCamera.transform.position, savedPosition) < 0.001f &&
                    Quaternion.Angle(focusCamera.transform.rotation, savedRotation) < 0.01f &&
                    Mathf.Abs(focusCamera.orthographicSize - savedOrthoSize) < 0.001f) RestoreCamera();
            }
        }

        private void RefreshText()
        {
            heading.text = $"{LegacyUnitLooks.Name(selected.Role)}  #{selected.MemberId}";
            details.text = UnitDetailsText.Format(selected);
            var isCaptain = selected.Role == UnitRole.Captain;
            unitSkills.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            details.rectTransform.offsetMin = new Vector2(32, 220);
            if (battleHud == null) battleHud = FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyBattleHud>();
            skillLabels[0].text = isCaptain ? "Edit\nFormation" : "Skill 1";
            skillButtons[0].targetGraphic.color = isCaptain ? new Color(0.56f, 0.42f, 0.20f) : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
            skillButtons[0].interactable = selected.IsAlive && (!isCaptain ||
                (battleHud != null && owner != null && battleHud.Session == owner.Session && battleHud.CanEditFormation));
            skillButtons[1].interactable = skillButtons[2].interactable = selected.IsAlive;
        }

        private void ClickUnitSkill(int index)
        {
            if (selected == null || !selected.IsAlive) return;
            if (index == 0 && selected.Role == UnitRole.Captain)
            {
                if (battleHud != null && owner != null && battleHud.Session == owner.Session && battleHud.CanEditFormation)
                { battleHud.OpenSquadEditor(); skillFeedback.text = "Edit Formation"; }
                return;
            }
            skillFeedback.text = $"Skill {index + 1}: not assigned yet";
        }

        public void CloseInspection()
        {
            EndSquadEdit();
            if (inspectionSession != null) inspectionSession.SetUnitInspectionActive(false);
            inspectionSession = null;
            selected = null; selectedView = null; selectedSimulation = null;
            if (uiRoot != null) uiRoot.SetActive(false);
            restoring = hasCameraSnapshot;
        }

        public void CloseImmediately() { CloseInspection(); RestoreCamera(); }
        private void RestoreCamera()
        {
            if (hasCameraSnapshot && focusCamera != null)
            {
                focusCamera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(savedPosition, savedRotation);
                focusCamera.orthographicSize = savedOrthoSize;
            }
            hasCameraSnapshot = false; restoring = false; focusCamera = null;
        }

        private void EnsurePanel()
        {
            if (uiRoot != null) return;
            uiRoot = new GameObject("Unit Inspection UI", typeof(RectTransform));
            uiRoot.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(uiRoot, 100, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f);
            var background = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(uiRoot.transform, "Unit Details Panel", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero,
                new Color(0.08f, 0.11f, 0.17f, 0.97f));
            background.raycastTarget = true;
            panel = background.rectTransform;
            panel.anchorMin = Vector2.zero; panel.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 1);
            panel.offsetMin = panel.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
            heading = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Heading", "", new Vector2(120, -45), new Vector2(380, 40),
                28, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            AnchorTopLeft(heading.rectTransform, new Vector2(120, -45));
            heading.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Left;
            icon = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(panel, Vector2.zero, 110);
            icon.Rect.anchorMin = icon.Rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 1);
            icon.Rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(65, -65);
            var close = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(panel, "Close Details", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(48, 40), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 6);
            var closeRect = (RectTransform)close.transform;
            closeRect.anchorMin = closeRect.anchorMax = closeRect.pivot = Vector2.one;
            closeRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-20, -20);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(close.transform, "Label", "X", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(48, 40), 24, Color.white);
            close.onClick.AddListener(CloseInspection);
            details = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Live Stats", "", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, 22, LegacyUiKit.TextColor);
            details.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft;
            details.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;
            details.rectTransform.anchorMin = Vector2.zero; details.rectTransform.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            details.rectTransform.offsetMin = new Vector2(32, 60);
            details.rectTransform.offsetMax = new Vector2(-28, -135);
            details.lineSpacing = 12;
            unitSkills = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(panel, "Unit Skills", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(336, 136));
            unitSkills.anchorMin = unitSkills.anchorMax = unitSkills.pivot = Vector2.zero;
            unitSkills.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(32, 80);
            for (var i = 0; i < skillButtons.Length; i++)
            {
                var index = i;
                var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(unitSkills, "Unit Skill " + (i + 1), Vector2.zero,
                    new Vector2(96, 96), i == 0 ? new Color(0.56f, 0.42f, 0.20f) : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 0);
                var rect = (RectTransform)button.transform;
                rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = rect.pivot = Vector2.zero;
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(i * 112, 36);
                var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(button.transform, "Label", i == 0 ? "Edit\nFormation" : "Skill " + (i + 1),
                    Vector2.zero, new Vector2(88, 88), 17, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
                label.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;
                skillLabels[i] = label;
                button.onClick.AddListener(() => ClickUnitSkill(index));
                skillButtons[i] = button;
            }
            skillFeedback = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(unitSkills, "Skill Feedback", "", Vector2.zero,
                new Vector2(336, 28), 15, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            skillFeedback.rectTransform.anchorMin = skillFeedback.rectTransform.anchorMax = skillFeedback.rectTransform.pivot = Vector2.zero;
            skillFeedback.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
            hint = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Close Hint", "Battle speed: 0.1x  |  Esc / X to close", Vector2.zero,
                new Vector2(500, 28), 16, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            hint.rectTransform.anchorMin = hint.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0);
            hint.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0, 28);
            if (EventSystem.current == null)
            {
                ownedEventSystem = new GameObject("Inspection EventSystem", typeof(EventSystem), typeof(InputSystemUIInputModule));
                ownedEventSystem.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                ownedEventSystem.GetComponent<InputSystemUIInputModule>().AssignDefaultActions();
            }
        }

        private static void AnchorTopLeft(RectTransform rect, Vector2 point)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = rect.pivot = new Vector2(0, 1);
            rect.anchoredPosition = point;
        }

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            CloseImmediately();
            if (uiRoot != null) { uiRoot.SetActive(false); Destroy(uiRoot); uiRoot = null; }
            if (ownedEventSystem != null) { ownedEventSystem.SetActive(false); Destroy(ownedEventSystem); ownedEventSystem = null; }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\TowerRangeView.cs ===
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    // Optional read-only observer. No colliders, targeting or combat commands.
    public sealed class TowerRangeView : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private TowerState tower;
        private TowerDefinition previewDefinition;
        private Mesh mesh;
        private MeshRenderer fill;
        private LineRenderer border;
        private Material fillMaterial, borderMaterial;
        private Vector3[] corners;
        private bool visible = true;
        public Bounds DisplayBounds { get; private set; }
        public int TowerId => tower?.Id ?? 0;

        public void Bind(TowerState state)
        {
            tower = state;
            previewDefinition = null;
            EnsureGeometry();
            Refresh();
        }

        // A map preview needs only the authored range configuration, never a running tower.
        public void BindPreview(TowerDefinition definition)
        {
            tower = null;
            previewDefinition = definition;
            EnsureGeometry();
            Refresh();
        }

        private void EnsureGeometry()
        {
            if (mesh == null)
            {
                mesh = new Mesh { name = "Tower Range XZ Quad" };
                gameObject.AddComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh = mesh;
                fill = gameObject.AddComponent<MeshRenderer>();
                // Same alpha values as the original Team5 indicator.
                fillMaterial = CreateMaterial(new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1, 0.15f));
                borderMaterial = CreateMaterial(new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1, 0.7f));
                fill.sharedMaterial = fillMaterial;
                fill.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
                fill.receiveShadows = false;
                border = new GameObject("Border").AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
                border.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                border.sharedMaterial = borderMaterial;
                border.loop = true;
                border.useWorldSpace = true;
                border.positionCount = 4;
                border.widthMultiplier = 0.035f;
                border.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
                border.receiveShadows = false;
            }
        }

        public void SetVisible(bool value) { visible = value; Refresh(); }
        private void LateUpdate() => Refresh();
        private void OnEnable() => Refresh();
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (fill != null) fill.enabled = false;
            if (border != null) border.enabled = false;
        }

        private void Refresh()
        {
            var definition = tower != null ? tower.Definition : previewDefinition;
            if (definition == null || fill == null || border == null) return;
            fill.enabled = border.enabled = isActiveAndEnabled && visible && (tower == null || tower.IsAlive);
            var position = tower != null ? tower.Position : definition.Position;
            var bounds = SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(position, definition.Area, definition.Range, definition.AttackWidth);
            // Top of the tower's support surface; a small lift prevents terrain z-fighting.
            transform.SetPositionAndRotation(bounds.center + Vector3.up * 0.035f, Quaternion.identity);
            transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
            if (DisplayBounds != bounds || mesh.vertexCount == 0)
            {
                var half = bounds.extents;
                corners = new[] { new Vector3(-half.x, 0, -half.z), new Vector3(-half.x, 0, half.z),
                    new Vector3(half.x, 0, half.z), new Vector3(half.x, 0, -half.z) };
                mesh.vertices = corners;
                mesh.triangles = new[] { 0, 1, 2, 0, 2, 3 };
                mesh.RecalculateBounds();
            }
            DisplayBounds = bounds;
            for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) border.SetPosition(i, transform.TransformPoint(corners[i]));
        }

        private static Material CreateMaterial(Color color)
        {
            var material = new Material(Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit")) { name = "Tower Range (Runtime)", renderQueue = 3000 };
            material.SetColor("_BaseColor", color);
            material.SetFloat("_Surface", 1);
            material.SetFloat("_SrcBlend", (float)BlendMode.SrcAlpha);
            material.SetFloat("_DstBlend", (float)BlendMode.OneMinusSrcAlpha);
            material.SetFloat("_ZWrite", 0);
            material.SetFloat("_Cull", (float)CullMode.Off);
            material.SetOverrideTag("RenderType", "Transparent");
            material.EnableKeyword("_SURFACE_TYPE_TRANSPARENT");
            return material;
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (mesh != null) Destroy(mesh);
            if (fillMaterial != null) Destroy(fillMaterial);
            if (borderMaterial != null) Destroy(borderMaterial);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\UnitDetailsText.cs ===
using System;
using System.Text;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    // Formatting only: values come from the selected member's read-only state.
    public static class UnitDetailsText
    {
        public static string Format(UnitState unit)
        {
            if (unit == null) return string.Empty;
            var stats = unit.Definition;
            var text = new StringBuilder();
            text.AppendLine($"Role: {unit.Role.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}");
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"HP: {unit.Hp:0.##} / {unit.MaxHp:0.##}"));
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Attack: {stats.Damage:0.##}"));
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Defense: {stats.Defense * 100:0.##}% damage reduction"));
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Move speed: {unit.Speed:0.##} units/s"));
            if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Attack || unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain)
            {
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Attack range: {stats.Range:0.##}"));
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Attack interval: {stats.AttackInterval:0.##} s"));
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Cooldown: {unit.AttackCooldown:0.##} s"));
            }
            if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Heal)
            {
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Healing: {stats.HealPerSecond * SquadCombatRules.HealInterval:0.##} HP / {SquadCombatRules.HealInterval:0.##} s"));
                text.AppendLine("Heal range: 8 neighboring slots");
            }
            text.AppendLine($"Member: #{unit.MemberId}");
            text.AppendLine($"Formation: row {unit.FormationSlot.y + 1}, column {unit.FormationSlot.x + 1}");
            text.Append($"Status: {(unit.LifeState == UnitLifeState.Dead ? "Dead" : unit.LifeState == UnitLifeState.ReachedGoal ? "Reached goal" : "Alive")}");
            return text.ToString();
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\UnitView.cs ===
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    // The root is the foot position; the authored square model uses the formation UI's look.
    // This observer can only read UnitState; it never advances or changes the simulation.
    public sealed class UnitView : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const float LookScale = 2f; // Largest Tank remains 0.88 wide inside one Small Cell.
        private UnitState state;
        private Vector3 previousPosition;
        private Vector3 currentPosition;
        private double sampleTime;
        private float sampleInterval;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock colors;
        public int UnitId => state?.Id ?? 0;
        public UnitState State => state;
        public bool TryGetVisibleBounds(out Bounds bounds)
        {
            bounds = default;
            var found = false;
            foreach (var renderer in GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
            {
                if (!renderer.enabled || !renderer.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) continue;
                if (!found) { bounds = renderer.bounds; found = true; }
                else bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
            }
            return found;
        }
        public Vector3 PreviousPosition => previousPosition;
        public Vector3 CurrentPosition => currentPosition;

        public void Bind(UnitState unit)
        {
            state = unit;
            if (colors == null) colors = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            previousPosition = currentPosition = unit.Position;
            sampleTime = Time.timeAsDouble;
            sampleInterval = Time.fixedDeltaTime;
            transform.position = currentPosition;
            ApplyAppearance(unit.Role);
        }

        public void ApplyAppearance(UnitRole role)
        {
            if (colors == null) colors = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            var look = LegacyUnitLooks.For(role);
            var body = role == UnitRole.Test ? new Color(1, 0.85f, 0.2f) : look.Body;
            SetPart("Outline", look.Outline, true, new Vector3(look.OuterSize * LookScale, 0.26f, look.OuterSize * LookScale));
            SetPart("Body", body, true, new Vector3(look.InnerSize * LookScale, 0.008f, look.InnerSize * LookScale));
            var showMark = role != UnitRole.Test;
            SetPart("Mark Diamond", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Diamond,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.DiamondSize * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.DiamondSize * LookScale));
            SetPart("Mark Plate", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Plate,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.PlateSize * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.PlateSize * LookScale));
            SetPart("Mark Across", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Cross,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.CrossLength * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.CrossWidth * LookScale));
            SetPart("Mark Upright", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Cross,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.CrossWidth * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.CrossLength * LookScale));
            for (var i = 0; i < SoldierLook.CrownSegments.Length; i += 2)
            {
                var partName = "Mark Crown " + i;
                var part = transform.Find(partName);
                if (part == null && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Crown)
                {
                    var template = transform.Find("Mark Plate");
                    if (template == null) continue;
                    part = Instantiate(template, transform);
                    part.name = partName;
                }
                if (part == null) continue;
                var start = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i];
                var end = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i + 1];
                var delta = end - start;
                var center = (start + end) * (LookScale / 2);
                part.localPosition = new Vector3(center.x, 0.268f, center.y);
                part.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, -Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg, 0);
                SetPart(partName, look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Crown,
                    new Vector3(delta.magnitude * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.CrownStroke * LookScale));
            }
        }

        private void SetPart(string partName, Color tint, bool visible, Vector3 scale)
        {
            var part = transform.Find(partName);
            if (part == null) return;
            part.gameObject.SetActive(visible);
            part.localScale = scale;
            colors.Clear();
            colors.SetColor("_BaseColor", tint);
            var renderer = part.GetComponent<Renderer>();
            if (renderer != null) renderer.SetPropertyBlock(colors);
        }

        public void SnapToState()
        {
            if (state == null) return;
            previousPosition = currentPosition = state.Position;
            transform.position = currentPosition;
            sampleTime = Time.timeAsDouble;
        }

        public void CaptureState()
        {
            previousPosition = currentPosition;
            currentPosition = state.Position;
            sampleTime = Time.timeAsDouble;
            sampleInterval = Time.fixedDeltaTime;
        }

        public void Present(float alpha)
        {
            transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(previousPosition, currentPosition, Mathf.Clamp01(alpha));
        }

        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            // One simulation tick of visual latency; no extrapolation or speed integration.
            // Use the last sample time, so a paused/ended simulation never oscillates with FixedUpdate.
            Present(sampleInterval > 0 ? (float)((Time.timeAsDouble - sampleTime) / sampleInterval) : 1);
        }
    }
}

using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem.UI;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Team5's authored HUD is preserved in the scene. This replaces only its owner bindings.
    public sealed class LegacyBattleHud : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleSession session;
        [SerializeField] private Slider speedSlider;
        [SerializeField] private Button editButton;
        private TextMeshProUGUI levelLabel, livesLabel;
        private Canvas canvas;
        private TMP_FontAsset hudFont;
        private BattleCombatControls debugControls;
        private bool previousDebugPanel;
        private bool previousEditButtonActive;
        private GameObject ownedEventSystem;
        private LegacyFormationEditor editor;
        private BattleSimulation bound;
        private LegacyHealthBars healthBars;
        private Button pauseButton;
        private TMP_Text pauseLabel;
        private readonly Button[] squadButtons = new Button[3];
        private LegacyUnitIconUI squadIcon;
        private BattleUnitInspection inspection;
        private bool HasOpenEditor => editor != null && editor.gameObject.activeSelf;
        public Button SquadButton(int index) => squadButtons[index];
        public BattleSession Session => session;
        public Slider SpeedSlider => speedSlider;
        public Button EditButton => editButton;
        public Button PauseButton => pauseButton;
        public bool TutorialOwnsPause { get; set; }
        public TMP_Text LevelLabel => levelLabel;
        public TMP_Text LivesLabel => livesLabel;
        public LegacyFormationEditor FormationEditor => editor;
        public bool CanEditFormation => isActiveAndEnabled && bound != null && bound.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running &&
            bound.AliveCount > 0 && bound.PendingSpawnCount == 0 && !HasOpenEditor;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            canvas = GetComponent<Canvas>(); canvas.enabled = true;
            if (session == null || speedSlider == null)
            { Debug.LogError("Legacy HUD needs its scene bindings.", this); return; }
            var authoredText = editButton != null ? editButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true) : GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true);
            hudFont = authoredText != null && authoredText.font != null ? authoredText.font : TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
            if (editButton != null)
            {
                previousEditButtonActive = editButton.gameObject.activeSelf;
                editButton.gameObject.SetActive(false); // Optional legacy entry; squad/skill buttons now handle editing.
            }
            debugControls = session.GetComponent<BattleCombatControls>();
            if (debugControls != null) { previousDebugPanel = debugControls.ShowDebugPanel; debugControls.ShowDebugPanel = false; }
            if (EventSystem.current == null)
            {
                ownedEventSystem = new GameObject("Battle UI EventSystem", typeof(EventSystem), typeof(InputSystemUIInputModule));
                ownedEventSystem.transform.SetParent(session.transform, false);
                ownedEventSystem.GetComponent<InputSystemUIInputModule>().AssignDefaultActions();
            }
            EnsureLevelLabel();
            EnsurePauseButton();
            EnsureSquadButtons();
            var barsRoot = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(transform, "World Health Bars", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(barsRoot);
            barsRoot.pivot = Vector2.zero;
            barsRoot.SetAsFirstSibling();
            healthBars = barsRoot.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyHealthBars>();
            session.SimulationCreated += OnSimulationCreated;
            OnSimulationCreated(session.Simulation);
            Refresh();
        }
        private void OnSimulationCreated(BattleSimulation next)
        {
            if (editor != null) { editor.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(editor.gameObject); editor = null; }
            bound = next;
            if (healthBars != null) healthBars.Bind(session, next);
            speedSlider.SetValueWithoutNotify(session.PlaybackSpeed - 1);
        }
        private GameObject recoveryPanel;
        private void Update()
        {
            Refresh();
            if (session != null && session.RecoveryRequired && recoveryPanel == null)
                recoveryPanel = LegacyLevelOneRecovery.Open(session);
        }
        private void Refresh()
        {
            if (session == null || levelLabel == null) return;
            levelLabel.text = $"LEVEL {Mathf.Max(1, session.DisplayedLevelNumber)}";
            livesLabel.text = LegacyUiKit.LifeCounterText(session.AttemptsRemaining, session.AttemptsPerLevel);
            if (editButton != null) editButton.interactable = CanEditFormation;
            speedSlider.interactable = bound != null && bound.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && !HasOpenEditor && !session.IsUnitInspectionActive && !TutorialOwnsPause;
            pauseButton.interactable = bound != null && bound.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && !HasOpenEditor && !TutorialOwnsPause;
            pauseLabel.text = bound != null && bound.IsPaused ? "Resume" : "Pause";
            squadButtons[0].interactable = CanEditFormation || (inspection != null && inspection.IsSquadSelected);
            UnitState captain = null;
            if (bound != null)
                foreach (var unit in bound.Units)
                { if (captain == null) captain = unit; if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain) { captain = unit; break; } }
            squadIcon.SetType(captain?.Role ?? UnitRole.Test);
            squadButtons[0].targetGraphic.color = inspection != null && inspection.IsSquadSelected ? LegacyUiKit.NodeBlueColor : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
        }
        private void EnsureSquadButtons()
        {
            if (squadButtons[0] != null) { foreach (var button in squadButtons) button.gameObject.SetActive(true); return; }
            var scaler = canvas.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            var scale = scaler != null && scaler.uiScaleMode == CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize ? scaler.referenceResolution.x / 1280f : 1f;
            for (var i = 0; i < squadButtons.Length; i++)
            {
                var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(transform, "Squad Selection " + (i + 1), Vector2.zero,
                    Vector2.one * 56 * scale, LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 0);
                var rect = (RectTransform)button.transform;
                rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = rect.pivot = new Vector2(0, 1);
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(24, -124 - i * 68) * scale;
                button.interactable = false;
                squadButtons[i] = button;
                if (i == 0)
                {
                    squadIcon = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(rect, Vector2.zero, 110 * scale);
                    squadIcon.Rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(28, -28) * scale;
                    squadIcon.Rect.anchorMin = squadIcon.Rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 1);
                    button.onClick.AddListener(SelectSquad);
                }
            }
        }
        public void SelectSquad()
        {
            if (inspection == null)
                foreach (var view in FindObjectsByType<BattlePresentation>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                    if (view.Session == session) { inspection = view.GetComponent<BattleUnitInspection>(); break; }
            if (inspection == null) return;
            if (inspection.IsSquadSelected) inspection.CloseInspection();
            else inspection.SelectSquad(this);
            Refresh();
        }
        private void EnsurePauseButton()
        {
            if (pauseButton == null)
            {
                pauseButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(speedSlider.transform, "Pause Battle", Vector2.zero,
                    new Vector2(80, 22), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 3);
                var rect = (RectTransform)pauseButton.transform;
                rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 1);
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0, 30);
                pauseLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(rect, "Label", "Pause", Vector2.zero,
                    new Vector2(76, 20), 12, Color.white);
                pauseLabel.font = hudFont;
                pauseButton.onClick.AddListener(TogglePause);
            }
            pauseButton.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }
        public void TogglePause()
        {
            if (bound == null || bound.Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running || HasOpenEditor || TutorialOwnsPause) return;
            bound.SetPaused(!bound.IsPaused);
            Refresh();
        }
        // Same method names as old persistent UnityEvents; only the receiver changes.
        public void SetSpeedFromSlider(float value)
        {
            var index = Mathf.RoundToInt(value);
            session.SetPlaybackSpeed(Mathf.Clamp(index + 1, 1, 3));
        }
        public void OpenSquadEditor()
        {
            if (!CanEditFormation) return;
            editor = LegacyFormationEditor.Open(this, bound); Refresh();
        }
        public LegacyFormationEditor OpenSquadEditorInPanel(RectTransform panel)
        {
            if (!CanEditFormation) return null;
            editor = LegacyFormationEditor.Open(this, bound, panel); Refresh();
            return editor;
        }
        private void EnsureLevelLabel()
        {
            var existing = transform.Find("Level Label");
            if (existing != null)
            {
                levelLabel = existing.GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
                livesLabel = transform.Find("Death Count").GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>(); return;
            }
            // Unchanged layout/font calculation from OffenseWaveManager.EnsureLevelLabel.
            var scaler = canvas.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            var uiScale = scaler != null && scaler.uiScaleMode == CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize ? scaler.referenceResolution.x / 1280f : 1;
            levelLabel = CreateCornerLabel("Level Label", new Vector2(24, -24) * uiScale, new Vector2(240, 60) * uiScale, 36 * uiScale, FontStyles.Bold);
            livesLabel = CreateCornerLabel("Death Count", new Vector2(56, -76) * uiScale, new Vector2(208, 40) * uiScale, 24 * uiScale, FontStyles.Normal);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateHeartIcon(transform, new Vector2(24, -80) * uiScale, uiScale);
        }
        private TextMeshProUGUI CreateCornerLabel(string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, float fontSize, FontStyles style)
        {
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(transform, name, "", position, size, fontSize, Color.white, style);
            label.font = hudFont;
            label.rectTransform.anchorMin = label.rectTransform.anchorMax = label.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0, 1);
            label.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = position;
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft;
            return label;
        }
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (recoveryPanel != null) { recoveryPanel.SetActive(false); Destroy(recoveryPanel); recoveryPanel = null; }
            if (inspection != null && inspection.IsSquadSelected) inspection.CloseImmediately();
            foreach (var button in squadButtons) if (button != null) button.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            if (pauseButton != null) pauseButton.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            if (editButton != null) editButton.gameObject.SetActive(previousEditButtonActive);
            if (session != null) session.SimulationCreated -= OnSimulationCreated;
            if (canvas != null) canvas.enabled = false;
            if (debugControls != null) debugControls.ShowDebugPanel = previousDebugPanel;
            if (editor != null) { editor.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(editor.gameObject); editor = null; }
            if (ownedEventSystem != null) { ownedEventSystem.SetActive(false); Destroy(ownedEventSystem); ownedEventSystem = null; }
            if (healthBars != null) { healthBars.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(healthBars.gameObject); healthBars = null; }
            bound = null;
        }
    }
}

using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Team5 AugmentOverlay's choose-one card flow, adapted to warehouse ownership.
    public sealed class LegacyClaimCardOverlay : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;
        public static LegacyClaimCardOverlay Open(LegacyUpgradeScene owner)
        {
            var offer = BattleSceneFlow.OpenClaimCards();
            if (offer.Count == 0) return null;
            var host = new GameObject("Claim Card Offer", typeof(RectTransform));
            var view = host.AddComponent<LegacyClaimCardOverlay>();
            view.owner = owner;
            // Root canvas: do not inherit an authored Canvas's scale.
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(host, 200, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f);
            var backdrop = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(host.transform, "Backdrop", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0.02f,0.03f,0.05f,0.96f));
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(backdrop.rectTransform); backdrop.raycastTarget = true;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(host.transform, "Title", "CHOOSE ONE", new Vector2(0,260), new Vector2(900,55),36,Color.white,FontStyles.Bold);
            var shop = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(host.transform, "Price", $"Each card costs {shop.NextPrice} gold  |  You have {BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance}",
                new Vector2(0,210),new Vector2(900,35),22,LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor);
            for(var i=0;i<offer.Count;i++) view.BuildCard(offer[i],new Vector2((i-(offer.Count-1)*.5f)*285,0),shop);
            var cancel = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(host.transform,"Cancel",new Vector2(0,-245),new Vector2(240,55),LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor,6);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(cancel.transform,"Label","CANCEL",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(230,50),23,Color.white,FontStyles.Bold);
            cancel.onClick.AddListener(view.Close);
            return view;
        }

        private void BuildCard(ClaimCard card,Vector2 position,ClaimCardShop shop)
        {
            var role = ClaimCardShop.RoleOf(card);
            var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(transform,card.ToString(),position,new Vector2(260,330),LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor,12);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(button.transform,"Face",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(254,324),LegacyUiKit.PanelColor,10);
            var introduction = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(button.transform,"Introduction",new Vector2(0,20),new Vector2(200,262));
            introduction.localScale = Vector3.one * 1.1f;
            LegacyUnitIntroductionUI.Build(introduction,SquadCombatRules.Create(role),false);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(button.transform,"Cost",$"{shop.NextPrice} GOLD",new Vector2(0,-140),new Vector2(240,35),22,LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor,FontStyles.Bold);
            button.onClick.AddListener(()=> { if(BattleSceneFlow.TryClaimCard(card)) Close(); });
        }

        public void Close()
        {
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
            if(owner != null) owner.OnClaimCardsClosed();
            Destroy(gameObject);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyFormationCellUI.cs ===
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public class LegacyFormationCellUI : MonoBehaviour, IPointerClickHandler, IBeginDragHandler, IDragHandler, IEndDragHandler
    {
        private LegacyFormationGrid grid;
        private int index;

        public void Initialize(LegacyFormationGrid owner, int cellIndex)
        {
            grid = owner;
            index = cellIndex;
        }

        public void OnPointerClick(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (!eventData.dragging)
            {
                grid.HandleClick(index);
            }
        }

        public void OnBeginDrag(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            grid.HandleBeginDrag(index, eventData);
        }

        public void OnDrag(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            grid.HandleDrag(eventData);
        }

        public void OnEndDrag(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            grid.HandleEndDrag(eventData);
        }
    }
}
