using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Layout copied from Team5 BattleSquadEditor. Only its owner/data adapter changes.
    public sealed class LegacyFormationEditor : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private LegacyFormationGrid grid;
        private LegacyBattleHud owner;
        private TextMeshProUGUI error;
        private bool wasPaused;
        public LegacyFormationGrid Grid => grid;
        public static LegacyFormationEditor Open(LegacyBattleHud owner, BattleSimulation simulation, RectTransform inspectionPanel = null)
        {
            var host = new GameObject("Battle Squad Editor", typeof(RectTransform));
            host.transform.SetParent(inspectionPanel != null ? inspectionPanel : owner.Session.transform, false);
            var editor = host.AddComponent<LegacyFormationEditor>();
            editor.owner = owner; editor.simulation = simulation; editor.wasPaused = simulation.IsPaused;
            simulation.SetPaused(true); editor.Build(inspectionPanel != null); return editor;
        }
        private void Build(bool embedded)
        {
            var root = (RectTransform)transform;
            if (embedded) LegacyUiKit.Stretch(root);
            else
            {
                LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(gameObject, 800, new Vector2(1280, 720), 1);
                var backdrop = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Input Blocker", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0, 0, 0, 0.15f));
                LegacyUiKit.Stretch(backdrop.rectTransform); backdrop.raycastTarget = true;
            }
            var panel = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Panel", new Vector2(-20, -20), new Vector2(320, 410), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 12);
            if (embedded)
            {
                panel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
            }
            else
            {
                panel.rectTransform.anchorMin = panel.rectTransform.anchorMax = panel.rectTransform.pivot = Vector2.one;
                panel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-20, -20);
            }
            panel.raycastTarget = true;
            var content = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(panel.transform, "Content", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(content, "Title", "EDIT SQUAD", new Vector2(0, 174), new Vector2(280, 30), 24, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(content, "Paused", "Battle paused", new Vector2(0, 145), new Vector2(280, 24), 16, LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor);
            grid = LegacyFormationGrid.Create(content, root, simulation);
            var done = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(content, "Apply And Resume", new Vector2(0, -170), new Vector2(260, 48), LegacyUiKit.NodeGreenColor, 8);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(done.transform, "Label", "APPLY & RESUME", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(250, 40), 20, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            done.onClick.AddListener(() => ApplyAndResume());
            error = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(content, "Error", "", new Vector2(0, -136), new Vector2(290, 20), 11, Color.red);
            if (EventSystem.current != null) EventSystem.current.SetSelectedGameObject(null);
        }
        public bool ApplyAndResume()
        {
            if (owner.Session.Simulation != simulation || !simulation.TryApplyFormation(grid.CopySourceSlots()))
            { error.text = "Formation cannot be applied."; return false; }
            simulation.SetPaused(wasPaused);
            gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(gameObject); return true;
        }
        public void CancelAndClose()
        {
            if (owner != null && owner.Session.Simulation == simulation) simulation.SetPaused(wasPaused);
            gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(gameObject);
        }
        // Removing a view must not change the pause or battle state.
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyFormationGrid.cs ===
using System;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Battle editing uses a slot permutation; preparation delegates to the bounded deployment model.
    // Neither mode changes a live simulation's state directly.
    public sealed class LegacyFormationGrid : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private readonly Image[] cells = new Image[9];
        private readonly LegacyUnitIconUI[] icons = new LegacyUnitIconUI[9];
        private readonly int[] sourceSlots = new int[9];
        private Func<int, UnitRole> roleAtSource;
        private SquadPreparation preparation;
        private RectTransform dragLayer;
        private LegacyUnitIconUI ghost;
        private int dragFrom = -1, selected = -1;
        public int[] CopySourceSlots() => (int[])sourceSlots.Clone();

        public static LegacyFormationGrid Create(Transform parent, RectTransform dragLayer, BattleSimulation simulation)
            => Create(parent, dragLayer, slot => simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(slot)?.Role ?? UnitRole.Test);

        public static LegacyFormationGrid CreateForPreparation(Transform parent, RectTransform dragLayer, SquadPreparation preparation)
        {
            var grid = Create(parent, dragLayer, preparation.RoleAtSlot);
            grid.preparation = preparation;
            preparation.Changed += grid.Refresh;
            grid.transform.Find("Hint").GetComponent<TMPro.TMP_Text>().text = "Drag out to return unit";
            return grid;
        }
        public bool TryPlaceWarehouseMember(int memberId, PointerEventData data)
            => preparation != null && preparation.TryAssign(memberId, FindCell(data));

        public static LegacyFormationGrid Create(Transform parent, RectTransform dragLayer, Func<int, UnitRole> roleAtSource)
        {
            const float cell = 58, gap = 8, size = cell * 3 + gap * 2;
            var rect = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(parent, "Squad Grid", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * size);
            var grid = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyFormationGrid>();
            grid.roleAtSource = roleAtSource; grid.dragLayer = dragLayer;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
            {
                grid.sourceSlots[slot] = slot;
                // Native X/Z slots: +Z is the top row. Member IDs are not UI indices.
                var position = new Vector2(slot % 3 - 1, slot / 3 - 1) * (cell + gap);
                var image = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(rect, "Cell " + slot, position, Vector2.one * cell, LegacyUiKit.CellColor, 8);
                image.raycastTarget = true;
                var outline = image.gameObject.AddComponent<Outline>();
                outline.effectColor = LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor; outline.effectDistance = new Vector2(1.5f, -1.5f);
                image.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyFormationCellUI>().Initialize(grid, slot);
                grid.cells[slot] = image;
                grid.icons[slot] = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(image.transform, Vector2.zero, cell * 1.4f);
            }
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(rect, "Top", "TOP", new Vector2(0, size / 2 + 22), new Vector2(size, 18), 12,
                LegacyUiKit.MutedColor, TMPro.FontStyles.Bold, 6);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(rect, "Hint", "Drag to swap", new Vector2(0, -size / 2 - 26), new Vector2(size + 120, 22), 14, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            grid.Refresh(); return grid;
        }
        private UnitRole RoleAt(int slot) => preparation != null ? preparation.RoleAtSlot(slot) : roleAtSource(sourceSlots[slot]);
        private void Refresh()
        {
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
            {
                icons[slot].SetType(RoleAt(slot));
                icons[slot].gameObject.SetActive(dragFrom != slot);
                cells[slot].GetComponent<Outline>().effectColor = selected == slot ? LegacyUiKit.DropColor : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
            }
        }
        public void HandleClick(int slot)
        {
            if (selected < 0) selected = slot;
            else { Swap(selected, slot); selected = -1; }
            Refresh();
        }
        private void Swap(int first, int second)
        {
            if (preparation != null) { preparation.TryMove(first, second); return; }
            var value = sourceSlots[first]; sourceSlots[first] = sourceSlots[second]; sourceSlots[second] = value;
        }
        public void HandleBeginDrag(int slot, PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (RoleAt(slot) == UnitRole.Test) return;
            dragFrom = slot; selected = -1;
            ghost = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(dragLayer, Vector2.zero, 58 * 1.4f * 1.15f);
            ghost.SetType(RoleAt(slot)); ghost.transform.SetAsLastSibling();
            MoveGhost(eventData); Refresh();
        }
        public void HandleDrag(PointerEventData eventData) { if (dragFrom >= 0) MoveGhost(eventData); }
        public void HandleEndDrag(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (dragFrom < 0) return;
            var target = FindCell(eventData); var from = dragFrom; dragFrom = -1;
            if (ghost != null) Destroy(ghost.gameObject);
            ghost = null;
            if (target >= 0 && target != from) Swap(from, target);
            else if (target < 0 && preparation != null) preparation.TryRemove(from);
            Refresh();
        }
        private void MoveGhost(PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (ghost != null && RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(dragLayer, data.position, data.pressEventCamera, out var point))
                ghost.Rect.anchoredPosition = point;
        }
        private int FindCell(PointerEventData data)
        {
            // Use the UI raycaster's actual hovered cell first (also handles scaled Game Views).
            var hit = data.pointerCurrentRaycast.gameObject;
            if (hit != null)
                for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                    if (hit.transform == cells[slot].transform || hit.transform.IsChildOf(cells[slot].transform)) return slot;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                if (RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(cells[slot].rectTransform, data.position, data.pressEventCamera)) return slot;
            return -1;
        }
        private void OnDisable() { if (ghost != null) Destroy(ghost.gameObject); ghost = null; dragFrom = -1; }
        private void OnDestroy() { if (preparation != null) preparation.Changed -= Refresh; }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyHealthBars.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Old HealthBar palette and left-aligned fill, adapted to an overlay for the 3D scene.
    public sealed class LegacyHealthBars : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private sealed class Bar { public RectTransform Root; public Image Fill; public float Width, Height, Inset; }
        private static readonly Color backgroundColor = new Color(0.055f, 0.067f, 0.082f, 0.92f);
        private static readonly Color fullColor = new Color(0.35f, 0.85f, 0.4f);
        private static readonly Color emptyColor = new Color(0.9f, 0.25f, 0.2f);
        private readonly Dictionary<int, Bar> unitBars = new Dictionary<int, Bar>();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, Bar> towerBars = new Dictionary<int, Bar>();
        private readonly HashSet<int> damaged = new HashSet<int>();
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private BattleSession session;
        private BattlePresentation presentation;
        public void Bind(BattleSession owner, BattleSimulation next)
        {
            if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged;
            foreach (Transform child in transform) { child.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(child.gameObject); }
            unitBars.Clear(); towerBars.Clear(); damaged.Clear();
            session = owner; simulation = next;
            presentation = null;
            foreach (var view in Object.FindObjectsByType<BattlePresentation>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (view.Session == owner) { presentation = view; break; }
            if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged += OnDamaged;
        }
        private void OnDamaged(DamageEvent value) => damaged.Add(value.UnitId);
        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            var camera = Camera.main;
            if (simulation == null || camera == null) return;
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
            {
                if (!unitBars.TryGetValue(unit.Id, out var bar))
                { bar = Create("Unit HP " + unit.Id, 9, 1.5f, 0.3f); unitBars.Add(unit.Id, bar); }
                var position = unit.Position + Vector3.up * 0.9f;
                Transform model = null;
                if (presentation != null && presentation.TryGetView(unit.Id, out var view)) model = view.transform;
                Present(bar, position, unit.Hp / unit.MaxHp, unit.IsAlive && (damaged.Contains(unit.Id) || unit.Hp < unit.MaxHp), camera, model);
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < simulation.Towers.Count; i++)
            {
                var tower = simulation.Towers[i];
                if (!towerBars.TryGetValue(tower.Id, out var bar))
                { bar = Create("Tower HP " + tower.Id, 28, 3.5f, 0.6f); towerBars.Add(tower.Id, bar); }
                Present(bar, tower.Position + Vector3.up * 1.5f, tower.Hp / tower.Definition.MaxHp, tower.IsAlive, camera, session.Towers[i].transform);
            }
        }
        private Bar Create(string name, float width, float height, float inset)
        {
            var background = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, name, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(width, height), backgroundColor);
            var rect = background.rectTransform;
            rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = Vector2.zero;
            var fill = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(rect, "Fill", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(width - inset * 2, height - inset * 2), fullColor);
            fill.rectTransform.anchorMin = fill.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);
            fill.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);
            fill.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(inset, 0);
            return new Bar { Root = rect, Fill = fill, Width = width, Height = height, Inset = inset };
        }
        private void Present(Bar bar, Vector3 position, float fraction, bool visible, Camera camera, Transform model)
        {
            var screen = camera.WorldToScreenPoint(position);
            if (model != null)
            {
                var renderers = model.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>();
                if (renderers.Length > 0)
                {
                    var bounds = renderers[0].bounds;
                    foreach (var renderer in renderers) bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
                    var top = float.MinValue;
                    for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
                    for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
                    for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
                        top = Mathf.Max(top, camera.WorldToScreenPoint(bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z))).y);
                    screen = camera.WorldToScreenPoint(bounds.center);
                    screen.y = top + (bar.Height / 2 + 1) * GetComponentInParent<Canvas>().scaleFactor;
                }
            }
            bar.Root.gameObject.SetActive(visible && screen.z > 0);
            if (!visible) return;
            if (RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle((RectTransform)transform, screen, null, out var local))
                bar.Root.anchoredPosition = local;
            fraction = Mathf.Clamp01(fraction);
            bar.Fill.rectTransform.sizeDelta = new Vector2((bar.Width - bar.Inset * 2) * fraction, bar.Height - bar.Inset * 2);
            bar.Fill.color = Color.Lerp(emptyColor, fullColor, fraction);
        }
        private void OnDisable() { if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged; }
    }
}

using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Adapted from Team5 HowToPlay.ShowLevelOneDefeat and OutOfLivesScreen.
    public sealed class LegacyLevelOneRecovery : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        public static GameObject Open(BattleSession session)
        {
            var tutorial = session.GetComponent<LevelOneTutorial>();
            if (tutorial != null) tutorial.SuspendForDefeat();
            var host = new GameObject("Level Recovery", typeof(RectTransform));
            // A nested Canvas inherits the HUD's reference scale and ignores its own scaler.
            // Parent under the non-UI session so this is a root Canvas with its own sizing.
            host.transform.SetParent(session.transform, false);
            var view = host.AddComponent<LegacyLevelOneRecovery>();
            view.session = session;
            view.Build();
            return host;
        }

        private void Build()
        {
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(gameObject, 2000, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f);
            var backdrop = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Backdrop", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0.025f, 0.035f, 0.055f, 0.95f));
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(backdrop.rectTransform); backdrop.raycastTarget = true;
            var card = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Recovery Card", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(760, 400), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 12).transform;
            var exhausted = session.AttemptsRemaining == 0;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(card, "Title", exhausted ? "OUT OF LIVES" : "SQUAD DEFEATED", new Vector2(0, 140), new Vector2(700, 55), 34, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(card, "Description", exhausted
                ? $"You have used all {session.AttemptsPerLevel} lives.\nExit and restart the game to try again."
                : $"Lives: {session.AttemptsRemaining}/{session.AttemptsPerLevel}\nRestart, or return to the last tutorial hint.\nRewinding does not refund a lost life.",
                new Vector2(0, 45), new Vector2(700, 110), 23, LegacyUiKit.TextColor);
            if (!exhausted)
            {
                Button(card, "Restart", "RESTART LEVEL", new Vector2(-170, -50), BattleSceneFlow.RestartCurrentLevel);
                var rewind = Button(card, "Rewind", "BACK TO LAST HINT", new Vector2(170, -50), () => BattleSceneFlow.RewindLevelOne());
                rewind.interactable = session.HasTutorialCheckpoint;
            }
            Button(card, "Exit", "EXIT & RESTART GAME", new Vector2(0, -140), BattleSceneFlow.ReturnToStart);
        }

        private static Button Button(Transform parent, string name, string label, Vector2 position, UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction action)
        {
            var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(parent, name, position, new Vector2(320, 58), LegacyUiKit.NodeBlueColor, 6);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(button.transform, "Label", label, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(310, 54), 21, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            button.onClick.AddListener(action);
            return button;
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (session != null && session.RecoveryRequired) return;
            gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(gameObject);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyLevelSettlement.cs ===
using System.Linq;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Reuses Team5 Level2Intro's authored layout and persistent Continue button.
    public sealed class LegacyLevelSettlement : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text missionHeading;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rewardHeading;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rewardAmount;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rewardDescription;
        private readonly List<LegacyUnitIconUI> rewardIcons = new List<LegacyUnitIconUI>();
        private float rewardFontSize;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            foreach (var icon in rewardIcons)
                if (icon != null) { icon.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(icon.gameObject); }
            rewardIcons.Clear();
            var victory = BattleSceneFlow.LastOutcome != BattleOutcome.Defeat;
            var level = Mathf.Max(1, BattleSceneFlow.CompletedLevelNumber);
            missionHeading.text = victory ? "MISSION COMPLETE" : "MISSION FAILED";
            rewardHeading.text = $"LEVEL {level} {(victory ? "COMPLETE" : "RETRY")}";
            if (victory)
            {
                var gold = BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardGold;
                var units = BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardUnits;
                // Direct Scene Play previews configured rewards without awarding anything.
                if (!BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive)
                {
                    var settings = Resources.Load<GameEconomySettings>("RebuildEconomy");
                    var reward = settings != null ? settings.RewardForLevel(level) : null;
                    gold = reward != null ? reward.gold : 0;
                    units = reward != null ? reward.CaptureUnitRoles() : System.Array.Empty<UnitRole>();
                }
                rewardAmount.text = gold > 0 ? $"+{gold:N0} GOLD" : "REWARDS";
                rewardDescription.text = string.Join("\n", units.GroupBy(role => role).Select(group =>
                    $"+{group.Count()} {LegacyUnitLooks.Name(group.Key)} {(group.Count() == 1 ? "UNIT" : "UNITS")}"));
                if (gold == 0 && units.Count == 0) rewardDescription.text = "No rewards for this level.";
                if (units.Count > 0)
                {
                    rewardFontSize = rewardAmount.fontSizeMax;
                    rewardAmount.enableAutoSizing = rewardDescription.enableAutoSizing = false;
                    rewardAmount.fontSize = rewardDescription.fontSize = rewardFontSize;
                    rewardDescription.font = rewardAmount.font;
                    rewardDescription.fontStyle = rewardAmount.fontStyle;
                    rewardDescription.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
                    foreach (var group in units.GroupBy(role => role))
                    {
                        var size = rewardFontSize * 0.8f;
                        var icon = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(rewardDescription.transform, Vector2.zero,
                            size / LegacyUnitLooks.For(group.Key).OuterSize);
                        icon.SetType(group.Key);
                        rewardIcons.Add(icon);
                    }
                }
            }
            else
            {
                rewardAmount.text = "REGROUP";
                rewardDescription.text = level == 1 ? "Try again with your Captain. Level 1 uses its fixed squad."
                    : "Rearrange your squad on the Upgrade screen, then retry this level.";
            }
        }

        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            if (rewardIcons.Count == 0) return;
            // Use one font size for both rewards, fitting the text and trailing icon together.
            rewardDescription.fontSize = rewardFontSize;
            rewardDescription.margin = Vector4.zero;
            var width = rewardDescription.GetPreferredValues(rewardDescription.text).x;
            var iconSize = rewardFontSize * 0.8f;
            var gap = rewardFontSize * 0.2f;
            var available = rewardDescription.rectTransform.rect.width;
            var scale = Mathf.Min(1, available / Mathf.Max(1, width + iconSize + gap));
            rewardAmount.fontSize = rewardDescription.fontSize = rewardFontSize * scale;
            iconSize *= scale; gap *= scale;
            rewardDescription.margin = new Vector4(0, 0, iconSize + gap, 0);
            rewardDescription.ForceMeshUpdate();
            var info = rewardDescription.textInfo;
            for (var i = 0; i < rewardIcons.Count && i < info.lineCount; i++)
            {
                var line = info.lineInfo[i];
                var end = info.characterInfo[line.lastVisibleCharacterIndex].topRight.x;
                rewardIcons[i].Rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(end + gap + iconSize / 2,
                    (line.ascender + line.descender) / 2);
                rewardIcons[i].Rect.localScale = Vector3.one * scale;
            }
        }

        public void ContinueToUpgrade() => BattleSceneFlow.ContinueToUpgrade();
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyNextLevelPreview.cs ===
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Loads the actual upcoming Scene temporarily, without simulation or battle UI.
    public sealed class LegacyNextLevelPreview : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const int PreviewLayer = 31;
        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;
        private string path;
        private Scene scene;
        private AsyncOperation load;
        private RawImage image;
        private TMP_Text status;
        private RenderTexture texture;
        private Camera previewCamera;
        private bool closed, loadRunning = true, finishing;
        public bool IsReady { get; private set; }
        public Scene LoadedScene => scene;

        public static LegacyNextLevelPreview Open(LegacyUpgradeScene owner)
        {
            if (!Application.CanStreamedLevelBeLoaded(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleScenePath) || !BattleSceneFlow.TryBeginLevelPreview()) return null;
            var host = new GameObject("Next Level Preview", typeof(RectTransform));
            var view = host.AddComponent<LegacyNextLevelPreview>();
            view.owner = owner; view.path = BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleScenePath;
            view.Build(); view.StartCoroutine(view.LoadMap());
            return view;
        }

        private void Build()
        {
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(gameObject, 500, new Vector2(1280,720),.5f);
            var background = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform,"Background",Vector2.zero,Vector2.zero,LegacyUiKit.CellColor);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(background.rectTransform); background.raycastTarget = true;
            var map = new GameObject("Map",typeof(RectTransform),typeof(CanvasRenderer),typeof(RawImage));
            map.transform.SetParent(transform,false); image = map.GetComponent<RawImage>();
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(image.rectTransform); image.raycastTarget = false; image.enabled = false;
            status = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(transform,"Status","Loading map...",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(1000,60),26,Color.white);
            var title = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(transform,"Title",$"LEVEL {BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber} PREVIEW",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(700,50),26,Color.white,FontStyles.Bold);
            title.rectTransform.anchorMin = title.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(.5f,1);
            title.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(.5f,1); title.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0,-18);
            var close = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(transform,"Close",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(52,48),LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor,6);
            var rect = (RectTransform)close.transform;
            rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = rect.pivot = Vector2.one; rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-20,-18);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(close.transform,"Label","X",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(48,44),27,Color.white,FontStyles.Bold);
            close.onClick.AddListener(Close);
        }

        private IEnumerator LoadMap()
        {
            try { load = SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(path,LoadSceneMode.Additive); }
            catch (System.Exception error) { status.text = "Unable to load map."; Debug.LogException(error,this); }
            if (load == null) { loadRunning = false; if(closed) yield return Finish(); yield break; }
            yield return load;
            scene = SceneManager.GetSceneByPath(path);
            if (closed) { loadRunning = false; yield return Finish(); yield break; }
            var prepared = false;
            try { prepared = PrepareMap(); }
            catch(System.Exception error) { Debug.LogException(error,this); }
            if (!prepared) { loadRunning = false; status.text = "Unable to display map."; yield break; }
            yield return null; // Let the camera render once before displaying its texture.
            loadRunning = false;
            if (closed) { yield return Finish(); yield break; }
            image.texture = texture; image.enabled = true; status.gameObject.SetActive(false); IsReady = true;
        }

        private bool PrepareMap()
        {
            var bounds = new Bounds(); var found = false;
            var rotation = Quaternion.Euler(55,0,0);
            var towers = new List<BattleTower>();
            foreach(var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
            {
                towers.AddRange(root.GetComponentsInChildren<BattleTower>(true));
                foreach(var camera in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Camera>(true)) { rotation = camera.transform.rotation; camera.enabled = false; }
                foreach(var listener in root.GetComponentsInChildren<AudioListener>(true)) listener.enabled = false;
                foreach(var canvas in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Canvas>(true)) canvas.enabled = false;
                foreach(var light in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Light>(true)) light.enabled = false;
                foreach(var collider in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>(true)) collider.enabled = false;
                foreach(var script in root.GetComponentsInChildren<MonoBehaviour>(true)) script.enabled = false;
                foreach(var child in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true)) child.gameObject.layer = PreviewLayer;
                foreach(var renderer in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>(true))
                {
                    if(!renderer.enabled || !renderer.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) continue;
                    if(!found) { bounds = renderer.bounds; found = true; } else bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
                }
            }
            if(!found) return false;
            var towerId = 0;
            foreach(var tower in towers)
            {
                if(!tower.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) continue;
                var rangeObject = new GameObject("Preview Tower Range " + ++towerId);
                SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(rangeObject,scene);
                var range = rangeObject.AddComponent<TowerRangeView>();
                range.BindPreview(tower.CaptureDefinition(towerId));
                foreach(var child in rangeObject.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true)) child.gameObject.layer = PreviewLayer;
                bounds.Encapsulate(range.DisplayBounds);
            }
            var height = Mathf.Clamp(Screen.height,360,1080);
            var width = Mathf.RoundToInt(height * (float)Screen.width / Mathf.Max(1,Screen.height));
            texture = new RenderTexture(width,height,24) { name = "Level Preview", antiAliasing = 4 };
            texture.Create();
            var cameraObject = new GameObject("Preview Camera"); SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(cameraObject,scene);
            previewCamera = cameraObject.AddComponent<Camera>();
            previewCamera.targetTexture = texture; previewCamera.cullingMask = 1 << PreviewLayer;
            previewCamera.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor; previewCamera.backgroundColor = LegacyUiKit.CellColor;
            previewCamera.orthographic = true; previewCamera.aspect = (float)width/height;
            var extent = Vector3.zero;
            for(var x=-1;x<=1;x+=2) for(var y=-1;y<=1;y+=2) for(var z=-1;z<=1;z+=2)
            {
                var p = Quaternion.Inverse(rotation)*Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents,new Vector3(x,y,z));
                extent = Vector3.Max(extent,new Vector3(Mathf.Abs(p.x),Mathf.Abs(p.y),Mathf.Abs(p.z)));
            }
            previewCamera.orthographicSize = Mathf.Max(1,extent.y,extent.x/previewCamera.aspect)*1.2f;
            previewCamera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(bounds.center-rotation*Vector3.forward*(extent.z+50),rotation);
            previewCamera.nearClipPlane = .1f; previewCamera.farClipPlane = extent.z*2+100;
            var lightObject = new GameObject("Preview Light"); SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(lightObject,scene);
            var lightSource = lightObject.AddComponent<Light>(); lightSource.type = LightType.Directional;
            lightSource.intensity = 1.2f; lightSource.cullingMask = 1 << PreviewLayer; lightSource.shadows = LightShadows.None;
            lightObject.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(50,-30,0);
            return true;
        }

        public void Close()
        {
            if(closed) return;
            closed = true; IsReady = false;
            if(!loadRunning) StartCoroutine(Finish());
        }
        private IEnumerator Finish()
        {
            if(finishing) yield break;
            finishing = true; image.enabled = false; status.gameObject.SetActive(true); status.text = "Closing preview...";
            if(scene.IsValid() && scene.isLoaded) yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(scene);
            BattleSceneFlow.EndLevelPreview(path);
            if(owner != null) owner.OnNextLevelPreviewClosed();
            Destroy(gameObject);
        }
        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if(previewCamera != null) { previewCamera.enabled = false; previewCamera.targetTexture = null; }
            if(texture != null) { texture.Release(); Destroy(texture); }
            if(scene.IsValid() && scene.isLoaded) SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(scene);
            if(load != null && !load.isDone)
            {
                var pendingPath = path;
                UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction<Scene,LoadSceneMode> cleanup = null;
                cleanup = (loaded,mode) =>
                {
                    if(loaded.path != pendingPath) return;
                    SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= cleanup;
                    foreach(var root in loaded.GetRootGameObjects()) root.SetActive(false);
                    SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(loaded);
                    BattleSceneFlow.EndLevelPreview(pendingPath);
                };
                SceneManager.sceneLoaded += cleanup;
            }
            else BattleSceneFlow.EndLevelPreview(path);
        }
    }
}

using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public static class LegacyUiKit
    {

        public static string LifeCounterText(int remaining, int limit)
        {
            return $": {Mathf.Clamp(remaining, 0, limit)}/{limit}";
        }

        public static void CreateHeartIcon(Transform parent, Vector2 topLeftPosition, float scale = 1f)
        {
            var root = CreateRect(parent, "Heart Icon", topLeftPosition, new Vector2(24f, 24f));
            root.anchorMin = root.anchorMax = root.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
            root.anchoredPosition = topLeftPosition;
            root.localScale = new Vector3(scale, scale, 1f);
            Color red = new Color(1f, 0.2f, 0.3f, 1f);
            // Reuse existing UI shapes so the heart does not depend on font glyph support.
            Image tip = CreateImage(root, "Heart Tip", new Vector2(0f, -2f), new Vector2(14f, 14f), red);
            tip.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 45f);
            CreateCircle(root, "Heart Left", new Vector2(-5f, 3f), 14f, red);
            CreateCircle(root, "Heart Right", new Vector2(5f, 3f), 14f, red);
        }

        public static readonly Color PanelColor = new Color(0.11f, 0.14f, 0.21f);
        public static readonly Color PanelLineColor = new Color(0.3f, 0.36f, 0.46f);
        public static readonly Color CellColor = new Color(0.07f, 0.09f, 0.14f);
        public static readonly Color TextColor = new Color(0.92f, 0.94f, 0.97f);
        public static readonly Color MutedColor = new Color(0.62f, 0.67f, 0.75f);
        public static readonly Color HighlightColor = new Color(1f, 0.88f, 0.25f);
        public static readonly Color NodeGreenColor = new Color(0.18f, 0.65f, 0.22f);
        public static readonly Color NodeGreyColor = new Color(0.25f, 0.25f, 0.25f);
        public static readonly Color NodeBlueColor = new Color(0.15f, 0.35f, 0.7f);
        public static readonly Color DropColor = new Color(0.35f, 0.85f, 0.45f);
        public static readonly Color LockColor = new Color(0.86f, 0.88f, 0.92f);

        private const int RoundedTextureSize = 64;
        private const float RoundedTextureRadius = 16f;
        private const float RoundedPixelsPerUnit = 100f;

        private static Sprite rounded;

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics()
        {
            rounded = null;
        }

        public static Canvas CreateCanvas(GameObject host, int sortingOrder, Vector2 referenceResolution, float matchWidthOrHeight)
        {
            var canvas = host.AddComponent<Canvas>();
            canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
            canvas.sortingOrder = sortingOrder;

            var scaler = host.AddComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
            scaler.referenceResolution = referenceResolution;
            scaler.screenMatchMode = CanvasScaler.ScreenMatchMode.MatchWidthOrHeight;
            scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = matchWidthOrHeight;

            host.AddComponent<GraphicRaycaster>();
            return canvas;
        }

        public static RectTransform CreateRect(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size)
        {
            var rect = (RectTransform)new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform)).transform;
            rect.SetParent(parent, false);
            rect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.anchoredPosition = position;
            rect.sizeDelta = size;
            return rect;
        }

        public static void Stretch(RectTransform rect)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rect.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        }

        public static Image CreateImage(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, Color color, float radius = 0f)
        {
            Image image = CreateRect(parent, name, position, size).gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
            image.color = color;
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            if (radius > 0f)
            {
                Round(image, radius);
            }
            return image;
        }

        public static Image CreateCircle(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, float diameter, Color color)
        {
            Image image = CreateImage(parent, name, position, new Vector2(diameter, diameter), color);
            image.sprite = LegacyUiShapes.Circle;
            return image;
        }

        public static void Round(Image image, float radius)
        {
            image.sprite = RoundedSprite;
            image.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
            image.pixelsPerUnitMultiplier = RoundedTextureRadius / radius;
        }

        public static TextMeshProUGUI CreateText(Transform parent, string name, string text, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, float fontSize,
            Color color, FontStyles style = FontStyles.Normal, float spacing = 0f)
        {
            TextMeshProUGUI label = CreateRect(parent, name, position, size).gameObject.AddComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
            if (TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset != null)
            {
                label.font = TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
            }
            label.text = text;
            label.fontSize = fontSize;
            label.color = color;
            label.fontStyle = style;
            label.characterSpacing = spacing;
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            label.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
            label.richText = true;
            label.raycastTarget = false;
            return label;
        }

        public static Button MakeButton(Image image)
        {
            image.raycastTarget = true;
            var button = image.gameObject.AddComponent<Button>();
            button.targetGraphic = image;
            button.navigation = new Navigation { mode = Navigation.Mode.None };
            return button;
        }

        public static Button CreateButton(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, Color color, float radius)
        {
            return MakeButton(CreateImage(parent, name, position, size, color, radius));
        }

        public static RectTransform CreateLock(Transform parent, Vector2 position, float scale)
        {
            RectTransform root = CreateRect(parent, "Lock", position, new Vector2(30f, 38f) * scale);
            CreateImage(root, "Shackle Left", new Vector2(-9f, 8f) * scale, new Vector2(5f, 16f) * scale, LockColor);
            CreateImage(root, "Shackle Right", new Vector2(9f, 8f) * scale, new Vector2(5f, 16f) * scale, LockColor);
            CreateImage(root, "Shackle Top", new Vector2(0f, 15f) * scale, new Vector2(23f, 5f) * scale, LockColor, 2.5f * scale);
            CreateImage(root, "Body", new Vector2(0f, -5f) * scale, new Vector2(30f, 22f) * scale, LockColor, 4f * scale);
            CreateCircle(root, "Keyhole", new Vector2(0f, -4f) * scale, 6.5f * scale, PanelColor);
            return root;
        }

        private static Sprite RoundedSprite
        {
            get
            {
                if (rounded == null)
                {
                    rounded = CreateRoundedSprite();
                }
                return rounded;
            }
        }

        private static Sprite CreateRoundedSprite()
        {
            int size = RoundedTextureSize;
            float radius = RoundedTextureRadius;
            float half = size * 0.5f;
            float inner = half - radius;

            var texture = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false)
            {
                name = "RK Rounded Panel",
                wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp,
                filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear
            };

            var pixels = new Color32[size * size];
            for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    float dx = Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(x + 0.5f - half) - inner, 0f);
                    float dy = Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(y + 0.5f - half) - inner, 0f);
                    float alpha = Mathf.Clamp01(radius - Mathf.Sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) + 0.5f);
                    pixels[y * size + x] = new Color32(255, 255, 255, (byte)Mathf.RoundToInt(alpha * 255f));
                }
            }
            texture.SetPixels32(pixels);
            texture.Apply(false, true);

            return Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0f, 0f, size, size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), RoundedPixelsPerUnit, 0,
                SpriteMeshType.FullRect, new Vector4(radius, radius, radius, radius));
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyUiShapes.cs ===
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Only the circle factory needed by UiKit, copied from old ShapeSprites.
    public static class LegacyUiShapes
    {
        private static Sprite circle;
        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics() => circle = null;
        public static Sprite Circle
        {
            get
            {
                if (circle != null) return circle;
                const int size = 64;
                var texture = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false)
                { name = "RK Circle", wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp, filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear };
                var center = Vector2.one * (size * 0.5f);
                var pixels = new Color32[size * size];
                for (var y = 0; y < size; y++)
                for (var x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    var distance = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f), center);
                    var alpha = Mathf.Clamp01(size * 0.5f - distance + 0.5f);
                    pixels[y * size + x] = new Color32(255, 255, 255, (byte)Mathf.RoundToInt(alpha * 255));
                }
                texture.SetPixels32(pixels); texture.Apply(false, true);
                circle = Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, size, size), Vector2.one * 0.5f, size, 0, SpriteMeshType.FullRect);
                circle.name = "RK Circle";
                return circle;
            }
        }
    }
}
