using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using TMPro;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Shared introduction content for warehouse members and Claim Card previews.
    public static class LegacyUnitIntroductionUI
    {
        public static TMP_Text Build(Transform panel, UnitDefinition stats, bool showAvailability = true)
        {
            var role = stats.Role;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(panel, "Emblem Ring", new Vector2(0, 75), 76, LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor);
            var face = LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(panel, "Emblem", new Vector2(0, 75), 70, LegacyUiKit.PanelColor);
            LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(face.transform, Vector2.zero, 76 * 1.15f).SetType(role);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Name", LegacyUnitLooks.Name(role), new Vector2(0, 15), new Vector2(200, 24), 18,
                LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold, 4);
            var status = showAvailability ? LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Availability", "", new Vector2(0, -8), new Vector2(200, 20), 14, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor) : null;
            var description = role == UnitRole.Captain ? "Shoots towers / DEF 50%" : role == UnitRole.Tank ? "Takes less damage" : role == UnitRole.Heal ? "Heals neighbors" : "Shoots towers";
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Role", description, new Vector2(0, -32), new Vector2(200, 20), 13, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(panel, "Divider", new Vector2(0, -52), new Vector2(160, 2), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor);
            var key = role == UnitRole.Attack || role == UnitRole.Captain ? "ATK" : role == UnitRole.Tank ? "DEF" : "HEAL";
            var value = role == UnitRole.Attack || role == UnitRole.Captain ? $"{stats.Damage:0.##}" : role == UnitRole.Tank ? $"{stats.Defense * 100:0}%" : $"{stats.HealPerSecond:0.##}/s";
            BuildStat(panel, key, value, -76);
            BuildStat(panel, "HP", $"{stats.MaxHp:0.##}", -104);
            return status;
        }

        private static void BuildStat(Transform panel, string name, string value, float y)
        {
            var size = new Vector2(148, 24);
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Stat", name, new Vector2(0, y), size, 14, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor, FontStyles.Bold, 2);
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Left;
            var number = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Value", value, new Vector2(0, y), size, 18, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            number.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Right;
        }
    }

    public class LegacyUnitIconUI : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const float ShadowAlpha = 0.5f;

        private static readonly Vector2 shadowOffset = new Vector2(0.03f, -0.05f);

        private RectTransform rect;
        private float pixelsPerUnit;
        private UnitRole shown = UnitRole.Test;

        public RectTransform Rect => rect;

        public static LegacyUnitIconUI Create(Transform parent, Vector2 position, float pixelsPerUnit)
        {
            RectTransform rect = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(parent, "Unit Icon", position, Vector2.one * pixelsPerUnit * 0.5f);
            var icon = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyUnitIconUI>();
            icon.rect = rect;
            icon.pixelsPerUnit = pixelsPerUnit;
            return icon;
        }

        public void SetType(UnitRole type)
        {
            if (type == shown)
            {
                return;
            }
            shown = type;

            for (int i = transform.childCount - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                Destroy(transform.GetChild(i).gameObject);
            }
            if (type == UnitRole.Test)
            {
                return;
            }

            SoldierLook look = LegacyUnitLooks.For(type);
            float k = pixelsPerUnit;
            Vector2 outer = Vector2.one * look.OuterSize * k;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Shadow", shadowOffset * k, outer, new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, ShadowAlpha));
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Outline", Vector2.zero, outer, look.Outline);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Body", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * look.InnerSize * k, look.Body);

            switch (look.Mark)
            {
                case SoldierMark.Crown:
                    for (var i = 0; i < SoldierLook.CrownSegments.Length; i += 2)
                    {
                        var start = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i];
                        var end = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i + 1];
                        var delta = end - start;
                        var line = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Crown " + i, (start + end) * (k / 2),
                            new Vector2(delta.magnitude, SoldierLook.CrownStroke) * k, look.MarkColor);
                        line.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
                    }
                    break;
                case SoldierMark.Diamond:
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * SoldierLook.DiamondSize * k, look.MarkColor)
                        .rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 45f);
                    break;
                case SoldierMark.Plate:
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * SoldierLook.PlateSize * k, look.MarkColor);
                    break;
                case SoldierMark.Cross:
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark Across", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(SoldierLook.CrossLength, SoldierLook.CrossWidth) * k, look.MarkColor);
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark Upright", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(SoldierLook.CrossWidth, SoldierLook.CrossLength) * k, look.MarkColor);
                    break;
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyUnitLooks.cs ===
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public static class LegacyUnitLooks
    {
        private const float OuterSize = 0.4f;
        private const float InnerSize = 0.34f;
        private const float TankOuterSize = 0.44f;
        private const float TankInnerSize = 0.3f;
        private const float DiamondTint = 0.6f;

        private static readonly Color attackBody = new Color(0.95f, 0.35f, 0.3f);
        private static readonly Color attackOutline = new Color(0.35f, 0.08f, 0.08f);
        private static readonly Color tankBody = new Color(0.5f, 0.56f, 0.65f);
        private static readonly Color tankOutline = new Color(0.16f, 0.18f, 0.22f);
        private static readonly Color tankPlate = new Color(0.74f, 0.79f, 0.86f);
        private static readonly Color healBody = new Color(0.3f, 0.78f, 0.45f);
        private static readonly Color healOutline = new Color(0.07f, 0.26f, 0.12f);
        private static readonly Color healCross = new Color(0.96f, 1f, 0.97f);

        public static SoldierLook For(UnitRole type)
        {
            switch (type)
            {
                case UnitRole.Captain:
                    return new SoldierLook(new Color(0.72f, 0.56f, 0.28f), new Color(0.36f, 0.26f, 0.10f),
                        OuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Crown, Color.white);
                case UnitRole.Tank:
                    return new SoldierLook(tankBody, tankOutline, TankOuterSize, TankInnerSize, SoldierMark.Plate, tankPlate);
                case UnitRole.Heal:
                    return new SoldierLook(healBody, healOutline, OuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Cross, healCross);
                default:
                    return new SoldierLook(attackBody, attackOutline, OuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Diamond,
                        Color.Lerp(attackBody, Color.white, DiamondTint));
            }
        }

        public static string Name(UnitRole type)
        {
            switch (type)
            {
                case UnitRole.Attack:
                    return "ATTACK";
                case UnitRole.Tank:
                    return "TANK";
                case UnitRole.Heal:
                    return "HEAL";
                case UnitRole.Captain:
                    return "CAPTAIN";
                default:
                    return string.Empty;
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyUpgradeScene.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Warehouse introductions reuse the old SquadScreen style; only selected IDs enter battle.
    public sealed class LegacyUpgradeScene : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private SquadRoster roster;
        [SerializeField] private Button startGameButton;
        private GameObject screen;
        private TMP_Text countLabel, warehouseHint, levelLabel, goldLabel;
        private RectTransform levelHeart;
        private RectTransform screenRoot;
        private readonly List<GameObject> warehouseCells = new List<GameObject>();
        private MemberSpawnDefinition[] renderedMembers;
        private Button claimButton;
        private TMP_Text claimLabel, quotaLabel;
        private Image quotaFill;
        private LegacyClaimCardOverlay claimOverlay;
        private Button previewButton;
        private LegacyNextLevelPreview levelPreview;
        public Button PreviewNextLevelButton => previewButton;
        public Button ClaimCardButton => claimButton;
        public TMP_Text GoldLabel => goldLabel;
        private readonly Dictionary<int, CanvasGroup> cardGroups = new Dictionary<int, CanvasGroup>();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, TMP_Text> cardStatuses = new Dictionary<int, TMP_Text>();
        public SquadRoster Roster => roster;
        public SquadPreparation Preparation { get; private set; }
        public LegacyFormationGrid Grid { get; private set; }
        public Button StartWaveButton => startGameButton;
        public TMP_Text LevelLabel => levelLabel;

        private void Start()
        {
            Preparation = BattleSceneFlow.PrepareSquad(roster);
            screen = new GameObject("Squad Screen", typeof(RectTransform));
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(screen, 10, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0);
            var root = (RectTransform)screen.transform;
            screenRoot = root;
            goldLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Gold Balance", "", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(260, 48),
                30, LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            goldLabel.font = startGameButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true).font;
            goldLabel.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Right;
            goldLabel.rectTransform.anchorMin = goldLabel.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(1, 1);
            goldLabel.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(1, 1);
            goldLabel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-28, -20);
            RefreshGoldLabel();
            // Keep Team5's level label style, positioned above the authored Start Wave button.
            levelLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Level Label", "", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(360, 60),
                36, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            levelLabel.font = startGameButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true).font;
            levelLabel.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Bottom;
            levelLabel.rectTransform.anchorMin = levelLabel.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            levelLabel.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateHeartIcon(levelLabel.transform, Vector2.zero);
            levelHeart = (RectTransform)levelLabel.transform.Find("Heart Icon");
            levelHeart.anchorMin = levelHeart.anchorMax = levelHeart.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            previewButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(root,"Preview Next Level",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(240,48),LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor,6);
            var previewLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(previewButton.transform,"Label","Preview Next Level",Vector2.zero,Vector2.zero,24,Color.white);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(previewLabel.rectTransform);
            previewLabel.font = levelLabel.font; previewLabel.enableAutoSizing = true;
            previewLabel.fontSizeMin = 14; previewLabel.fontSizeMax = 24;
            var previewRect = (RectTransform)previewButton.transform;
            previewRect.anchorMin = previewRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(.5f,.5f); previewRect.pivot = new Vector2(.5f,0);
            previewButton.onClick.AddListener(OpenNextLevelPreview);
            RefreshLevelLabel();
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Warehouse Title", "UNIT STORAGE", new Vector2(-165, 300), new Vector2(780, 30),
                24, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold, 3);
            for (var index = 0; index < SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; index++) BuildWarehouseCell(root, index);
            renderedMembers = Preparation.Members.ToArray();
            var quota = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root,"Card Quota",new Vector2(-165,-160),new Vector2(330,10),LegacyUiKit.CellColor,5);
            quotaFill = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(quota.transform,"Fill",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(330,10),LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor,5);
            quotaFill.rectTransform.anchorMin = quotaFill.rectTransform.anchorMax = quotaFill.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0,.5f);
            quotaLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root,"Card Quota Text","",new Vector2(-165,-140),new Vector2(600,26),18,LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor);
            claimButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(root,"Claim Card Button",new Vector2(-165,-210),new Vector2(330,58),LegacyUiKit.NodeGreenColor,6);
            claimLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(claimButton.transform,"Label","CLAIM CARD",Vector2.zero,new Vector2(320,54),22,Color.white,FontStyles.Bold);
            claimButton.onClick.AddListener(OpenClaimCards);
            warehouseHint = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Warehouse Hint", "Drag units into the squad", new Vector2(-165, -115),
                new Vector2(780, 26), 16, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            Grid = LegacyFormationGrid.CreateForPreparation(root, root, Preparation);
            ((RectTransform)Grid.transform).anchoredPosition = new Vector2(465, 43);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(Grid.transform, "Title", "SQUAD", new Vector2(0, 151), new Vector2(310, 26),
                18, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold, 3);
            countLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(Grid.transform, "Squad Count", "", new Vector2(0, 189), new Vector2(310, 30),
                26, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            Preparation.Changed += Refresh;
            startGameButton.onClick.AddListener(StartWave);
            Refresh();
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            RefreshLevelLabel(); RefreshGoldLabel(); RefreshClaimCards();
            if (startGameButton != null && Preparation != null)
                startGameButton.interactable = Preparation.CanStart && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives && claimOverlay == null && levelPreview == null;
            if(previewButton != null) previewButton.interactable = levelPreview == null && claimOverlay == null && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives;
        }

        public void OpenNextLevelPreview()
        {
            if(levelPreview != null || claimOverlay != null || BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives) return;
            levelPreview = LegacyNextLevelPreview.Open(this);
        }
        public void OnNextLevelPreviewClosed() { levelPreview = null; Refresh(); }

        public void OpenClaimCards()
        {
            if(claimOverlay != null || levelPreview != null || BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives) return;
            claimOverlay = LegacyClaimCardOverlay.Open(this);
            RefreshClaimCards();
        }
        public void OnClaimCardsClosed() { claimOverlay = null; Refresh(); RefreshClaimCards(); }
        private void RefreshClaimCards()
        {
            var shop = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop;
            if(claimButton == null || shop == null) return;
            claimButton.interactable = shop.CanClaim && claimOverlay == null && levelPreview == null && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives;
            claimButton.image.color = claimButton.interactable ? LegacyUiKit.NodeGreenColor : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
            claimLabel.text = shop.HasNextCard ? $"CLAIM CARD ({shop.NextPrice} GOLD)" : "ALL CARDS CLAIMED";
            quotaLabel.text = shop.HasNextCard ? $"{BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance} / {shop.NextPrice} gold in bank" : "All cards taken";
            quotaFill.enabled = shop.HasNextCard && BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance > 0;
            quotaFill.rectTransform.sizeDelta = new Vector2(shop.HasNextCard ? 330 * Mathf.Clamp01((float)BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance / shop.NextPrice) : 0,10);
        }

        private void RefreshWarehouseCards()
        {
            if(renderedMembers != null && Preparation.Members.SequenceEqual(renderedMembers)) return;
            foreach(var cell in warehouseCells) { cell.SetActive(false); Destroy(cell); }
            warehouseCells.Clear(); cardGroups.Clear(); cardStatuses.Clear();
            for(var index=0; index<SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; index++) BuildWarehouseCell(screenRoot,index);
            renderedMembers = Preparation.Members.ToArray();
        }

        private void RefreshGoldLabel()
        {
            if (goldLabel != null) goldLabel.text = $"$ {BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance:N0}";
        }

        private void RefreshLevelLabel()
        {
            if (levelLabel == null) return;
            var lives = $"{BattleSceneFlow.CurrentLevelLivesRemaining}/{BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel}";
            levelLabel.text = $"LEVEL {Mathf.Max(1, BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber)}  <space=30>{lives}";
            var buttonRect = (RectTransform)startGameButton.transform;
            var top = buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.center.x, buttonRect.rect.yMax, 0));
            var point = levelLabel.rectTransform.parent.InverseTransformPoint(top);
            var labelBottom = point.y + 12;
            if(previewButton != null)
            {
                var parent = levelLabel.rectTransform.parent;
                var left = parent.InverseTransformPoint(buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.xMin,buttonRect.rect.center.y,0)));
                var right = parent.InverseTransformPoint(buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.xMax,buttonRect.rect.center.y,0)));
                var bottom = parent.InverseTransformPoint(buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.center.x,buttonRect.rect.yMin,0)));
                var rect = (RectTransform)previewButton.transform;
                rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(Vector3.Distance(left,right)*.85f,Vector3.Distance(point,bottom)*.75f);
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(point.x,point.y+12);
                labelBottom = rect.anchoredPosition.y + rect.sizeDelta.y + 12;
            }
            levelLabel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(point.x,labelBottom);
            if (levelHeart != null)
            {
                levelLabel.ForceMeshUpdate();
                var info = levelLabel.textInfo;
                if (info.characterCount >= lives.Length)
                {
                    var first = info.characterInfo[info.characterCount - lives.Length];
                    levelHeart.localPosition = new Vector3(first.bottomLeft.x - 18,
                        (first.topRight.y + first.bottomLeft.y) / 2, 0);
                }
            }
        }

        private void BuildWarehouseCell(RectTransform root, int index)
        {
            // Six columns, two rows. Keep the old introduction panel at 60% scale inside each cell.
            var position = new Vector2(-500 + index % SquadPreparation.WarehouseColumns * 134, 185 - index / SquadPreparation.WarehouseColumns * 180);
            var size = new Vector2(200, 262);
            var cell = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Warehouse Cell " + index, position, size + Vector2.one * 4, LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 14).rectTransform;
            warehouseCells.Add(cell.gameObject);
            cell.localScale = Vector3.one * 0.6f;
            var panelImage = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(cell, "Introduction", Vector2.zero, size, LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 12);
            var panel = panelImage.rectTransform;
            if (index >= Preparation.Members.Count)
            {
                LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Empty", "EMPTY", Vector2.zero, size, 18, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
                return;
            }
            var member = Preparation.Members[index];
            panelImage.raycastTarget = true;
            panel.gameObject.AddComponent<WarehouseUnitCardUI>().Initialize(this, root, member);
            cardGroups[member.MemberId] = cell.gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasGroup>();
            cardStatuses[member.MemberId] = LegacyUnitIntroductionUI.Build(panel,member.Unit);
        }

        public void StartWave()
        {
            if (Grid == null || !Preparation.CanStart || BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives || claimOverlay != null || levelPreview != null) return;
            BattleSceneFlow.StartPreparedBattle(Preparation);
        }

        public bool TryPlaceMember(int memberId, PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (Grid.TryPlaceWarehouseMember(memberId, data)) return true;
            warehouseHint.text = Preparation.DeployedCount >= SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed
                ? "Squad full (6/6). Replace or return a unit." : "Drop the unit onto a squad slot.";
            return false;
        }

        private void Refresh()
        {
            RefreshWarehouseCards();
            countLabel.text = $"{Preparation.DeployedCount}/{SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed}";
            startGameButton.interactable = Preparation.CanStart && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives && claimOverlay == null && levelPreview == null;
            warehouseHint.text = BattleSceneFlow.PendingUnitRewardCount > 0
                ? $"Storage full: {BattleSceneFlow.PendingUnitRewardCount} reward units waiting."
                : "Drag units into the squad";
            foreach (var member in Preparation.Members)
            {
                var deployed = Preparation.IsDeployed(member.MemberId);
                cardGroups[member.MemberId].alpha = deployed ? 0.45f : 1;
                cardStatuses[member.MemberId].text = deployed ? "IN SQUAD" : "AVAILABLE";
            }
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if(levelPreview != null) Destroy(levelPreview.gameObject);
            if(claimOverlay != null) Destroy(claimOverlay.gameObject);
            if (startGameButton != null) startGameButton.onClick.RemoveListener(StartWave);
            if (Preparation != null) Preparation.Changed -= Refresh;
            if (screen != null) Destroy(screen);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LevelOneTutorial.cs ===
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Adapted from Team5 HowToPlay's six freeze-frame lessons. Observes simulation
    // state/events and uses the real formation editor; never writes HP, slots or damage.
    [DisallowMultipleComponent, DefaultExecutionOrder(300)]
    public sealed class LevelOneTutorial : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleSession session;
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private LegacyBattleHud hud;
        private BattleUnitInspection inspection;
        private Coroutine routine;
        private GameObject ui, blocker;
        private RectTransform root, banner;
        private TMP_Text title, body;
        private Button nextButton;
        private Camera camera;
        private Vector3 originalPosition, targetPosition;
        private Quaternion originalRotation;
        private float originalOrthoSize, targetOrthoSize;
        private bool cameraOwned, hasCameraSnapshot, ownsPause, wasPaused, next, firstHit;
        private BattlePresentation presentation;
        private Renderer[] glowingRenderers;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock[] originalGlowProperties;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock glowProperties;
        private Image glowingButton;
        private Color originalButtonColor;
        private bool blueWorldGlow;
        private RectTransform squadArrow;
        private readonly Vector3[] buttonCorners = new Vector3[4];
        public int CurrentStep { get; private set; }
        private int TotalSteps => simulation != null && simulation.Towers.Count > 1 ? 8 : 6;
        public bool IsActive => routine != null;
        private int checkpointStep, resumeStep;
        private bool checkpointResult, resumeResult;
        private string checkpointHeading, checkpointMessage;
        private void PrepareRewind() { resumeStep = checkpointStep; resumeResult = checkpointResult; }
        public void SuspendForDefeat() => StopTutorial();

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            if (session == null) session = GetComponent<BattleSession>();
            if (session == null) return;
            session.SimulationCreated += Bind;
            session.TutorialRewindRequested += PrepareRewind;
            Bind(session.Simulation);
        }
        private void Start() { if (!BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene) && session != null) Bind(session.Simulation); }
        private void Bind(BattleSimulation value)
        {
            if (simulation == value && routine != null) return;
            StopTutorial();
            simulation = value;
            if (value == null || !isActiveAndEnabled) return;
            routine = StartCoroutine(Run());
        }
        private void OnDamaged(DamageEvent value)
        {
            // The coroutine acquires pause on the next frame, outside the simulation tick.
            if (Captain() != null && value.UnitId == Captain().Id) firstHit = true;
        }
        private UnitState Captain()
        {
            if (simulation != null)
                foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
                    if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain && unit.IsAlive) return unit;
            return null;
        }
        private bool Running => session != null && session.Simulation == simulation && simulation != null &&
            simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && Captain() != null;

        private IEnumerator Run()
        {
            var startStep = resumeStep;
            var replayResult = resumeResult;
            resumeStep = 0; resumeResult = false;
            yield return null;
            while (simulation != null && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && Captain() == null) yield return null;
            if (!Running) { routine = null; yield break; }
            foreach (var candidate in FindObjectsByType<LegacyBattleHud>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (candidate.Session == session) { hud = candidate; break; }
            foreach (var candidate in FindObjectsByType<BattlePresentation>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (candidate.Session == session) { presentation = candidate; inspection = candidate.GetComponent<BattleUnitInspection>(); break; }
            camera = Camera.main;
            if (hud == null || inspection == null || camera == null) { routine = null; yield break; }
            originalPosition = camera.transform.position; originalRotation = camera.transform.rotation;
            originalOrthoSize = camera.orthographicSize; hasCameraSnapshot = true;
            BuildUi();
            simulation.UnitDamaged += OnDamaged;
            firstHit = startStep >= 3;
            if (startStep == 0) yield return new WaitForSecondsRealtime(0.55f);
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            AcquirePause();
            if (startStep > 0) wasPaused = session.CheckpointPausedBeforeHint;
            if (startStep <= 1)
            {
            Focus(Captain().Position, 2);
            GlowCaptain();
            Show(1, "YOU ARE THE ATTACKER!", "\nThe gold crown is your Captain. It marches automatically.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            }

            var firstTower = simulation.Towers.Count > 0 ? simulation.Towers[0] : null;
            if (startStep <= 2)
            {
            RestoreCamera();
            if (firstTower != null)
            {
                GlowWorld(session.Towers[0].transform, blue: true);
            }
            Show(2, "ENEMY TOWERS", "\nBlue strips mark fixed firing lanes.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            }
            if (startStep <= 3)
            {
            Hide(); RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause();
            while (Running && !firstHit) yield return null;
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            AcquirePause();
            FocusCaptainAndTower(firstTower);
            GlowCaptain();
            Show(3, "LIVE STATS", $"\nThe Captain was hit. Click a unit during normal play to inspect its live stats.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            }
            if (startStep <= 5)
            {
                if (startStep == 5 && replayResult)
                {
                    FocusCaptainAndTower(firstTower); GlowCaptain();
                    Show(5, checkpointHeading, checkpointMessage, true, true);
                    yield return WaitForNext();
                }
                else yield return GuideFormation(4, firstTower, startStep == 5);
            }
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            Hide(); RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause();

            var secondTower = simulation.Towers.Count > 1 ? simulation.Towers[1] : null;
            if (secondTower != null)
            {
                while (Running && secondTower.IsAlive && !Approaching(Captain(), secondTower)) yield return null;
                if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
                if (secondTower.IsAlive)
                {
                    AcquirePause();
                    if (OutsideLane(Captain(), secondTower))
                    {
                        Focus((Captain().Position + secondTower.Position) / 2, Vector3.Distance(Captain().Position, secondTower.Position) / 2 + 2);
                        GlowWorld(session.Towers[1].transform, blue: true);
                        Show(6, "AVOID THE TOWER", "\nThis tower fires upward. Your Captain is already outside its firing line.\nDifferent tower directions require different formation positions.", true);
                        yield return WaitForNext();
                    }
                    else yield return GuideFormation(6, secondTower);
                    Hide(); RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause();
                }
            }
            var route = session.Route.CaptureTopCenters();
            var length = 0f;
            for (var i = 1; i < route.Length; i++) length += Vector3.Distance(route[i - 1], route[i]);
            while (Running && length - Captain().RouteProgress > 6) yield return null;
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            AcquirePause();
            Focus(route[route.Length - 1], 3);
            GlowWorld(session.Route.Cells[session.Route.Cells.Count - 1].transform);
            Show(TotalSteps, "REACH THE GOAL TO WIN!", "\nGet your Captain to the end of the road to win.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            Finish();
        }

        private IEnumerator GuideFormation(int step, TowerState tower, bool reopenEditor = false)
        {
            if (!Running) yield break;
            inspection.CloseImmediately(); RestoreCamera();
            ClearWorldGlow();
            Show(step, step == 4 ? "EDIT YOUR FORMATION" : "AVOID THE TOWER",
                step == 4 ? "\nClick the highlighted squad crown to edit formation."
                    : "Click the highlighted squad crown.\nMove Captain to the <b>left or right column</b>, outside the vertical firing strip. Choose <b>APPLY & RESUME</b>.", false);
            GlowButton(hud.SquadButton(0));
            if (reopenEditor) hud.SelectSquad();
            var moved = false;
            while (Running && !moved)
            {
                var originalSlot = Captain().SlotId;
                while (Running && (hud.FormationEditor == null || !hud.FormationEditor.gameObject.activeSelf)) yield return null;
                if (!Running) yield break;
                ClearButtonGlow();
                Show(step + 1, "EDIT YOUR FORMATION",
                    step == 4 ? "\nDrag your unit <b>down by one cell to avoid attack</b>."
                        : "Move Captain to the <b>left or right column</b>, outside the vertical firing strip. Choose <b>APPLY & RESUME</b>.", false);
                while (Running && hud.FormationEditor != null && hud.FormationEditor.gameObject.activeSelf) yield return null;
                if (!Running) yield break;
                // Let inspection release its view; the editor restores the tutorial's pause.
                yield return null;
                moved = Captain().SlotId != originalSlot && (tower == null || OutsideLane(Captain(), tower));
                if (!moved)
                {
                    Show(step, "EDIT YOUR FORMATION", "The Captain is still in the firing lane, or the draft was canceled.\nOpen the squad again, move to a safe row / column, then <b>APPLY & RESUME</b>.", false);
                    GlowButton(hud.SquadButton(0));
                }
            }
            if (!Running) yield break;
            FocusCaptainAndTower(tower);
            GlowCaptain();
            Show(step + 1, "POSITIONING AVOIDS DAMAGE", "\nYour Captain moved to a different slot.\n The new position is outside this tower's firing line.", true, true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
        }
        public static bool OutsideLane(UnitState unit, TowerState tower)
        {
            if (unit == null || tower == null) return false;
            var offset = unit.Position - tower.Position;
            if (tower.Definition.Area == AttackArea.Square)
                return !SquadCombatRules.Contains(offset, tower.Definition.Area, tower.Definition.Range, tower.Definition.AttackWidth);
            var direction = SquadCombatRules.Direction(tower.Definition.Area);
            return Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(offset, new Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x))) > tower.Definition.AttackWidth / 2;
        }
        private static bool Approaching(UnitState unit, TowerState tower)
        {
            var offset = unit.Position - tower.Position;
            var direction = SquadCombatRules.Direction(tower.Definition.Area);
            var forward = Vector3.Dot(offset, direction);
            var side = Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(offset, new Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x)));
            return forward >= 0 && forward <= tower.Definition.Range + 3 && side <= 3;
        }
        private IEnumerator WaitForNext()
        {
            while (Running && !next) yield return null;
            next = false;
        }
        public void Continue() => next = true;
        public void Skip() => StopTutorial();
        [ContextMenu("Replay Level 1 Tutorial")]
        public void Replay()
        {
            if (!Application.isPlaying || session == null) return;
            StopTutorial();
            simulation = null;
            session.StartBattle();
        }
        private void AcquirePause()
        {
            if (ownsPause || simulation == null) return;
            wasPaused = simulation.IsPaused; simulation.SetPaused(true); ownsPause = true;
            hud.TutorialOwnsPause = true; inspection.IsInputSuppressed = true;
        }
        private void ReleasePause()
        {
            if (ownsPause && session != null && session.Simulation == simulation && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running)
                simulation.SetPaused(wasPaused);
            ownsPause = false;
            if (hud != null) hud.TutorialOwnsPause = false;
            if (inspection != null) inspection.IsInputSuppressed = false;
        }
        private void Finish()
        {
            Cleanup(); routine = null;
        }
        private void StopTutorial()
        {
            if (routine != null) StopCoroutine(routine);
            routine = null; Cleanup();
        }
        private void Cleanup()
        {
            if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged;
            if (inspection != null && ownsPause) inspection.CloseImmediately();
            RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause(); Hide();
            firstHit = false; next = false; CurrentStep = 0; hasCameraSnapshot = false;
        }
        private void FocusCaptainAndTower(TowerState tower)
        {
            var captain = Captain();
            if (captain == null) return;
            if (tower == null) { Focus(captain.Position, 2); return; }
            inspection.CloseImmediately();
            var center = (captain.Position + tower.Position) / 2;
            var bounds = new Bounds(captain.Position, Vector3.one);
            if (presentation != null && presentation.TryGetView(captain.Id, out var view) && view.TryGetVisibleBounds(out var visible)) bounds = visible;
            foreach (var sceneTower in session.Towers)
                if (sceneTower.RuntimeState == tower)
                    foreach (var renderer in sceneTower.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>()) bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
            var extent = Vector3.zero;
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
            for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
            for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
            {
                var corner = Quaternion.Inverse(originalRotation) * (bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z)) - center);
                extent = Vector3.Max(extent, new Vector3(Mathf.Abs(corner.x), Mathf.Abs(corner.y), Mathf.Abs(corner.z)));
            }
            // Frame both complete models around their midpoint, above the tutorial banner.
            targetOrthoSize = Mathf.Max(2, extent.y / 0.6f, extent.x / (camera.aspect * 0.85f));
            var tan = Mathf.Tan(camera.fieldOfView * Mathf.Deg2Rad / 2);
            var distance = Mathf.Max(6, targetOrthoSize / tan + extent.z, camera.nearClipPlane + extent.z + 1);
            var offset = camera.orthographic ? targetOrthoSize * 0.3f : distance * tan * 0.3f;
            targetPosition = center - originalRotation * Vector3.forward * distance - originalRotation * Vector3.up * offset;
            cameraOwned = true;
        }
        private void Focus(Vector3 point, float radius)
        {
            inspection.CloseImmediately();
            cameraOwned = true;
            var tan = Mathf.Tan(camera.fieldOfView * Mathf.Deg2Rad / 2);
            var distance = Mathf.Max(6, radius * 1.2f / tan);
            targetOrthoSize = radius * 1.2f;
            var verticalOffset = camera.orthographic ? targetOrthoSize * 0.3f : distance * tan * 0.3f;
            targetPosition = point - originalRotation * Vector3.forward * distance - originalRotation * Vector3.up * verticalOffset;
        }
        private void RestoreCamera()
        {
            if (cameraOwned && hasCameraSnapshot && camera != null)
            { camera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(originalPosition, originalRotation); camera.orthographicSize = originalOrthoSize; }
            cameraOwned = false;
        }
        private void GlowCaptain()
        {
            var captain = Captain();
            if (captain != null && presentation != null && presentation.TryGetView(captain.Id, out var view))
                GlowWorld(view.transform, true);
            else ClearWorldGlow();
        }
        private static Color GoldPulse => Color.Lerp(new Color(1, 0.64f, 0.08f), new Color(1, 0.94f, 0.48f),
            (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2);
        private Color WorldPulse => blueWorldGlow ? Color.Lerp(new Color(0.12f, 0.35f, 0.95f), new Color(0.45f, 0.82f, 1f),
            (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2) : GoldPulse;
        private void GlowWorld(Transform target, bool keepCrown = false, bool blue = false)
        {
            ClearWorldGlow();
            if (target == null) return;
            blueWorldGlow = blue;
            var renderers = new List<Renderer>();
            foreach (var renderer in target.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
                if (!keepCrown || renderer.name == "Body" || renderer.name == "Outline") renderers.Add(renderer);
            glowingRenderers = renderers.ToArray();
            originalGlowProperties = new MaterialPropertyBlock[glowingRenderers.Length];
            for (var i = 0; i < glowingRenderers.Length; i++)
            {
                originalGlowProperties[i] = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
                glowingRenderers[i].GetPropertyBlock(originalGlowProperties[i]);
            }
            PulseWorld(WorldPulse);
        }
        private void PulseWorld(Color gold)
        {
            if (glowingRenderers == null) return;
            if (glowProperties == null) glowProperties = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            foreach (var renderer in glowingRenderers)
            {
                if (renderer == null) continue;
                renderer.GetPropertyBlock(glowProperties);
                glowProperties.SetColor("_BaseColor", gold); glowProperties.SetColor("_Color", gold);
                renderer.SetPropertyBlock(glowProperties);
            }
        }
        private void ClearWorldGlow()
        {
            if (glowingRenderers != null)
                for (var i = 0; i < glowingRenderers.Length; i++)
                    if (glowingRenderers[i] != null) glowingRenderers[i].SetPropertyBlock(originalGlowProperties[i]);
            glowingRenderers = null; originalGlowProperties = null;
        }
        private void GlowButton(Button button)
        {
            ClearButtonGlow();
            glowingButton = button != null ? button.targetGraphic as Image : null;
            if (glowingButton == null) return;
            originalButtonColor = glowingButton.color; glowingButton.color = GoldPulse;
        }
        private void ClearButtonGlow()
        {
            if (glowingButton != null) glowingButton.color = originalButtonColor;
            glowingButton = null;
            if (squadArrow != null) squadArrow.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
        private void Show(int step, string heading, string message, bool modal, bool formationResult = false)
        {
            checkpointStep = step; checkpointHeading = heading; checkpointMessage = message; checkpointResult = formationResult;
            session.CaptureTutorialCheckpoint(wasPaused);
            CurrentStep = step; next = false; ui.SetActive(true); banner.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            title.text = $"{step}/{TotalSteps} - {heading}"; body.text = message;
            LayoutBanner(!modal);
            blocker.SetActive(modal); nextButton.gameObject.SetActive(modal);
            ClearButtonGlow();
        }
        private void Hide()
        {
            ClearWorldGlow(); ClearButtonGlow();
            if (ui != null) ui.SetActive(false);
        }
        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            if (cameraOwned && camera != null)
            {
                var alpha = 1 - Mathf.Exp(-12 * Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
                camera.transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(camera.transform.position, targetPosition, alpha);
                if (camera.orthographic) camera.orthographicSize = Mathf.Lerp(camera.orthographicSize, targetOrthoSize, alpha);
            }
            if (ui == null || !ui.activeSelf) return;
            var gold = GoldPulse;
            PulseWorld(WorldPulse);
            if (glowingButton != null) glowingButton.color = gold;
            var showArrow = CurrentStep == 4 && glowingButton != null;
            squadArrow.gameObject.SetActive(showArrow);
            if (showArrow)
            {
                glowingButton.rectTransform.GetWorldCorners(buttonCorners);
                var sourceCanvas = glowingButton.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
                var eventCamera = sourceCanvas != null && sourceCanvas.renderMode != RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay ? sourceCanvas.worldCamera : null;
                var screenPoint = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera, (buttonCorners[2] + buttonCorners[3]) / 2);
                if (!RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(root, screenPoint, null, out var right)) return;
                var scale = Mathf.Lerp(0.8f, 1.15f, (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2);
                squadArrow.localScale = Vector3.one * scale;
                squadArrow.SetAsLastSibling();
                // Keep the left-pointing tip next to the icon while the body grows and shrinks.
                squadArrow.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(right.x + 12 + 42 * scale, right.y);
            }
        }
        private void BuildUi()
        {
            if (ui != null) return;
            ui = new GameObject("Level 1 Tutorial", typeof(RectTransform)); ui.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(ui, 900, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f); root = (RectTransform)ui.transform;
            var shade = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Modal Input Blocker", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0, 0, 0, 0.08f));
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(shade.rectTransform); shade.raycastTarget = true; blocker = shade.gameObject;
            // Match the original HowToPlay.BuildFreezeFrameUI card and TMP typography.
            // Both projects use LiberationSans SDF through TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset.
            var panel = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Tutorial Banner", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(880, 175), new Color(0.08f, 0.11f, 0.17f, 0.96f));
            panel.raycastTarget = true; banner = panel.rectTransform;
            var outline = panel.gameObject.AddComponent<Outline>();
            outline.effectColor = new Color(0.3f, 0.85f, 0.4f, 1);
            outline.effectDistance = new Vector2(3, -3);
            title = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(banner, "Title", "", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, 30, new Color(0.35f, 0.95f, 0.45f), FontStyles.Bold);
            title.enableAutoSizing = true; title.fontSizeMin = 26; title.fontSizeMax = 30;
            body = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(banner, "Body", "", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, 23, Color.white);
            body.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal; body.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            // Keep every existing message, including its line breaks, visible in the legacy card.
            body.enableAutoSizing = true; body.fontSizeMin = 18; body.fontSizeMax = 23;
            var skip = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(banner, "Skip Tutorial", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(110, 30), Color.white, 0);
            var skipRect = (RectTransform)skip.transform;
            skipRect.anchorMin = skipRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 0); skipRect.pivot = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);
            skipRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(24, 22);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(skip.transform, "Label", "SKIP", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(100, 28), 18, new Color(0.196f, 0.196f, 0.196f));
            skip.onClick.AddListener(Skip);
            nextButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(banner, "Continue", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(144, 30), new Color(0.2f, 0.6f, 0.2f), 0);
            var nextRect = (RectTransform)nextButton.transform;
            nextRect.anchorMin = nextRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1, 0); nextRect.pivot = new Vector2(1, 0.5f);
            nextRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-24, 22);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(nextButton.transform, "Label", "CONTINUE", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(138, 28), 18, Color.white);
            nextButton.onClick.AddListener(Continue);
            LayoutBanner(false);
            squadArrow = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(root, "Team1 Tutorial Arrow", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(84, 62));
            // Native Image strokes use the same rendering path as the existing working HUD.
            ArrowStroke("Shaft Border", new Vector2(-34, 0), new Vector2(42, 0), 12, Color.black);
            ArrowStroke("Upper Border", new Vector2(-38, 0), new Vector2(-10, 22), 12, Color.black);
            ArrowStroke("Lower Border", new Vector2(-38, 0), new Vector2(-10, -22), 12, Color.black);
            ArrowStroke("Shaft", new Vector2(-34, 0), new Vector2(38, 0), 6, Color.white);
            ArrowStroke("Upper", new Vector2(-37, 0), new Vector2(-10, 22), 6, Color.white);
            ArrowStroke("Lower", new Vector2(-37, 0), new Vector2(-10, -22), 6, Color.white);
            squadArrow.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
        private void LayoutBanner(bool interactive)
        {
            // The full-width legacy card would cover the embedded formation editor's Apply button.
            // Use the same visual style on a narrower right-hand card during interactive lessons.
            banner.anchorMin = banner.anchorMax = new Vector2(interactive ? 0.75f : 0.5f, 0);
            banner.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0);
            banner.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0, 24);
            banner.sizeDelta = interactive ? new Vector2(560, 230) : new Vector2(880, 175);
            LayoutBannerText(title.rectTransform, interactive ? 0.72f : 0.68f, 0.96f, 40);
            LayoutBannerText(body.rectTransform, interactive ? 0.20f : 0.24f, interactive ? 0.72f : 0.68f, 50);
        }
        private static void LayoutBannerText(RectTransform rect, float bottom, float top, float horizontalInset)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0, bottom); rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1, top);
            rect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f); rect.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
            rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(-horizontalInset, 0);
        }
        private void ArrowStroke(string name, Vector2 from, Vector2 to, float thickness, Color tint)
        {
            var delta = to - from;
            var image = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(squadArrow, name, (from + to) / 2, new Vector2(delta.magnitude, thickness), tint);
            image.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
        }
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (session != null) { session.SimulationCreated -= Bind; session.TutorialRewindRequested -= PrepareRewind; }
            StopTutorial(); simulation = null;
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI

    public readonly struct SoldierLook
    {

        public const float PlateSize = 0.16f;

        public const float CrownStroke = 0.014f;
        // Shared line segments keep the warehouse, formation and world-space crown identical.
        public static readonly Vector2[] CrownSegments =
        {
            new Vector2(-0.10f, 0.065f), new Vector2(-0.065f, -0.055f),
            new Vector2(-0.065f, -0.055f), new Vector2(0.065f, -0.055f),
            new Vector2(0.065f, -0.055f), new Vector2(0.10f, 0.065f),
            new Vector2(0.10f, 0.065f), new Vector2(0.04f, 0.025f),
            new Vector2(0.04f, 0.025f), new Vector2(0, 0.09f),
            new Vector2(0, 0.09f), new Vector2(-0.04f, 0.025f),
            new Vector2(-0.04f, 0.025f), new Vector2(-0.10f, 0.065f),
            new Vector2(-0.07f, -0.09f), new Vector2(0.07f, -0.09f)
        };

        public SoldierLook(Color body, Color outline, float outerSize, float innerSize, SoldierMark mark, Color markColor)
        {
            Body = body;
            Outline = outline;
            OuterSize = outerSize;
            InnerSize = innerSize;
            Mark = mark;
            MarkColor = markColor;
        }

        public Color Body { get; }

        public Color Outline { get; }

        public float OuterSize { get; }

        public float InnerSize { get; }

        public SoldierMark Mark { get; }

        public Color MarkColor { get; }
    }
}
