using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    // A derived query result, never a second editable map or a renderer/collider query.
    public sealed class SmallCellOccupancy
    {
        public ReadOnlyCollection<int> TowerIds { get; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<int> UnitIds { get; }
        public bool IsOccupied => TowerIds.Count != 0 || UnitIds.Count != 0;
        internal SmallCellOccupancy(List<int> towers, List<int> units)
        { TowerIds = towers.AsReadOnly(); UnitIds = units.AsReadOnly(); }
        public static Vector3Int PositionToCell(Vector3 feet) => new Vector3Int(
            Mathf.FloorToInt(feet.x + 0.5f), Mathf.FloorToInt((feet.y - 0.25f) / 0.5f + 0.5f), Mathf.FloorToInt(feet.z + 0.5f));
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Simulation\SquadCombatRules.cs ===
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public enum UnitRole { Test, Attack, Tank, Heal, Captain }
    public enum BattleObjective { DefendGoal, ReachGoal }
    // Value 0 preserves existing Scene configurations while replacing the old circle.
    public enum AttackArea { Square = 0, PositiveX = 1, NegativeX = 2, PositiveZ = 3, NegativeZ = 4 }
    public enum ProjectileTargetKind { Tower, FormationSlot }

    // Verified against old Team5 SquadRules/OffenseSoldier. No old project dependency.
    public static class SquadCombatRules
    {
        public const float Speed = 0.5f;
        public const float AttackInterval = 1;
        public const float AttackRange = 2.5f; // Actual scene override, not the old field default (2).
        public const float ProjectileSpeed = 8;
        public const float HealInterval = 2;
        public const float HealAmount = 0.4f;
        public static UnitDefinition Create(UnitRole role)
        {
            switch (role)
            {
                case UnitRole.Attack: return new UnitDefinition(4, Speed, role, 1, AttackRange, AttackInterval);
                case UnitRole.Tank: return new UnitDefinition(8, Speed, role, defense: 0.2f);
                case UnitRole.Heal: return new UnitDefinition(3, Speed, role);
                case UnitRole.Captain: return new UnitDefinition(6, Speed, role, 0.5f, AttackRange, AttackInterval, defense: 0.5f);
                default: throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(role));
            }
        }
        public static Vector2Int Slot(int id) => new Vector2Int(id % 3, id / 3);
        public static Vector3 Offset(int id) => new Vector3(id % 3 - 1, 0, id / 3 - 1);
        public static bool AreNeighbors(int first, int second) => first != second &&
            Math.Abs(first % 3 - second % 3) <= 1 && Math.Abs(first / 3 - second / 3) <= 1;
        public static Vector3 Direction(AttackArea area)
        {
            switch (area)
            {
                case AttackArea.PositiveX: return Vector3.right;
                case AttackArea.NegativeX: return Vector3.left;
                case AttackArea.PositiveZ: return Vector3.forward;
                case AttackArea.NegativeZ: return Vector3.back;
                default: return Vector3.zero;
            }
        }
        public static bool Contains(Vector3 offset, AttackArea area, float range, float width)
        {
            if (area == AttackArea.Square) return Mathf.Abs(offset.x) <= range && Mathf.Abs(offset.z) <= range;
            var direction = Direction(area);
            var forward = Vector3.Dot(offset, direction);
            var side = Vector3.Dot(offset, new Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x));
            return forward >= 0 && forward <= range && Mathf.Abs(side) <= width / 2;
        }

        // The same XZ geometry is used by targeting, Game View indicators and Gizmos.
        public static Bounds AreaBounds(Vector3 position, AttackArea area, float range, float width)
        {
            if (area == AttackArea.Square) return new Bounds(position, new Vector3(range * 2, 0, range * 2));
            var direction = Direction(area);
            var alongX = direction.x != 0;
            return new Bounds(position + direction * (range / 2),
                new Vector3(alongX ? range : width, 0, alongX ? width : range));
        }
    }

    // Stable identity and scheduled slot are separate even before the member exists.
    public sealed class MemberSpawnDefinition
    {
        public int MemberId { get; }
        public int SlotId { get; }
        public float SpawnTime { get; }
        public UnitDefinition Unit { get; }
        public MemberSpawnDefinition(int memberId, int slotId, UnitDefinition unit, float spawnTime = 0)
        {
            if (memberId <= 0 || slotId < 0 || slotId > 8 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(spawnTime) || spawnTime < 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(memberId));
            MemberId = memberId; SlotId = slotId; Unit = unit ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(unit)); SpawnTime = spawnTime;
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleCell : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const float LayerHeight = 0.5f;
        public static Vector3 Size => new Vector3(1, LayerHeight, 1);
        [SerializeField] private CellKind kind;
        public CellKind Kind => kind;
        public Vector3Int Occupancy => new Vector3Int(Mathf.RoundToInt(transform.position.x),
            Mathf.RoundToInt(transform.position.y / LayerHeight), Mathf.RoundToInt(transform.position.z));
        public Vector3 TopCenter => transform.position + Vector3.up * (LayerHeight / 2);
        public bool IsRouteSurface => kind == CellKind.Road || kind == CellKind.Spawn || kind == CellKind.Goal;

        public void CollectErrors(List<string> errors)
        {
            var position = transform.position;
            var key = Occupancy;
            if (!float.IsFinite(position.x) || !float.IsFinite(position.y) || !float.IsFinite(position.z) ||
                Vector3.Distance(position, new Vector3(key.x, key.y * LayerHeight, key.z)) > 0.001f)
                errors.Add($"{name}: cell center must align to X/Z = 1 and Y = 0.5.");
            if (Quaternion.Angle(transform.rotation, Quaternion.identity) > 0.01f ||
                Vector3.Distance(transform.lossyScale, Vector3.one) > 0.001f)
                errors.Add($"{name}: cells require identity world rotation and scale; physical size is 1 x 0.5 x 1.");
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\World\BattleGrid.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleGrid : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const int MacroSize = 3;
        // Only a temporary lookup. The Scene's child BattleCells remain the authority.
        private readonly Dictionary<Vector3Int, BattleCell> index = new Dictionary<Vector3Int, BattleCell>();
        public BattleCell[] SceneCells => GetComponentsInChildren<BattleCell>(true);

        public void RebuildIndex(List<string> errors)
        {
            index.Clear();
            var cells = SceneCells;
            if (cells.Length == 0) errors.Add("BattleGrid contains no cells.");
            foreach (var cell in cells)
            {
                cell.CollectErrors(errors);
                if (!cell.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) errors.Add($"{cell.name}: configured cells must be active.");
                if (index.TryGetValue(cell.Occupancy, out var other))
                    errors.Add($"Grid conflict at {cell.Occupancy}: {other.name} and {cell.name}.");
                else index.Add(cell.Occupancy, cell);
            }
        }

        // RebuildIndex must be called before querying after an edit.
        public bool TryGetCell(Vector3Int occupancy, out BattleCell cell) => index.TryGetValue(occupancy, out cell);
        public bool Contains(BattleCell cell) => cell != null && index.TryGetValue(cell.Occupancy, out var value) && value == cell;
        public bool HasClearTop(BattleCell cell) => !index.ContainsKey(cell.Occupancy + Vector3Int.up);

        public static Vector3Int ToMacroCell(Vector3Int smallCell) => new Vector3Int(
            Mathf.FloorToInt(smallCell.x / 3f), smallCell.y, Mathf.FloorToInt(smallCell.z / 3f));

        // Macro coordinates are (macro X, unchanged height layer, macro Z). No serialized Macro positions.
        public BattleCell[] GetMacroCells(Vector3Int macro)
        {
            var cells = new BattleCell[9];
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 3; x++)
                index.TryGetValue(new Vector3Int(macro.x * 3 + x, macro.y, macro.z * 3 + z), out cells[z * 3 + x]);
            return cells;
        }

        public SmallCellOccupancy[] GetMacroOccupancy(Vector3Int macro, BattleSimulation simulation)
        {
            var result = new SmallCellOccupancy[9];
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 3; x++)
                result[z * 3 + x] = simulation.GetCellOccupancy(new Vector3Int(macro.x * 3 + x, macro.y, macro.z * 3 + z));
            return result;
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\World\BattleTower.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleTower : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private float range = 2.4f;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float damage = 18;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float attackInterval = 0.8f;
        [SerializeField] private TargetRule targetRule = TargetRule.FurthestAlongRoute;
        [SerializeField] private bool usesMacroFootprint;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float maxHp = 20;
        [SerializeField] private AttackArea attackArea = AttackArea.Square;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float attackWidth = 0.3f;
        public int FootprintSize => usesMacroFootprint ? 3 : 1;
        public float Range => range;
        public float Damage => damage;
        public float AttackInterval => attackInterval;
        public TargetRule TargetRule => targetRule;
        public TowerState RuntimeState { get; private set; }
        public float CurrentCooldown => RuntimeState?.CooldownRemaining ?? 0;
        public TowerDefinition CaptureDefinition(int id) => new TowerDefinition(id, transform.position, range, damage, attackInterval, targetRule, FootprintSize, maxHp, attackArea, attackWidth);
        public void BindRuntimeState(TowerState state) => RuntimeState = state;

        public bool HasValidConfiguration => float.IsFinite(range) && range >= 0 &&
            float.IsFinite(damage) && damage > 0 && float.IsFinite(attackInterval) && attackInterval > 0 &&
            float.IsFinite(transform.position.x) && float.IsFinite(transform.position.y) && float.IsFinite(transform.position.z) &&
            Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TargetRule), targetRule) && float.IsFinite(maxHp) && maxHp > 0 &&
            float.IsFinite(attackWidth) && attackWidth > 0 && Enum.IsDefined(typeof(AttackArea), attackArea);

        public void CollectFootprintErrors(BattleGrid grid, HashSet<Vector3Int> occupied, List<string> errors)
        {
            var position = transform.position;
            var center = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(position);
            if (usesMacroFootprint)
            {
                var macro = BattleGrid.ToMacroCell(center);
                var expected = new Vector3(macro.x * 3 + 1, center.y * 0.5f + 0.25f, macro.z * 3 + 1);
                if (Vector3.Distance(position, expected) > 0.001f)
                    errors.Add($"{name}: 3x3 tower must be centered on a Macro Cell at its top surface.");
            }
            var radius = FootprintSize / 2;
            for (var z = -radius; z <= radius; z++)
            for (var x = -radius; x <= radius; x++)
            {
                var key = center + new Vector3Int(x, 0, z);
                if (!occupied.Add(key)) errors.Add($"{name}: tower footprint overlap at {key}.");
                if (!grid.TryGetCell(key, out var support) || support.Kind != CellKind.TowerPlatform ||
                    !grid.HasClearTop(support) || Mathf.Abs(position.y - support.TopCenter.y) > 0.001f)
                    errors.Add($"{name}: footprint {key} needs a clear, equal-height TowerPlatform surface.");
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\World\SceneRoute.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class SceneRoute : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private List<BattleCell> cells = new List<BattleCell>();
        [SerializeField] private int requiredWidth = 1;
        public IReadOnlyList<BattleCell> Cells => cells.AsReadOnly();
        public int RequiredWidth => requiredWidth;

        public void CollectErrors(BattleGrid grid, List<string> errors)
        {
            if (cells.Count < 2) { errors.Add("SceneRoute needs a Spawn and a Goal with an ordered road."); return; }
            var visited = new HashSet<BattleCell>();
            if (requiredWidth != 1 && requiredWidth != 3) errors.Add("Route width must be 1 or 3.");
            var previousPhase = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < cells.Count; i++)
            {
                var cell = cells[i];
                if (cell == null) { errors.Add($"Route entry {i} is missing."); continue; }
                if (!visited.Add(cell)) errors.Add($"Route repeats {cell.name}.");
                if (!grid.Contains(cell)) errors.Add($"{cell.name}: route cell is outside its BattleGrid.");
                if (!cell.IsRouteSurface || !grid.HasClearTop(cell)) errors.Add($"{cell.name}: route surface is not passable.");
                var expected = i == 0 ? CellKind.Spawn : i == cells.Count - 1 ? CellKind.Goal : CellKind.Road;
                if (requiredWidth == 1 || i == 0 || i == cells.Count - 1)
                {
                    if (cell.Kind != expected) errors.Add($"Route entry {i} must be {expected}.");
                }
                else
                {
                    var phase = cell.Kind == CellKind.Spawn ? 0 : cell.Kind == CellKind.Road ? 1 : 2;
                    if (phase < previousPhase) errors.Add($"Route entry {i}: Spawn/Road/Goal order is invalid.");
                    previousPhase = phase;
                }
                if (requiredWidth == 3)
                    for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z++)
                    for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x++)
                    {
                        var key = cell.Occupancy + new Vector3Int(x, 0, z);
                        if (!grid.TryGetCell(key, out var neighbor) || !neighbor.IsRouteSurface || !grid.HasClearTop(neighbor))
                            errors.Add($"Formation clearance blocked at {key} around route entry {i}.");
                    }
                if (i == 0 || cells[i - 1] == null) continue;
                var offset = cell.Occupancy - cells[i - 1].Occupancy;
                if (offset.y != 0 || Mathf.Abs(offset.x) + Mathf.Abs(offset.z) != 1)
                    errors.Add($"Route disconnected or unequal height between entries {i - 1} and {i}.");
                else if (requiredWidth == 3)
                    for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                        foreach (var swept in FormationClearance.SweptCells(cells[i - 1].TopCenter + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot),
                            cell.TopCenter + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot)))
                            if (!grid.TryGetCell(swept, out var support) || !support.IsRouteSurface || !grid.HasClearTop(support))
                                errors.Add($"Formation clearance blocked along slot {slot} trajectory, segment {i - 1}, cell {swept}.");
            }
        }

        public Vector3[] CaptureTopCenters()
        {
            var points = new Vector3[cells.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < points.Length; i++) points[i] = cells[i].TopCenter;
            return points;
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\BattleSimulationTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class BattleSimulationTests
    {
        private static readonly Vector3[] LongRoute = { Vector3.zero, new Vector3(20, 0, 0) };
        private static BattleSimulation Create(IEnumerable<TowerDefinition> towers = null, int count = 1,
            float hp = 100, float speed = 1, float interval = 1, int lives = 10, IEnumerable<Vector3> route = null)
            => new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(route ?? LongRoute, towers ?? Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(),
                new UnitDefinition(hp, speed), count, interval, lives));
        private static TowerDefinition Tower(int id = 1, float damage = 10, float interval = 0.5f,
            TargetRule rule = TargetRule.FurthestAlongRoute, Vector3? position = null, float range = 30)
            => new TowerDefinition(id, position ?? Vector3.zero, range, damage, interval, rule);
        private static void Finish(BattleSimulation simulation, int ticks = 20000)
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < ticks && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) simulation.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running), "Battle must terminate.");
        }

        [Test]
        public void MovementIsContinuousAndConsumesDistanceAcrossCorners()
        {
            var sim = Create(speed: 2, route: new[] { Vector3.zero, Vector3.right, new Vector3(1, 0, 1), new Vector3(2, 0, 1) });
            sim.Step(0.25f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(new Vector3(0.5f, 0, 0)));
            sim.Step(0.5f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(new Vector3(1, 0, 0.5f)));
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].RouteProgress, Is.EqualTo(1.5f).Within(0.001));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DamageCooldownDeathAndVictoryEmitExactlyOnceInOrder()
        {
            var sim = Create(new[] { Tower(damage: 30) }, hp: 50, speed: 0.01f);
            var events = new List<string>();
            var damage = new List<DamageEvent>();
            sim.UnitSpawned += _ => events.Add("spawn");
            sim.TowerAttacked += _ => events.Add("attack");
            sim.UnitDamaged += value => { events.Add("damage"); damage.Add(value); };
            sim.UnitDied += _ => events.Add("death");
            sim.BattleEnded += _ => events.Add("end");
            sim.Step(0.1f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(20));
            sim.Step(0.2f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(20));
            sim.Step(0.3f);
            sim.Step(1);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].LifeState, Is.EqualTo(UnitLifeState.Dead));
            Assert.That(sim.KilledCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            Assert.That(damage.Select(value => value.Amount), Is.EqualTo(new[] { 30f, 20f }));
            Assert.That(damage[0].RemainingHp, Is.EqualTo(20));
            Assert.That(events, Is.EqualTo(new[] { "spawn", "attack", "damage", "attack", "damage", "death", "end" }));
        }

        [Test]
        public void MultipleTowersDoNotAttackAlreadyDeadUnits()
        {
            var sim = Create(new[] { Tower(1, 100), Tower(2, 100) }, hp: 10);
            var attacks = 0;
            sim.TowerAttacked += _ => attacks++;
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(attacks, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sim.KilledCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
        }

        [TestCase(TargetRule.FurthestAlongRoute, 1)]
        [TestCase(TargetRule.Nearest, 2)]
        public void TargetSelectionUsesRequestedRule(TargetRule rule, int expectedId)
        {
            var sim = Create(new[] { Tower(rule: rule, position: new Vector3(100, 0, 0)) }, count: 2);
            sim.Step(1);
            sim.SetTowerPosition(1, Vector3.zero);
            sim.Step(0.1f);
            Assert.That(sim.Towers[0].TargetId, Is.EqualTo(expectedId));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SquareRangeUsesWorldXZCoordinatesAndLiveTowerPosition()
        {
            var sim = Create(new[] { Tower(range: 1, position: new Vector3(2, 0, 0)) });
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(100));
            sim.SetTowerPosition(1, sim.Units[0].Position);
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(90));
        }

        [Test]
        public void GoalEventAndDefeatOccurOnceAndStopFutureSpawns()
        {
            var sim = Create(count: 100, speed: 100, lives: 1);
            var goals = 0;
            var ends = 0;
            sim.UnitReachedGoal += _ => goals++;
            sim.BattleEnded += _ => ends++;
            sim.Step(1);
            sim.Step(1);
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Defeat));
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].LifeState, Is.EqualTo(UnitLifeState.ReachedGoal));
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(100));
            Assert.That(goals, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(ends, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sim.SpawnedCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sim.LivesRemaining, Is.Zero);
        }

        [Test]
        public void LeaksBelowLifeLimitStillAllowVictoryAfterWaveCompletes()
        {
            var sim = Create(count: 3, speed: 20, lives: 4);
            Finish(sim);
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            Assert.That(sim.ReachedGoalCount, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(sim.AliveCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(sim.LivesRemaining, Is.EqualTo(1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void FiveHundredTwelveConcurrentUnitsCompleteWithoutVisualObjects()
        {
            var sim = Create(count: 512, speed: 1, interval: 0.02f, lives: 513);
            var maximumAlive = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < 3000 && sim.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++)
            {
                sim.Step(0.02f);
                maximumAlive = Math.Max(maximumAlive, sim.AliveCount);
            }
            Assert.That(maximumAlive, Is.EqualTo(512));
            Assert.That(sim.ReachedGoalCount, Is.EqualTo(512));
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DefinitionCopiesInputsAndRuntimeDoesNotMutateConfiguration()
        {
            var points = new List<Vector3>(LongRoute);
            var tower = Tower();
            var definition = new BattleDefinition(points, new[] { tower }, new UnitDefinition(100, 1), 1, 1, 1);
            points[0] = Vector3.one * 99;
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            sim.SetTowerPosition(1, Vector3.one);
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(definition.Waypoints[0], Is.EqualTo(Vector3.zero));
            Assert.That(tower.Position, Is.EqualTo(Vector3.zero));
            Assert.That(definition.Unit.MaxHp, Is.EqualTo(100));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SameInputsAndFixedTicksProduceSameResults()
        {
            var a = Create(new[] { Tower() }, count: 20, interval: 0.25f, lives: 5);
            var b = Create(new[] { Tower() }, count: 20, interval: 0.25f, lives: 5);
            Finish(a);
            Finish(b);
            Assert.That(a.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(b.Outcome));
            Assert.That(a.KilledCount, Is.EqualTo(b.KilledCount));
            Assert.That(a.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(b.ElapsedTime));
            Assert.That(a.Units.Select(unit => (unit.Hp, unit.Position, unit.LifeState)),
                Is.EqualTo(b.Units.Select(unit => (unit.Hp, unit.Position, unit.LifeState))));
        }

        [Test]
        public void InvalidNumbersAndDuplicateTowerIdsAreRejected()
        {
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => new UnitDefinition(float.NaN, 1));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => Tower(interval: 0));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => Create(new[] { Tower(), Tower() }));
            var sim = Create();
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => sim.Step(float.PositiveInfinity));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => sim.Step(0));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SimulationAssemblyHasNoComponentTypesOrPresentationReferences()
        {
            var assembly = typeof(BattleSimulation).Assembly;
            Assert.That(assembly.GetTypes().Any(type => typeof(Component).IsAssignableFrom(type)), Is.False);
            Assert.That(assembly.GetReferencedAssemblies().Any(name =>
                name.Name.Contains("World") || name.Name.Contains("Session") || name.Name.Contains("Presentation") ||
                name.Name.Contains("Animation") || name.Name.Contains("Particle") || name.Name.Contains("UI")), Is.False);
            foreach (var type in assembly.GetTypes())
            foreach (var field in type.GetFields(System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Public))
                Assert.That(typeof(UnityEngine.Object).IsAssignableFrom(field.FieldType), Is.False, $"{type.Name}.{field.Name}");
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\GameMenuSceneTests.cs ===
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using TMPro;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class GameMenuSceneTests
    {
        [TearDown] public void Close() => EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);

        [TestCase(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene, "StartGame", "Reverse Tower Defense")]
        [TestCase(BattleSceneFlow.EndScene, "GoToStart", "Thanks for playing")]
        public void ImportedMenuKeepsLegacyTextAndHasValidPersistentButton(string path, string method, string heading)
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(path);
            var menu = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacySceneMenu>();
            Assert.That(menu, Is.Not.Null);
            var button = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>();
            Assert.That(button.onClick.GetPersistentEventCount(), Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(button.onClick.GetPersistentTarget(0), Is.SameAs(menu));
            Assert.That(button.onClick.GetPersistentMethodName(0), Is.EqualTo(method));
            var labels = Object.FindObjectsByType<TMP_Text>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
            Assert.That(labels.Any(label => label.text.Trim() == heading), Is.True);
            Assert.That(labels.All(label => label.font != null), Is.True);
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<CanvasScaler>().referenceResolution, Is.EqualTo(new Vector2(500, 500)));
            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
                Assert.That(root.GetComponentsInChildren<Component>(true).All(component => component != null), Is.True, root.name);
            Assert.That(scene.GetRootGameObjects().Any(root => root.name.Contains("Analytics")), Is.False);
        }

        [Test]
        public void BuildStartsAtTitleAndIncludesBattleAndEnd()
        {
            var scenes = EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Where(scene => scene.enabled).Select(scene => scene.path).ToArray();
            Assert.That(scenes[0], Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.LevelThreeScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.LevelFourScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene));
            Assert.That(scenes, Does.Contain(BattleSceneFlow.EndScene));
            Assert.That(scenes.Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(scenes.Length));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SettlementHasPortedLabelsAndValidContinueCallback()
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
            var controller = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyLevelSettlement>();
            Assert.That(controller, Is.Not.Null);
            var button = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>();
            Assert.That(button.onClick.GetPersistentTarget(0), Is.SameAs(controller));
            Assert.That(button.onClick.GetPersistentMethodName(0), Is.EqualTo("ContinueToUpgrade"));
            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
                Assert.That(root.GetComponentsInChildren<Component>(true).All(component => component != null), Is.True);
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<TMP_Text>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).All(label => label.font != null), Is.True);
        }
    }
}

using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class LegacyHudSceneTests
    {
        [TearDown] public void Close() => EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);
        [Test]
        public void HudUsesCompactControlsWithoutCurrencyAndKeepsPersistentCallbacks()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(SquadBattleMenu.ScenePath);
            var hud = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyBattleHud>();
            Assert.That(hud, Is.Not.Null); Assert.That(hud.Session, Is.Not.Null);
            var scaler = hud.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            Assert.That(scaler.referenceResolution, Is.EqualTo(new Vector2(500, 500)));
            Assert.That(scaler.matchWidthOrHeight, Is.Zero);
            // Positions are authored by the user in Scene; do not enforce the old imported coordinates.
            Assert.That(((RectTransform)hud.SpeedSlider.transform).sizeDelta, Is.EqualTo(new Vector2(80, 15)));
            Assert.That(((RectTransform)hud.EditButton.transform).sizeDelta, Is.EqualTo(new Vector2(80, 25)));
            Assert.That(hud.SpeedSlider.transform.localScale, Is.EqualTo(Vector3.one * 0.75f));
            Assert.That(hud.EditButton.transform.localScale, Is.EqualTo(Vector3.one * 0.75f));
            Assert.That(hud.transform.Find("Gold"), Is.Null);
            Assert.That(hud.EditButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMPro.TMP_Text>().font.name, Is.EqualTo("LiberationSans SDF"));
            Assert.That(hud.SpeedSlider.wholeNumbers, Is.True);
            Assert.That(hud.SpeedSlider.maxValue, Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(hud.SpeedSlider.onValueChanged.GetPersistentTarget(0), Is.SameAs(hud));
            Assert.That(hud.SpeedSlider.onValueChanged.GetPersistentMethodName(0), Is.EqualTo("SetSpeedFromSlider"));
            Assert.That(hud.EditButton.onClick.GetPersistentTarget(0), Is.SameAs(hud));
            Assert.That(hud.EditButton.onClick.GetPersistentMethodName(0), Is.EqualTo("OpenSquadEditor"));
        }
    }

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\MacroGridTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class MacroGridTests
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        private const string RoundTrip = "Assets/Rebuild/Tests/EditMode/MacroRoundTrip.tmp.unity";
        [SetUp]
        public void Load()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(MacroPrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath);
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
        }
        [TearDown]
        public void Close()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);
            if (File.Exists(RoundTrip)) AssetDatabase.DeleteAsset(RoundTrip);
        }

        [Test]
        public void MapIs36By24WithNineActualSmallCellsPerMacro()
        {
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            var ground = session.Grid.SceneCells.Where(cell => cell.Occupancy.y == 0).ToArray();
            Assert.That(ground.Length, Is.EqualTo(36 * 24));
            Assert.That(ground.Select(cell => cell.Occupancy.x).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(36));
            Assert.That(ground.Select(cell => cell.Occupancy.z).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(24));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Length, Is.EqualTo(900));
            for (var z = 0; z < 8; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 12; x++)
            {
                var group = session.Grid.GetMacroCells(new Vector3Int(x, 0, z));
                Assert.That(group.Length, Is.EqualTo(9));
                Assert.That(group.All(cell => cell != null), Is.True);
                Assert.That(group.Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(9));
                Assert.That(group.All(cell => BattleGrid.ToMacroCell(cell.Occupancy) == new Vector3Int(x, 0, z)), Is.True);
            }
            Assert.That(BattleGrid.ToMacroCell(new Vector3Int(-1, 2, -3)), Is.EqualTo(new Vector3Int(-1, 2, -1)));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.All(cell => cell.transform.lossyScale == Vector3.one &&
                cell.transform.Find("Cell Mesh").localScale == BattleCell.Size), Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.transform.parent.Find("Decorative Border Tiles").childCount, Is.EqualTo(1404));
        }

        [Test]
        public void EachTowerOwnsExactlyNineCellsAndConflictsAreRejected()
        {
            var definition = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
            {
                Assert.That(tower.FootprintSize, Is.EqualTo(3));
                var key = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(tower.transform.position);
                var occupied = session.Grid.GetMacroOccupancy(BattleGrid.ToMacroCell(key), sim);
                Assert.That(occupied.Count(cell => cell.TowerIds.Count == 1), Is.EqualTo(9));
            }
            session.Towers[1].transform.position = session.Towers[0].transform.position;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("overlap")), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void MovingOneTowerToSpareMacroChangesItsFootprintAndActualAttacks()
        {
            var before = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var tower = session.Towers[0];
            var oldCenter = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(tower.transform.position);
            tower.transform.position = new Vector3(4, 0.25f, 16);
            var after = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(after.Towers[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(tower.transform.position));
            var updated = new BattleSimulation(after);
            Assert.That(updated.GetCellOccupancy(oldCenter).TowerIds, Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.GetMacroOccupancy(new Vector3Int(1, 0, 5), updated).All(cell => cell.TowerIds.SequenceEqual(new[] { 1 })), Is.True);
            BattleSimulation Make(TowerDefinition definition) => new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(
                new[] { new Vector3(7, 0.25f, 7), new Vector3(8, 0.25f, 7) }, new[] { definition },
                new UnitDefinition(100, 0.01f), 1, 1, 1));
            var oldBattle = Make(before.Towers[0]);
            var newBattle = Make(after.Towers[0]);
            oldBattle.Step(0.02f);
            newBattle.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(oldBattle.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(82));
            Assert.That(newBattle.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(100));
        }

        [Test]
        public void ExactHalfCellMovementStillGivesNineDistinctOccupiedCells()
        {
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(new[] { new Vector3(1, 0.25f, 1), new Vector3(5, 0.25f, 1) },
                Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(), new UnitDefinition(100, 1), 9, 3, 10, 3));
            sim.Step(0.5f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Select(unit => unit.OccupiedCell).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(sim.Units.All(unit => sim.GetCellOccupancy(unit.OccupiedCell).UnitIds.Count == 1), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TowerRequiresAllNineLegalEqualHeightSupportsAndMacroCenter()
        {
            var tower = session.Towers[0];
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            var support = session.Grid.GetMacroCells(BattleGrid.ToMacroCell(SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(tower.transform.position)))[0];
            support.transform.position += Vector3.up * 0.5f;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("footprint")), Is.True);
            support.transform.position -= Vector3.up * 0.5f;
            var data = new SerializedObject(support);
            data.FindProperty("kind").enumValueIndex = (int)CellKind.Road;
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("footprint")), Is.True);
            tower.transform.position += Vector3.right * 0.2f;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("centered")), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void MissingOrBlockedCornerClearancePreventsBattle()
        {
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            var points = session.Route.Cells;
            var turn = Enumerable.Range(1, points.Count - 2).First(i =>
                points[i].Occupancy - points[i - 1].Occupancy != points[i + 1].Occupancy - points[i].Occupancy);
            var key = points[turn].Occupancy + new Vector3Int(1, 0, 1);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.TryGetCell(key, out var corner), Is.True);
            var data = new SerializedObject(corner);
            data.FindProperty("kind").enumValueIndex = (int)CellKind.Obstacle;
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("Formation clearance blocked")), Is.True);
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => session.CaptureDefinition());
            corner.transform.position += Vector3.up * 0.5f;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("Formation clearance blocked")), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void NineUnitsStayOnRoadThroughEveryCornerAndDoNotShareCells()
        {
            var captured = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(captured.Waypoints, Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(),
                captured.Unit, 9, 3, 10, 3));
            for (var i = 0; i < 2000 && sim.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++)
            {
                sim.Step(0.02f);
                var alive = sim.Units.Where(unit => unit.IsAlive).ToArray();
                Assert.That(alive.Select(unit => unit.OccupiedCell).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(alive.Length));
                foreach (var unit in alive)
                {
                    Assert.That(session.Grid.TryGetCell(unit.OccupiedCell, out var cell), Is.True);
                    Assert.That(cell.IsRouteSurface, Is.True);
                    Assert.That((unit.AnchorPosition - alive[0].AnchorPosition).magnitude, Is.LessThan(0.0002f));
                    Assert.That(sim.GetCellOccupancy(unit.OccupiedCell).UnitIds, Is.EqualTo(new[] { unit.Id }));
                }
            }
            Assert.That(sim.SpawnedCount, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(sim.ReachedGoalCount, Is.EqualTo(9));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DeathLeavesFormationSlotVacantAndDoesNotChangeOtherOffsets()
        {
            var captured = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var tower = new TowerDefinition(1, captured.Waypoints[0] + new Vector3(-1, 0, -1), 0.2f, 1000, 100, TargetRule.Nearest);
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(captured.Waypoints, new[] { tower }, captured.Unit, 9, 3, 10, 3));
            var died = new List<int>();
            sim.UnitDied += value => died.Add(value.UnitId);
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(died, Is.EqualTo(new[] { 1 }));
            var slots = sim.Units.Select(unit => unit.FormationSlot).ToArray();
            for (var i = 0; i < 300; i++) sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Select(unit => unit.FormationSlot), Is.EqualTo(slots));
            Assert.That(sim.AliveCount, Is.EqualTo(8));
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Where(unit => unit.IsAlive).All(unit => unit.Hp == unit.MaxHp), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SceneTransformEditsAndSaveReopenFeedNewBattleDefinitions()
        {
            var before = session.CaptureDefinition();
            session.Grid.transform.position += Vector3.right * 3;
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers) tower.transform.position += Vector3.right * 3;
            var updated = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(updated.Waypoints[0], Is.EqualTo(before.Waypoints[0] + Vector3.right * 3));
            Assert.That(updated.Towers[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(before.Towers[0].Position + Vector3.right * 3));
            Assert.That(EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(session.gameObject.scene, RoundTrip, true), Is.True);
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(RoundTrip);
            var reloaded = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(reloaded.Waypoints, Is.EqualTo(updated.Waypoints));
            Assert.That(reloaded.Towers.Select(tower => tower.Position), Is.EqualTo(updated.Towers.Select(tower => tower.Position)));
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(reloaded);
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[4].Position.x, Is.GreaterThan(updated.Waypoints[0].x));
        }

        [Test]
        public void PerspectiveCameraIncludesAllTerrainAndBordersAt16By9()
        {
            var camera = Camera.main;
            Assert.That(camera.orthographic, Is.False);
            camera.aspect = 16f / 9f;
            var bounds = Object.FindObjectsByType<MeshRenderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Select(renderer => renderer.bounds).ToArray();
            var overall = bounds[0];
            foreach (var box in bounds) overall.Encapsulate(box);
            for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
            for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
            {
                var viewport = camera.WorldToViewportPoint(overall.center + Vector3.Scale(overall.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z)));
                Assert.That(viewport.x, Is.InRange(0f, 1f));
                Assert.That(viewport.y, Is.InRange(0f, 1f));
                Assert.That(viewport.z, Is.InRange(camera.nearClipPlane, camera.farClipPlane));
            }
        }
    }
}

using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;
using UnityEngine.Rendering.Universal;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class MinimalPresentationSceneTests
    {
        [SetUp]
        public void Load() => EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath);
        [TearDown]
        public void Close() => EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);

        [Test]
        public void SavedSceneUsesOriginalEntitiesWithEditableCorrectlySizedMeshes()
        {
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            foreach (var cell in session.Grid.SceneCells)
            {
                var mesh = cell.transform.Find("Cell Mesh");
                Assert.That(mesh, Is.Not.Null);
                Assert.That(mesh.localPosition, Is.EqualTo(Vector3.zero));
                Assert.That(mesh.localScale, Is.EqualTo(BattleCell.Size));
                Assert.That(mesh.GetComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh, Is.Not.Null);
                Assert.That(mesh.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().enabled, Is.True);
                Assert.That(mesh.gameObject.scene, Is.EqualTo(cell.gameObject.scene));
                Assert.That(PrefabUtility.IsPartOfPrefabAsset(mesh), Is.False);
            }
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
            {
                var mesh = tower.transform.Find("Tower Mesh");
                Assert.That(mesh.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>(), Is.Not.Null);
                Assert.That(mesh.position.y, Is.GreaterThan(tower.transform.position.y));
            }
            var colors = session.Grid.SceneCells.GroupBy(cell => cell.Kind).Select(group =>
                group.First().GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial.GetColor("_BaseColor")).ToArray();
            Assert.That(colors.Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(6));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<Collider>(FindObjectsSortMode.None), Is.Empty);
            var border = session.Grid.transform.parent.Find("Decorative Border Tiles");
            Assert.That(border, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(border.IsChildOf(session.Grid.transform), Is.False);
            Assert.That(border.childCount, Is.EqualTo(156)); // 18x14 outer rectangle minus the 12x8 battle grid.
            Assert.That(border.GetComponentsInChildren<MonoBehaviour>(true), Is.Empty);
            var positions = border.Cast<Transform>().Select(tile => tile.position).ToArray();
            Assert.That(positions.Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(156));
            foreach (Transform tile in border)
            {
                Assert.That(tile.localScale, Is.EqualTo(BattleCell.Size));
                Assert.That(tile.gameObject.layer, Is.EqualTo(2));
                Assert.That(tile.position.y, Is.Zero);
                Assert.That(tile.position.x, Is.InRange(-3, 14));
                Assert.That(tile.position.z, Is.InRange(-3, 10));
                Assert.That(tile.position.x < 0 || tile.position.x > 11 || tile.position.z < 0 || tile.position.z > 7, Is.True);
                Assert.That(session.Grid.TryGetCell(new Vector3Int(Mathf.RoundToInt(tile.position.x), 0, Mathf.RoundToInt(tile.position.z)), out _), Is.False);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void SquarePrefabCameraAndMaterialsUseExistingUniversal3DRenderer()
        {
            var presentation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattlePresentation>();
            Assert.That(presentation.Session, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(presentation.UnitPrefab), Is.EqualTo(MinimalPresentationSetup.UnitPrefabPath));
            Assert.That(presentation.UnitPrefab.GetComponentInChildren<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh.name, Is.EqualTo("Cube"));
            var pipeline = GraphicsSettings.defaultRenderPipeline as UniversalRenderPipelineAsset;
            Assert.That(pipeline, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Camera>().GetUniversalAdditionalCameraData().scriptableRenderer, Is.TypeOf<UniversalRenderer>());
            foreach (var renderer in Object.FindObjectsByType<MeshRenderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                Assert.That(renderer.sharedMaterial.shader.name, Is.EqualTo("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit"));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<Light>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Any(light => light.type == LightType.Directional), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void ReopeningScenePreservesStaticMeshReferencesAndUnitPrefab()
        {
            var before = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var meshAssets = before.Grid.SceneCells.Select(cell => AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(cell.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial)).ToArray();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath);
            var after = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(after.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(after.Grid.SceneCells.Select(cell => AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(cell.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial)), Is.EqualTo(meshAssets));
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattlePresentation>().UnitPrefab, Is.Not.Null);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\SceneConfigurationTests.cs ===
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class SceneConfigurationTests
    {
        private const string RoundTripPath = "Assets/Rebuild/Tests/EditMode/SceneRoundTrip.tmp.unity";
        private BattleSession session;
        [SetUp]
        public void LoadPrototype()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(PrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath, OpenSceneMode.Single);
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
        }
        [TearDown]
        public void Cleanup()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            if (File.Exists(RoundTripPath)) AssetDatabase.DeleteAsset(RoundTripPath);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SavedPrototypeHasExpectedStaticLayoutAndValidRoute()
        {
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Count(cell => cell.Occupancy.y == 0), Is.EqualTo(96));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Count(cell => cell.Occupancy.y > 0), Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(session.Towers.Count, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells.Count, Is.EqualTo(18));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells[0].Kind, Is.EqualTo(CellKind.Spawn));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells[17].Kind, Is.EqualTo(CellKind.Goal));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.All(cell => cell.transform.lossyScale == Vector3.one), Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>().Length +
                session.Towers.Sum(tower => tower.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>().Length), Is.EqualTo(103));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<Animator>(FindObjectsSortMode.None), Is.Empty);
        }

        [Test]
        public void MovingSceneTowerChangesCapturedPositionAndActualAttacks()
        {
            var tower = session.Towers[0];
            var before = tower.CaptureDefinition(1);
            var sparePlatform = session.Grid.SceneCells.Single(cell => cell.Occupancy == new Vector3Int(1, 0, 5));
            tower.transform.position = sparePlatform.TopCenter;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            var after = tower.CaptureDefinition(1);
            Assert.That(after.Position, Is.EqualTo(tower.transform.position));
            Assert.That(after.Position, Is.Not.EqualTo(before.Position));
            // A unit beside the old tower is in its old range and outside its new range.
            var points = new[] { new Vector3(2, 0.25f, 2), new Vector3(3, 0.25f, 2) };
            BattleSimulation Make(TowerDefinition definition) => new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(points,
                new[] { definition }, new UnitDefinition(100, 0.01f), 1, 1, 1));
            var oldSimulation = Make(before);
            var newSimulation = Make(after);
            oldSimulation.Step(0.02f);
            newSimulation.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(oldSimulation.Units[0].Hp, Is.LessThan(100));
            Assert.That(newSimulation.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(100));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SceneTowerCanMoveFreelyWithinPlatformWithoutSnapping()
        {
            var tower = session.Towers[0];
            tower.transform.position += new Vector3(0.2f, 0, -0.15f);
            var captured = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(captured.Towers[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(tower.transform.position));
            Assert.That(captured.Towers[0].Position.x, Is.EqualTo(2.2f));
        }

        [Test]
        public void MovingRoadTransformChangesNextSimulationWaypointsAndMovement()
        {
            var old = session.CaptureDefinition();
            // Move an actual Road cell and Goal, swapping ground cells to retain unique occupancy.
            var road = session.Route.Cells[16];
            var roadGround = session.Grid.SceneCells.Single(cell => cell.Occupancy == new Vector3Int(9, 0, 1));
            var roadPosition = road.transform.position;
            road.transform.position = roadGround.transform.position;
            roadGround.transform.position = roadPosition;
            var goal = session.Route.Cells[17];
            var ground = session.Grid.SceneCells.Single(cell => cell.Occupancy == new Vector3Int(10, 0, 1));
            var position = goal.transform.position;
            goal.transform.position = ground.transform.position;
            ground.transform.position = position;
            var updated = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(updated.Waypoints[16], Is.EqualTo(road.TopCenter));
            Assert.That(updated.Waypoints[16], Is.Not.EqualTo(old.Waypoints[16]));
            Assert.That(updated.Waypoints[17], Is.EqualTo(goal.TopCenter));
            Assert.That(updated.Waypoints[17], Is.Not.EqualTo(old.Waypoints[17]));
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(updated.Waypoints, new TowerDefinition[0],
                new UnitDefinition(100, 16), 1, 1, 2));
            sim.Step(1);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(road.TopCenter));
            sim.Step(1);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(goal.TopCenter));
            Assert.That(sim.ReachedGoalCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void GridConflictsAndDisconnectedRoutesAreDetected()
        {
            session.Route.Cells[1].transform.position = session.Route.Cells[3].transform.position;
            var errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
            Assert.That(errors.Any(error => error.Contains("Grid conflict")), Is.True);
            Assert.That(errors.Any(error => error.Contains("disconnected")), Is.True);
            Assert.Throws<System.InvalidOperationException>(() => session.CaptureDefinition());
        }

        [Test]
        public void UnequalHeightBlockedAndMissingRouteCellsAreDetected()
        {
            var road = session.Route.Cells[2];
            road.transform.position += Vector3.up * BattleCell.LayerHeight;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("unequal height")), Is.True);
            road.transform.position -= Vector3.up * BattleCell.LayerHeight;
            var stacked = session.Grid.SceneCells.First(cell => cell.Occupancy.y == 1);
            stacked.transform.position = road.transform.position + Vector3.up * BattleCell.LayerHeight;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("not passable")), Is.True);
            var data = new SerializedObject(session.Route);
            data.FindProperty("cells").GetArrayElementAtIndex(1).objectReferenceValue = null;
            data.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration().Any(error => error.Contains("missing")), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SaveAndReopenPreservesEditedTransformsAndSceneReferences()
        {
            var tower = session.Towers[0];
            tower.transform.position = session.Grid.SceneCells.Single(cell => cell.Occupancy == new Vector3Int(1, 0, 5)).TopCenter;
            var expected = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(session.gameObject.scene, RoundTripPath, true), Is.True);
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(RoundTripPath, OpenSceneMode.Single);
            var reloaded = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var actual = reloaded.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(actual.Waypoints, Is.EqualTo(expected.Waypoints));
            Assert.That(actual.Towers.Select(value => value.Position), Is.EqualTo(expected.Towers.Select(value => value.Position)));
            Assert.That(reloaded.Grid.SceneCells.Length, Is.EqualTo(100));
            Assert.That(reloaded.Route.Cells.All(cell => cell.gameObject.scene == reloaded.gameObject.scene), Is.True);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\SquadBattleSceneTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class SquadBattleSceneTests
    {
        [TearDown]
        public void Close() => EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);

        [Test]
        public void LevelOneUsesSavedSmallCellsAndLegacyRouteWithCurrentSessionAndHud()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(Team5.Rebuild.Session.BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Length, Is.EqualTo(20 * 14));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells.Count, Is.EqualTo(19));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells.First().TopCenter, Is.EqualTo(new Vector3(10, 0.25f, 16)));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells.Last().TopCenter, Is.EqualTo(new Vector3(19, 0.25f, 7)));
            Assert.That(session.Towers.Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            var definition = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(session.UsesSceneRosterOnly, Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.LevelTimeMultiplier, Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(definition.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(definition.Members[0].Unit.Role, Is.EqualTo(UnitRole.Captain));
            Assert.That(definition.Members[0].SlotId, Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(definition.Towers[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(new Vector3(22, 0.25f, 16)));
            Assert.That(definition.Towers[0].Area, Is.EqualTo(AttackArea.NegativeX));
            Assert.That(definition.Towers[0].Range, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI.LegacyBattleHud>().Session, Is.SameAs(session));
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => new BattleSimulation(definition));
            var count = session.Grid.SceneCells.Length;
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(Team5.Rebuild.Session.BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Length, Is.EqualTo(count));
        }

        [Test]
        public void MigrationScenePreservesEveryTerrainRouteAndCameraBlock()
        {
            Dictionary<string, string> Blocks(string path) => Regex.Split(File.ReadAllText(path), @"(?=^--- !u!)", RegexOptions.Multiline)
                .Where(b => b.StartsWith("---")).ToDictionary(b => Regex.Match(b, @"^--- !u!\d+ &(-?\d+)").Groups[1].Value);
            var before = Blocks(MacroPrototypeSceneBuilder.ScenePath);
            var after = Blocks(SquadBattleMenu.ScenePath);
            var changes = before.Where(pair => !after.TryGetValue(pair.Key, out var block) || block != pair.Value).ToArray();
            Assert.That(changes.Length, Is.EqualTo(7));
            Assert.That(changes.Count(pair => pair.Value.Contains("Team5.Rebuild.World.BattleTower")), Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(changes.Count(pair => pair.Value.Contains("Team5.Rebuild.Session.BattleSession")), Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(changes.Count(pair => pair.Value.StartsWith("--- !u!1 &")), Is.EqualTo(1));
            var sessionParent = changes.Single(pair => pair.Value.StartsWith("--- !u!4 &"));
            Assert.That(sessionParent.Value, Does.Contain("m_GameObject: {fileID: 2044225505}"));
            // Only parenting the authored HUD is allowed; every transform value is unchanged.
            var parentAfter = Regex.Replace(after[sessionParent.Key], @"  m_Children:\r?\n  - \{fileID: \d+\}", "  m_Children: []");
            Assert.That(parentAfter, Is.EqualTo(sessionParent.Value));
            // Saving an edited Scene now serializes the existing optional range toggle.
            var presentation = changes.Single(pair => pair.Value.Contains("Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.BattlePresentation"));
            Assert.That(Regex.Replace(after[presentation.Key], @"  showTowerRanges: [01]\r?\n", ""), Is.EqualTo(presentation.Value));
            // HUD minus the removed currency GameObject and its four component blocks.
            Assert.That(after.Count, Is.EqualTo(before.Count + 39));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DebugSceneProducesSixIndependentMembersAndSceneTransformsRemainAuthoritative()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(SquadBattleMenu.ScenePath);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            var first = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(first.Objective, Is.EqualTo(BattleObjective.ReachGoal));
            Assert.That(first.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(6));
            Assert.That(first.Members.Select(m => m.MemberId).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(6));
            Assert.That(first.Members.Select(m => m.Unit.Role).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(3));
            var before = session.Grid.SceneCells.Select(c => c.transform.position).ToArray();
            session.Grid.transform.position += Vector3.right * 3;
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers) tower.transform.position += Vector3.right * 3;
            var next = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(next.Waypoints[0], Is.EqualTo(first.Waypoints[0] + Vector3.right * 3));
            Assert.That(next.Towers[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(first.Towers[0].Position + Vector3.right * 3));
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => new BattleSimulation(next));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Select(c => c.transform.position), Is.EqualTo(before.Select(p => p + Vector3.right * 3)));
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\SquadCombatTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class SquadCombatTests
    {
        private static readonly Vector3[] Route = { new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 0), new Vector3(100, 0.25f, 0) };
        private static MemberSpawnDefinition Member(int id, int slot, UnitRole role = UnitRole.Attack, float at = 0)
            => new MemberSpawnDefinition(id, slot, SquadCombatRules.Create(role), at);
        private static UnitDefinition Passive(float hp = 4) => new UnitDefinition(hp, 0.01f);
        private static TowerDefinition Tower(Vector3 position, float range = 10, float damage = 1, float interval = 100,
            float hp = 20, AttackArea area = AttackArea.Square, float width = 0.3f, int id = 1)
            => new TowerDefinition(id, position, range, damage, interval, TargetRule.Nearest, 1, hp, area, width);
        private static BattleSimulation Make(IEnumerable<MemberSpawnDefinition> members, params TowerDefinition[] towers)
            => new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(Route, towers, members));
        private static void Tick(BattleSimulation sim, int count)
        { for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) sim.Step(0.02f); }

        [Test]
        public void CaptainUsesConfiguredStatsAndExistingProjectileCombat()
        {
            var stats = SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain);
            Assert.That((stats.MaxHp, stats.Defense, stats.Damage), Is.EqualTo((6f, 0.5f, 0.5f)));
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(110, 4, UnitRole.Captain) }, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 2)));
            Tick(sim, 40);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(5.5f).Within(0.0001));
            Assert.That(sim.Towers[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(19.5f).Within(0.0001));
        }

        [Test]
        public void LegacyRoleValuesComeFromVerifiedCode()
        {
            var attack = SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Attack);
            Assert.That((attack.MaxHp, attack.Damage, attack.Range, attack.AttackInterval, attack.Speed), Is.EqualTo((4f, 1f, 2.5f, 1f, 0.5f)));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Tank).Speed, Is.EqualTo(0.5f));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Heal).Speed, Is.EqualTo(0.5f));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Tank).Defense, Is.EqualTo(0.2f));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Tank).MaxHp, Is.EqualTo(8));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Heal).MaxHp, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.HealInterval, Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.HealAmount, Is.EqualTo(0.4f));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SwapPreservesIdentityHpCooldownAndLocksProjectileToOriginalSlot()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(104, 4), Member(101, 1, UnitRole.Tank) }, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 2)));
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            var attack = sim.Units.Single(u => u.MemberId == 104);
            var tank = sim.Units.Single(u => u.MemberId == 101);
            var attackCooldown = attack.AttackCooldown;
            Assert.That(sim.Projectiles.Single(p => p.TargetKind == ProjectileTargetKind.FormationSlot).TargetId, Is.EqualTo(4));
            sim.SetPaused(true);
            var swap = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).ToArray(); swap[4] = 1; swap[1] = 4;
            Assert.That(sim.TryApplyFormation(swap), Is.True);
            Assert.That(sim.GetMemberAtSlot(1), Is.SameAs(attack));
            Assert.That(sim.GetMemberAtSlot(4), Is.SameAs(tank));
            Assert.That((attack.Hp, attack.AttackCooldown), Is.EqualTo((4f, attackCooldown)));
            sim.SetPaused(false); Tick(sim, 25);
            Assert.That(attack.Hp, Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(tank.Hp, Is.EqualTo(7.2f).Within(0.0001));
        }

        [Test]
        public void EmptyLockedSlotMissesAndDeadMemberNeverRevives()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(77, 4, UnitRole.Tank) }, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 2)));
            sim.Step(0.02f); sim.SetPaused(true);
            var swap = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).ToArray(); swap[4] = 1; swap[1] = 4;
            Assert.That(sim.TryApplyFormation(swap), Is.True);
            sim.SetPaused(false); Tick(sim, 30);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(8));
            Assert.That(sim.GetMemberAtSlot(4), Is.Null);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SwappingAnAlreadyInjuredMemberDoesNotResetItsHpOrCooldown()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(104, 4), Member(101, 1, UnitRole.Tank) }, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 2)));
            Tick(sim, 20);
            var attack = sim.Units.Single(u => u.MemberId == 104);
            Assert.That(attack.Hp, Is.EqualTo(3));
            var before = (attack.MemberId, attack.Hp, attack.AttackCooldown, attack.RouteProgress);
            sim.SetPaused(true);
            var swap = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).ToArray(); swap[4] = 1; swap[1] = 4;
            Assert.That(sim.TryApplyFormation(swap), Is.True);
            Assert.That((attack.MemberId, attack.Hp, attack.AttackCooldown, attack.RouteProgress), Is.EqualTo(before));
        }

        [Test]
        public void PauseFreezesSpawnMovementShotsCooldownAndHealingTime()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(1, 4), Member(2, 0, UnitRole.Heal, 1) }, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 2)));
            sim.Step(0.02f); sim.SetPaused(true);
            var unit = sim.Units[0]; var shot = sim.Projectiles[0];
            var before = (unit.Position, unit.Hp, unit.AttackCooldown, shot.Position, sim.ElapsedTime, sim.PendingSpawnCount);
            Tick(sim, 200);
            Assert.That((unit.Position, unit.Hp, unit.AttackCooldown, shot.Position, sim.ElapsedTime, sim.PendingSpawnCount), Is.EqualTo(before));
            sim.SetPaused(false); Tick(sim, 60);
            Assert.That(sim.PendingSpawnCount, Is.Zero);
        }

        [Test]
        public void FirstArrivalWinsOnceAndCancelsPendingGeneration()
        {
            var members = new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(33, 4, new UnitDefinition(4, 100)), new MemberSpawnDefinition(44, 0, new UnitDefinition(4, 100), 10) };
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(new[] { Vector3.zero, Vector3.right }, Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(), members));
            var ended = 0; var arrived = 0;
            sim.BattleEnded += _ => ended++;
            sim.UnitReachedGoal += _ => { arrived++; Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory)); };
            Tick(sim, 1000);
            Assert.That((ended, arrived, sim.SpawnedCount), Is.EqualTo((1, 1, 1)));
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
        }

        [Test]
        public void NoLivingMembersDoesNotLoseWhileAnotherMemberIsScheduled()
        {
            var members = new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(11, 4, Passive(1)), new MemberSpawnDefinition(22, 0, Passive(1), 2) };
            var sim = Make(members, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 0), damage: 10, interval: 1));
            var ends = 0; sim.BattleEnded += _ => ends++;
            Tick(sim, 10);
            Assert.That((sim.AliveCount, sim.PendingSpawnCount), Is.EqualTo((0, 1)));
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
            Tick(sim, 180);
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Defeat));
            Assert.That((sim.KilledCount, ends), Is.EqualTo((2, 1)));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DelayedMembersJoinTheMovingAnchorWithoutBreakingFormation()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(1, 4), Member(2, 0, UnitRole.Tank, 0.5f) });
            Tick(sim, 40);
            Assert.That((sim.Units[0].AnchorPosition - sim.Units[1].AnchorPosition).magnitude, Is.LessThan(0.0001));
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Select(u => u.OccupiedCell).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(2));
        }

        [Test]
        public void AllEightNeighborsAreHealedIncludingDiagonalsAtSqrtTwoWorldDistance()
        {
            var members = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).Select(slot => new MemberSpawnDefinition(100 + slot, slot,
                slot == 4 ? new UnitDefinition(3, 0.01f, UnitRole.Heal) : Passive())).ToArray();
            var towers = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).Select(slot => Tower(Route[0] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot), range: 0.1f, id: slot + 1)).ToArray();
            var sim = Make(members, towers);
            var heals = new List<HealEvent>(); sim.UnitHealed += heals.Add;
            Tick(sim, 99);
            Assert.That(heals, Is.Empty);
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(heals.Count, Is.EqualTo(8));
            Assert.That(heals.All(h => h.Amount == SquadCombatRules.HealAmount), Is.True);
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Where(u => u.SlotId != 4).All(u => Mathf.Abs(u.Hp - 3.4f) < 0.0001), Is.True);
            Assert.That(sim.GetMemberAtSlot(4).Hp, Is.EqualTo(2), "Healer does not heal itself.");
            Assert.That(Vector3.Distance(sim.GetMemberAtSlot(4).Position, sim.GetMemberAtSlot(0).Position), Is.GreaterThan(1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void HealingDoesNotReachNonNeighborsOverhealOrResurrect()
        {
            var members = new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 0, new UnitDefinition(3, 0.01f, UnitRole.Heal)),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(2, 8, Passive()), new MemberSpawnDefinition(3, 1, Passive(0.5f)),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(4, 3, Passive()) };
            var sim = Make(members,
                Tower(Route[0] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(8), range: 0.1f, id: 1),
                Tower(Route[0] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(1), range: 0.1f, id: 2));
            Tick(sim, 220);
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Single(u => u.MemberId == 2).Hp, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Single(u => u.MemberId == 3).LifeState, Is.EqualTo(UnitLifeState.Dead));
            Assert.That(sim.Units.Single(u => u.MemberId == 4).Hp, Is.EqualTo(4));
        }

        [Test]
        public void AttackTargetsNearestTowerAndClampsDamageToRemainingHp()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(99, 4) },
                Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 2), range: 0, hp: 0.5f),
                Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 3), range: 0, id: 2));
            var deaths = 0; var damage = new List<TowerDamageEvent>();
            sim.TowerDied += _ => deaths++; sim.TowerDamaged += damage.Add;
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Towers[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(0.5f), "Shots have travel time.");
            Assert.That(sim.Projectiles.Single().TargetId, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Tick(sim, 25);
            Assert.That((sim.Towers[0].Hp, deaths), Is.EqualTo((0f, 1)));
            Assert.That(damage.Single().Amount, Is.EqualTo(0.5f));
            Assert.That(sim.Towers[1].Hp, Is.EqualTo(20));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DestroyingAllTowersIsNotAVictoryAndShotsDoNotOverkill()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(1, 4), Member(2, 1) }, Tower(new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 1), range: 0, hp: 1));
            var deaths = 0; sim.TowerDied += _ => deaths++;
            Tick(sim, 60);
            Assert.That(sim.Towers[0].IsAlive, Is.False);
            Assert.That(deaths, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sim.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DirectionalGeometryAndFastProjectileSweepRespectNarrowLane()
        {
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(1, 0, 0.2f), AttackArea.PositiveX, 3, 0.3f), Is.False);
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(-1, 0, 0), AttackArea.PositiveX, 3, 0.3f), Is.False);
            var sim = Make(new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(8, 4, Passive()) },
                Tower(new Vector3(-0.1f, 0.25f, 0), range: 3, area: AttackArea.PositiveX));
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(3), "Swept hit must not skip a nearby target.");
        }

        [Test]
        public void IllegalFormationIsRejectedAtomicallyAndNotAllowedWhileRunning()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(55, 4) }); sim.Step(0.02f);
            var permutation = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).ToArray();
            Assert.That(sim.TryApplyFormation(permutation), Is.False);
            sim.SetPaused(true); permutation[0] = 1;
            Assert.That(sim.TryApplyFormation(permutation), Is.False);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].SlotId, Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => Make(new[] { Member(1, 4), Member(1, 0) }));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => Make(new[] { Member(1, 4), Member(2, 4) }));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => Make(Array.Empty<MemberSpawnDefinition>()));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SlotTrajectoriesDetectBlockedTurnEvenWhenCenterlineIsClear()
        {
            var route = new[] { new Vector3(1, 0.25f, 1), new Vector3(3, 0.25f, 1), new Vector3(3, 0.25f, 3) };
            var cells = new HashSet<Vector3Int>();
            for (var segment = 1; segment < route.Length; segment++)
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                foreach (var cell in FormationClearance.SweptCells(route[segment - 1] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot), route[segment] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot))) cells.Add(cell);
            var members = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).Select(slot => Member(100 + slot, slot)).ToArray();
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(route, Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(), members, cells)));
            Assert.That(cells.Remove(new Vector3Int(4, 0, 2)), Is.True);
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(route, Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(), members, cells)));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => FormationClearance.SweptCells(Vector3.zero, new Vector3(1, 0, 1)).ToArray());
        }

        [Test]
        public void CallbacksCannotReenterTicksOrMutateFormation()
        {
            var sim = Make(new[] { Member(1, 4) });
            sim.UnitSpawned += _ =>
            {
                Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => sim.Step(0.02f));
                Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => sim.SetPaused(true));
                Assert.That(sim.TryApplyFormation(Enumerable.Range(0, 9).ToArray()), Is.False);
            };
            sim.Step(0.02f);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\SquareUnitAppearanceTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class SquareUnitAppearanceTests
    {
        // MPB stores colors in linear space; an sRGB readback can differ by a few float ULPs.
        private static void SameColor(Color actual, Color expected)
            => Assert.That(Vector4.Distance(actual, expected), Is.LessThan(0.00001f));
        [TestCase(UnitRole.Attack, "Mark Diamond")]
        [TestCase(UnitRole.Tank, "Mark Plate")]
