        [TestCase(UnitRole.Heal, "Mark Across")]
        public void WorldSquareUsesFormationIconColorsAndMarksWithoutChangingState(UnitRole role, string markName)
        {
            var prefab = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<UnitView>(MinimalPresentationSetup.UnitPrefabPath);
            var view = Object.Instantiate(prefab);
            var uiRoot = new GameObject("Icon Comparison", typeof(RectTransform));
            try
            {
                var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(new[] { Vector3.zero, Vector3.right * 10 },
                    Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(), new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(73, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(role)) }));
                sim.Step(0.02f);
                var state = sim.Units[0];
                var before = (state.MemberId, state.Position, state.Hp, state.AttackCooldown, state.SlotId);
                view.Bind(state);
                var icon = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(uiRoot.transform, Vector2.zero, 100);
                icon.SetType(role);
                Color Tint(string name)
                {
                    var block = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
                    view.transform.Find(name).GetComponent<Renderer>().GetPropertyBlock(block);
                    return block.GetColor("_BaseColor");
                }
                SameColor(Tint("Body"), icon.transform.Find("Body").GetComponent<Image>().color);
                SameColor(Tint("Outline"), icon.transform.Find("Outline").GetComponent<Image>().color);
                SameColor(Tint(markName), LegacyUnitLooks.For(role).MarkColor);
                var outline = view.transform.Find("Outline");
                Assert.That(outline.localScale.x, Is.EqualTo(outline.localScale.z));
                Assert.That(outline.localScale.x, Is.LessThanOrEqualTo(1));
                Assert.That(view.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>(true).All(mesh => mesh.sharedMesh.name == "Cube"), Is.True);
                Assert.That(view.GetComponentInChildren<Collider>(true), Is.Null);
                Assert.That(view.transform.Find(markName).gameObject.activeSelf, Is.True);
                Assert.That(view.transform.Find(markName).localPosition.y, Is.GreaterThan(view.transform.Find("Body").localPosition.y));
                Assert.That(view.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>().All(renderer => renderer.sharedMaterial.shader.name == "Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit"), Is.True);
                foreach (var part in new[] { "Mark Diamond", "Mark Plate", "Mark Across", "Mark Upright" })
                    Assert.That(view.transform.Find(part).gameObject.activeSelf,
                        Is.EqualTo(part == markName || role == UnitRole.Heal && part == "Mark Upright"));
                Assert.That((state.MemberId, state.Position, state.Hp, state.AttackCooldown, state.SlotId), Is.EqualTo(before));
                view.ApplyAppearance(UnitRole.Tank);
                view.ApplyAppearance(role);
                SameColor(Tint("Body"), LegacyUnitLooks.For(role).Body);
                Object.DestroyImmediate(view.transform.Find("Body").GetComponent<Renderer>());
                Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => view.ApplyAppearance(role));
                Assert.That((state.MemberId, state.Position, state.Hp, state.AttackCooldown, state.SlotId), Is.EqualTo(before));
            }
            finally { Object.DestroyImmediate(view.gameObject); Object.DestroyImmediate(uiRoot); }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\TowerRangeTests.cs ===
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class TowerRangeTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void SquareIncludesCornersAndUsesHorizontalWorldDistance()
        {
            Assert.That((int)AttackArea.Square, Is.Zero, "Existing Scene enum value must keep its meaning.");
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(4.5f, 7, 4.5f), AttackArea.Square, 4.5f, 1), Is.True);
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(-4.5f, 0, -4.5f), AttackArea.Square, 4.5f, 1), Is.True);
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(4.501f, 0, 0), AttackArea.Square, 4.5f, 1), Is.False);
            Assert.That(SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(0, 0, -4.501f), AttackArea.Square, 4.5f, 1), Is.False);
        }

        [TestCase(AttackArea.Square)]
        [TestCase(AttackArea.PositiveX)]
        [TestCase(AttackArea.NegativeX)]
        [TestCase(AttackArea.PositiveZ)]
        [TestCase(AttackArea.NegativeZ)]
        public void DisplayBoundsMatchTargetingForEveryArea(AttackArea area)
        {
            var origin = new Vector3(10, 0.25f, 20);
            var bounds = SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(origin, area, 3, 1);
            for (var z = -4f; z <= 4; z += 0.25f)
            for (var x = -4f; x <= 4; x += 0.25f)
            {
                var offset = new Vector3(x, 0, z);
                Assert.That(bounds.Contains(origin + offset), Is.EqualTo(SquadCombatRules.Contains(offset, area, 3, 1)));
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void TowerActuallyAttacksMemberInsideSquareCorner()
        {
            var tower = new TowerDefinition(1, new Vector3(3, 0.25f, 3), 3, 1, 1, TargetRule.Nearest);
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(new[] { new Vector3(0, 0.25f, 0), new Vector3(10, 0.25f, 0) },
                new[] { tower }, new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, new UnitDefinition(4, 0.01f)) }));
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Towers[0].TargetId, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sim.Projectiles.Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            for (var i = 0; i < 30; i++) sim.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sim.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(3));
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\UnitDetailsTextTests.cs ===
using System;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class UnitDetailsTextTests
    {
        [TestCase(UnitRole.Attack, "Attack: 1", "HP: 4 / 4")]
        [TestCase(UnitRole.Tank, "Defense: 20% damage reduction", "HP: 8 / 8")]
        [TestCase(UnitRole.Heal, "Healing: 0.4 HP / 2 s", "HP: 3 / 3")]
        [TestCase(UnitRole.Captain, "Defense: 50% damage reduction", "HP: 6 / 6")]
        public void DetailsUseTheSelectedMembersActualRules(UnitRole role, string expected, string hp)
        {
            var sim = new BattleSimulation(new BattleDefinition(new[] { Vector3.zero, Vector3.right * 10 },
                Array.Empty<TowerDefinition>(), new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(731, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(role)) }));
            sim.Step(0.02f);
            var unit = sim.Units[0];
            var before = (unit.Hp, unit.Position, unit.AttackCooldown, unit.SlotId);
            var text = UnitDetailsText.Format(unit);
            Assert.That(text, Does.Contain(expected));
            Assert.That(text, Does.Contain(hp));
            Assert.That(text, Does.Contain("Move speed: 0.5 units/s"));
            Assert.That((unit.Hp, unit.Position, unit.AttackCooldown, unit.SlotId), Is.EqualTo(before));
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\UpgradePreparationTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class UpgradePreparationTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void LevelRewardConfigurationSupportsIndependentGoldAndUnitLists()
        {
            var settings = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameEconomySettings>("Assets/Rebuild/Resources/RebuildEconomy.asset");
            var first = settings.RewardForLevel(1);
            Assert.That(first.gold, Is.EqualTo(10));
            Assert.That(first.CaptureUnitRoles(), Is.EqualTo(new[] { UnitRole.Tank }));
            Assert.That(settings.RewardForLevel(2).gold, Is.EqualTo(100));
            Assert.That(settings.RewardForLevel(2).CaptureUnitRoles(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(settings.RewardForLevel(99), Is.Null);
        }

        [Test]
        public void RewardMemberHasNewIdentityAndDoesNotMutateInventoryOrDeployment()
        {
            var original = new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(110, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)) };
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(original);
            preparation.TryAssign(110, 4);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Tank), Is.True);
            Assert.That(original.Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(preparation.Members.Select(member => member.MemberId), Is.EqualTo(new[] { 110, 111 }));
            Assert.That(preparation.MemberAtSlot(4), Is.EqualTo(110));
            Assert.That(preparation.IsDeployed(111), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(111, 7), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.CaptureDeployment().Single(member => member.MemberId == 111).Unit.Role, Is.EqualTo(UnitRole.Tank));
            for (var i = preparation.Members.Count; i < SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; i++) preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Attack);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Heal), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(12));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Test));
        }
        [Test]
        public void WalletRewardsEachLevelOnceAndPurchasesNeverOverdraw()
        {
            var wallet = new CampaignWallet();
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100), Is.False);
            Assert.That(wallet.TrySpend(40), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(60));
            Assert.That(wallet.TrySpend(61), Is.False);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(60));
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(2, 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(160));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TrySpend(-1));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TrySpend(0));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TryRewardLevel(0, 100));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TryRewardLevel(3, -1));
            Assert.That(new CampaignWallet().Balance, Is.Zero);
        }

        [Test]
        public void RewardOverflowDoesNotConsumeLevelReward()
        {
            var wallet = new CampaignWallet(int.MaxValue);
            Assert.Throws<OverflowException>(() => wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100));
            Assert.That(wallet.TrySpend(100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(int.MaxValue));
        }
        [TearDown] public void Close() => EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);

        [Test]
        public void UpgradeHasNoMissingDependenciesAndSharesPrototypeRoster()
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            var upgrade = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            Assert.That(upgrade.Roster, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(upgrade.StartWaveButton, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(upgrade.transform.Find("GoldTXT"), Is.Null);
            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
                Assert.That(root.GetComponentsInChildren<Component>(true).All(component => component != null), Is.True);
            var roster = upgrade.Roster;
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.PrototypeBattleScene);
            Assert.That(UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().InitialRoster, Is.SameAs(roster));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SelectedWarehouseMembersKeepIdentityAndStatsInBattle()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.PrototypeBattleScene);
            var session = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var before = session.InitialRoster.CaptureMembers();
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(session.InitialRoster);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(preparation.CanStart, Is.False);
            var selected = preparation.Members.First(member => member.SlotId == 4);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(selected.MemberId, 8), Is.True);
            session.SetPreparedMembers(preparation.CaptureDeployment());
            var after = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var result = after.Members.Single();
            Assert.That((result.MemberId, result.SlotId), Is.EqualTo((selected.MemberId, 8)));
            Assert.That(result.Unit, Is.SameAs(selected.Unit));
            Assert.That(result.SpawnTime, Is.EqualTo(selected.SpawnTime));
            Assert.That(session.InitialRoster.CaptureMembers().Select(member => (member.MemberId, member.SlotId)),
                Is.EqualTo(before.Select(member => (member.MemberId, member.SlotId))));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => session.SetPreparedMembers(before.Take(7).ToArray()));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SixMemberCapAllowsMovesReplacementAndReturnWithoutDuplicates()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.PrototypeBattleScene);
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().InitialRoster);
            var available = preparation.Members;
            var changes = 0; preparation.Changed += () => changes++;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 6; slot++) Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[slot].MemberId, slot), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[6].MemberId, 8), Is.False);
            Assert.That((preparation.DeployedCount, changes), Is.EqualTo((6, 6)));
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[0].MemberId, 8), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.MemberAtSlot(0), Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[6].MemberId, 8), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.IsDeployed(available[0].MemberId), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.EqualTo(6));
            Assert.That(preparation.TryRemove(8), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.EqualTo(5));
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[7].MemberId, 7), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.CaptureDeployment().Select(member => member.MemberId).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(6));
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\PlayMode\BattleSessionPlayModeTests.cs ===
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class BattleSessionPlayModeTests
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        [UnitySetUp]
        public IEnumerator LoadScene()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/GridBattlePrototype.unity");
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false; // Tests control ticks; StartBattle remains an explicit public operation.
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("logEvents", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, false);
        }
        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Cleanup()
        {
            var empty = SceneManager.CreateScene("Rebuild Test Cleanup");
            SceneManager.SetActiveScene(empty);
            yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(session.gameObject.scene);
        }
        private static void Finish(BattleSession value)
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < 10000 && value.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) value.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(value.Simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator DeletingSceneTowerLeavesRemainingTowersAlignedAcrossRestart()
        {
            var original = session.Towers.ToArray();
            Object.Destroy(original[1].gameObject);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Towers, Is.EqualTo(new[] { original[0], original[2] }));
            for (var restart = 0; restart < 2; restart++)
            {
                session.StartBattle();
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Towers.Count, Is.EqualTo(2));
                for (var i = 0; i < session.Towers.Count; i++)
                {
                    Assert.That(session.Towers[i].RuntimeState, Is.SameAs(session.Simulation.Towers[i]));
                    Assert.That(session.Simulation.Towers[i].Definition.Id, Is.EqualTo(i + 1));
                    Assert.That(session.Simulation.Towers[i].Position, Is.EqualTo(session.Towers[i].transform.position));
                }
                Finish(session);
            }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator NormalFixedUpdateStartsAndCompletesBattle()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 20;
            try
            {
                session.enabled = true;
                session.StartBattle();
                var deadline = Time.realtimeSinceStartup + 15;
                while (session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && Time.realtimeSinceStartup < deadline) yield return null;
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.KilledCount + session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            }
            finally { Time.timeScale = 1; }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator CombatRunsWithDebugViewDisabledAndStaticEntitiesAreNeverCloned()
        {
            var cells = session.Grid.SceneCells;
            var towers = session.Towers.ToArray();
            var cellPositions = cells.Select(value => value.transform.position).ToArray();
            var initialObjects = cells.SelectMany(value => value.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true))
                .Concat(towers.SelectMany(value => value.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true)))
                .Select(value => value.GetInstanceID()).ToArray();
            var view = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleDebugGizmos>();
            view.enabled = false;
            session.StartBattle();
            var damage = 0;
            var deaths = 0;
            var ended = 0;
            session.Simulation.UnitDamaged += _ => damage++;
            session.Simulation.UnitDied += _ => deaths++;
            session.Simulation.BattleEnded += _ => ended++;
            Finish(session);
            Assert.That(damage, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(deaths, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(ended, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells, Is.EquivalentTo(cells));
            Assert.That(session.Towers, Is.EqualTo(towers));
            Assert.That(cells.Select(value => value.transform.position), Is.EqualTo(cellPositions));
            Assert.That(cells.SelectMany(value => value.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true))
                .Concat(towers.SelectMany(value => value.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true)))
                .Select(value => value.GetInstanceID()), Is.EqualTo(initialObjects));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleCell>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Length, Is.EqualTo(100));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleTower>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Length, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<Animator>(FindObjectsSortMode.None), Is.Empty);
            yield return null;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator RemovingDebugViewPreservesResultAndRestartResetsRuntimeOnly()
        {
            session.StartBattle();
            Finish(session);
            var expected = (session.Simulation.Outcome, session.Simulation.KilledCount, session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount);
            Object.Destroy(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleDebugGizmos>());
            yield return null;
            session.StartBattle();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.SpawnedCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Towers.All(tower => tower.CooldownRemaining == 0), Is.True);
            Finish(session);
            Assert.That((session.Simulation.Outcome, session.Simulation.KilledCount, session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount), Is.EqualTo(expected));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator SceneTowerTransformDrivesAttackOriginAtRuntime()
        {
            session.StartBattle();
            var tower = session.Towers[0];
            tower.transform.position += new Vector3(0.2f, 0, 0.1f);
            TowerAttackEvent? attack = null;
            session.Simulation.TowerAttacked += value => { if (value.TowerId == 1) attack = value; };
            for (var i = 0; i < 500 && !attack.HasValue; i++) session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(attack.HasValue, Is.True);
            Assert.That(attack.Value.Origin, Is.EqualTo(tower.transform.position));
            Assert.That(tower.RuntimeState, Is.SameAs(session.Simulation.Towers[0]));
            yield return null;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator LargerWaveSpawnsManyUnitsAndGoalEventsResolveCorrectly()
        {
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("unitCount", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, 128);
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("spawnInterval", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, 0.02f);
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("goalLives", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, 129);
            session.StartBattle();
            var goals = 0;
            var maximumAlive = 0;
            session.Simulation.UnitReachedGoal += _ => goals++;
            for (var i = 0; i < 10000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++)
            {
                session.Advance(0.02f);
                maximumAlive = System.Math.Max(maximumAlive, session.Simulation.AliveCount);
            }
            Assert.That(maximumAlive, Is.GreaterThan(100));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.SpawnedCount, Is.EqualTo(128));
            Assert.That(goals, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(goals, Is.EqualTo(session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.KilledCount + goals, Is.EqualTo(128));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            yield return null;
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\PlayMode\GameMenuFlowTests.cs ===
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class GameMenuFlowTests
    {
        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TutorialPauseSkipAndReplayRestoreControlsWithoutChangingCaptain()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false;
            if (session.Simulation.Units.Count == 0) session.Advance(0.02f);
            var tutorial = session.GetComponent<LevelOneTutorial>();
            var hud = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyBattleHud>();
            var inspection = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleUnitInspection>();
            var camera = Camera.main;
            var pose = camera.transform.position;
            var size = camera.orthographicSize;
            var fov = camera.fieldOfView;
            var state = (session.Simulation.Units[0].Id, session.Simulation.Units[0].Hp, session.Simulation.Units[0].SlotId);
            for (var i = 0; i < 300 && tutorial.CurrentStep != 1; i++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(tutorial.CurrentStep, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            Assert.That(hud.TutorialOwnsPause, Is.True);
            Assert.That(inspection.IsInputSuppressed, Is.True);
            hud.TogglePause();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            tutorial.Skip();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.False);
            Assert.That(hud.TutorialOwnsPause, Is.False);
            Assert.That(inspection.IsInputSuppressed, Is.False);
            Assert.That(camera.transform.position, Is.EqualTo(pose));
            Assert.That(camera.orthographicSize, Is.EqualTo(size));
            Assert.That(camera.fieldOfView, Is.EqualTo(fov));
            Assert.That((session.Simulation.Units[0].Id, session.Simulation.Units[0].Hp, session.Simulation.Units[0].SlotId), Is.EqualTo(state));
            tutorial.Replay(); session.Advance(0.02f);
            for (var i = 0; i < 300 && tutorial.CurrentStep != 1; i++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(tutorial.CurrentStep, Is.EqualTo(1));
            tutorial.enabled = false;
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.False);
            Assert.That(hud.TutorialOwnsPause, Is.False);
            Assert.That(inspection.IsInputSuppressed, Is.False);
        }

        private static IEnumerator WaitForCaptainBattle()
        {
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>(), Is.Null);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            if (session.Simulation.Units.Count == 0) session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.UsesSceneRosterOnly, Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units.Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            var captain = session.Simulation.Units.Single();
            Assert.That(captain.Role, Is.EqualTo(UnitRole.Captain));
            Assert.That(captain.SlotId, Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(captain.MaxHp, Is.EqualTo(6));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.PendingSpawnCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.Throws<System.InvalidOperationException>(() => session.SetPreparedMembers(
                new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(101, 0, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Attack)) }));
        }

        private static IEnumerator WaitForScene(string path)
        {
            for (var frame = 0; frame < 600 && SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path != path; frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(path));
            yield return null; // Let Start and the asynchronous load finish.
            if (path == BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene)
            {
                var tutorial = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LevelOneTutorial>();
                if (tutorial != null) tutorial.enabled = false;
            }
        }

        private static void ConfigureOutcome(BattleSession session, bool victory)
        {
            var tutorial = session.GetComponent<LevelOneTutorial>();
            if (tutorial != null) tutorial.enabled = false;
            session.enabled = false;
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("logEvents", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(session, false);
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
            {
                typeof(BattleTower).GetField("damage", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(tower, 1000f);
                typeof(BattleTower).GetField("range", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(tower, victory ? 0f : 1000f);
                typeof(BattleTower).GetField("attackInterval", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance).SetValue(tower, 0.02f);
            }
            session.StartBattle();
        }

        private static void Finish(BattleSession session)
        {
            for (var tick = 0; tick < 10000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; tick++) session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
        }

        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Close()
        {
            foreach (var flow in Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleSceneFlow>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)) Object.Destroy(flow.gameObject);
            yield return null;
            var previous = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
            SceneManager.SetActiveScene(SceneManager.CreateScene("Menu Flow Cleanup"));
            if (previous.IsValid() && previous.isLoaded) yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(previous);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TitleLevelOneSettlementUpgradeLevelTwoEndAndNewRun()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            var start = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>();
            start.onClick.Invoke(); start.onClick.Invoke(); // A double click must not create a second run.
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleSceneFlow>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            ConfigureOutcome(session, true);
            Finish(session);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene), "Transition waits until the tick returns.");
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.LastOutcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(10));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.PendingUnitRewardCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardGold, Is.EqualTo(10));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardUnits, Is.EqualTo(new[] { UnitRole.Tank }));
            var rewardTexts = Object.FindObjectsByType<TMP_Text>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Select(label => label.text).ToArray();
            Assert.That(rewardTexts, Does.Contain("+10 GOLD"));
            Assert.That(rewardTexts, Does.Contain("+1 TANK UNIT"));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.TrySpendGold(1), Is.False, "Currency can only be spent in UpgradeScene.");
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            var upgrade = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            Assert.That(upgrade.LevelLabel.text, Does.StartWith("LEVEL 2").And.EndWith("3/3"));
            Assert.That(upgrade.LevelLabel.transform.Find("Heart Icon"), Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(upgrade.GoldLabel.text, Does.EndWith("10"));
            var tankReward = upgrade.Preparation.Members.Single(member => member.Unit.Role == UnitRole.Tank);
            Assert.That(tankReward.MemberId, Is.Not.EqualTo(110));
            Assert.That(upgrade.Roster.CaptureMembers().Count, Is.EqualTo(1), "Reward must not modify the asset.");
            var rewardCount = upgrade.Preparation.Members.Count;
            BattleSceneFlow.PrepareSquad(upgrade.Roster);
            Assert.That(upgrade.Preparation.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(rewardCount), "Reward was granted twice.");
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.PendingUnitRewardCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(upgrade.GoldLabel.rectTransform.anchorMax, Is.EqualTo(Vector2.one));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.TrySpendGold(3), Is.True);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.TrySpendGold(8), Is.False);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(upgrade.GoldLabel.text, Does.EndWith("7"));
            Assert.That(upgrade.Preparation.TryAssign(110, 4), Is.True);
            Assert.That(upgrade.Preparation.TryAssign(tankReward.MemberId, 7), Is.True);
            upgrade.StartWave(); upgrade.StartWave();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(session.DisplayedLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(7));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.TrySpendGold(1), Is.False);
            Assert.That(session.GetComponent<LevelOneTutorial>(), Is.Null);
            if (session.Simulation.Units.Count == 0) session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units.Select(unit => unit.Id), Is.EquivalentTo(new[] { 110, tankReward.MemberId }));
            ConfigureOutcome(session, true);
            Finish(session);
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.EndScene);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(107));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<TMP_Text>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)
                .Any(label => label.text.Trim() == "Thanks for playing"), Is.True);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive, Is.False);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(session.AttemptsPerLevel));
            Assert.That(session.PlaybackSpeed, Is.EqualTo(1));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator DefeatSettlesOnceAndRetryKeepsLevelAndLives()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            ConfigureOutcome(session, false);
            var transitions = 0;
            void Loaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode) { if (scene.path == BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene) transitions++; }
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded += Loaded;
            try
            {
                Finish(session);
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Defeat));
                session.Advance(0.02f);
                yield return null;
                Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene));
                Assert.That(transitions, Is.Zero);
                Assert.That(session.RecoveryRequired, Is.True);
                Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2));
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(1));
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.Zero, "Defeat grants no gold.");
                BattleSceneFlow.RestartCurrentLevel();
                yield return null;
                for (var frame = 0; frame < 600 && Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>() == session; frame++) yield return null;
                yield return null;
                Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2));
            }
            finally { SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= Loaded; }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator DirectLevelOneVictoryTransitionsToSettlementExactlyOnce()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive, Is.True);
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleSceneFlow>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            ConfigureOutcome(session, true);
            var transitions = 0;
            void Loaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode) { if (scene.path == BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene) transitions++; }
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded += Loaded;
            try
            {
                Finish(session);
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
                Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene));
                session.Advance(0.02f);
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
                yield return null;
                Assert.That(transitions, Is.EqualTo(1));
            }
            finally { SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= Loaded; }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator LevelOneRewindReopensTheActualLastTutorialHint()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false;
            if (session.Simulation.Units.Count == 0) session.Advance(.02f);
            var tutorial = session.GetComponent<LevelOneTutorial>();
            for (var frame = 0; frame < 300 && tutorial.CurrentStep != 1; frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(tutorial.CurrentStep, Is.EqualTo(1));
            var position = session.Simulation.Units[0].Position;
            var elapsed = session.Simulation.ElapsedTime;
            tutorial.Skip();
            Finish(session);
            Assert.That(session.RecoveryRequired, Is.True);
            yield return null;
            var recovery = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyLevelOneRecovery>();
            Assert.That(recovery, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(recovery.GetComponent<Canvas>().isRootCanvas, Is.True, "Recovery must not inherit the scaled HUD Canvas.");
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.RewindLevelOne(), Is.True);
            for (var frame = 0; frame < 300 && tutorial.CurrentStep != 1; frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(tutorial.CurrentStep, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(elapsed));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(position));
            Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2));
            tutorial.Skip();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.False);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator LevelOneRewindPreservesLostLivesAndExhaustionOnlyAllowsTitle()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false;
            var checkpointPosition = session.Simulation.Units[0].Position;
            var checkpointHp = session.Simulation.Units[0].Hp;
            session.CaptureTutorialCheckpoint(false);
            for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++)
            {
                Finish(session);
                Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2 - attempt));
                Assert.That(session.RecoveryRequired, Is.True);
                if (attempt == 2) break;
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.RewindLevelOne(), Is.True);
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units[0].Position, Is.EqualTo(checkpointPosition));
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units[0].Hp, Is.EqualTo(checkpointHp));
                Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2 - attempt));
            }
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.RewindLevelOne(), Is.False);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives, Is.True);
            BattleSceneFlow.ContinueToUpgrade();
            BattleSceneFlow.RestartCurrentLevel();
            yield return null; yield return null;
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene));
            var recovery = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyLevelOneRecovery>();
            Assert.That(recovery.GetComponentsInChildren<Button>().Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
            recovery.GetComponentInChildren<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives, Is.False);
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(3));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator DirectLevelTwoPlaySupportsThreeAttemptsAndEndsAfterThirdDefeat()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive, Is.True);
            var hud = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyBattleHud>();
            Assert.That(hud.LevelLabel, Is.Not.Null, "HUD must initialize even if the obsolete Edit Formation button is absent.");
            Assert.That(hud.LevelLabel.text, Is.EqualTo("LEVEL 2"));
            Assert.That(hud.LivesLabel, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(hud.PauseButton, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(hud.SquadButton(0), Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(hud.Session.GetComponent<BattleCombatControls>().ShowDebugPanel, Is.False);
            for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++)
            {
                var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
                Assert.That(session.DisplayedLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(2));
                Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(3 - attempt));
                ConfigureOutcome(session, false);
                Finish(session);
                if (attempt == 2)
                {
                    yield return null; yield return null;
                    Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.Zero);
                    Assert.That(session.RecoveryRequired, Is.True);
                    Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives, Is.True);
                    var recovery = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyLevelOneRecovery>();
                    Assert.That(recovery, Is.Not.Null);
                    Assert.That(recovery.GetComponentsInChildren<Button>().Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
                    BattleSceneFlow.ContinueToUpgrade();
                    BattleSceneFlow.RestartCurrentLevel();
                    yield return null;
                    Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene));
                    recovery.GetComponentInChildren<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
                    yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
                    break;
                }
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(2));
                Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
                var upgrade = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
                Assert.That(upgrade.LevelLabel.text, Does.StartWith("LEVEL 2").And.EndWith($"{2 - attempt}/3"));
                upgrade.Preparation.TryAssign(110, 4);
                upgrade.StartWave();
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
            }
        }

        [UnityTest]

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator UpcomingLevelPreviewLoadsActualMapWithoutStartingBattleThenClosesCleanly()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            ConfigureOutcome(session,true); Finish(session);
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            var ui = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            var gold = BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance;
            var lives = BattleSceneFlow.CurrentLevelLivesRemaining;
            var camera = Camera.main;
            var pose = camera.transform.position;
            var rotation = camera.transform.rotation;
            ui.PreviewNextLevelButton.onClick.Invoke();
            var preview = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyNextLevelPreview>();
            Assert.That(preview,Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(preview.GetComponent<Canvas>().isRootCanvas,Is.True);
            for(var frame=0;frame<600 && !preview.IsReady;frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(preview.IsReady,Is.True);
            Assert.That(preview.LoadedScene.path,Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene));
            var previewSession = preview.LoadedScene.GetRootGameObjects().SelectMany(root=>root.GetComponentsInChildren<BattleSession>(true)).Single();
            Assert.That(previewSession.Simulation,Is.Null,"Map preview must never start a battle.");
            var ranges = preview.LoadedScene.GetRootGameObjects().SelectMany(root=>root.GetComponentsInChildren<TowerRangeView>(true)).ToArray();
            Assert.That(ranges.Length,Is.EqualTo(previewSession.Towers.Count));
            for(var index=0;index<ranges.Length;index++)
            {
                var definition = previewSession.Towers[index].CaptureDefinition(index+1);
                Assert.That(ranges[index].DisplayBounds,Is.EqualTo(SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(definition.Position,definition.Area,definition.Range,definition.AttackWidth)));
            }
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path,Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber,Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance,Is.EqualTo(gold));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CurrentLevelLivesRemaining,Is.EqualTo(lives));
            preview.transform.Find("Close").GetComponent<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            for(var frame=0;frame<600 && preview!=null;frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(preview==null,Is.True);
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetSceneByPath(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene).isLoaded,Is.False);
            Assert.That(camera.transform.position,Is.EqualTo(pose));
            Assert.That(camera.transform.rotation,Is.EqualTo(rotation));
            ui.Preparation.TryAssign(110,4);
            ui.StartWave();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().Simulation,Is.Not.Null,"Preview cleanup must allow the real battle to start.");
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator ClosingMapPreviewDuringLoadingDoesNotLeakItsScene()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            yield return null;

            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path,Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance,Is.Zero);
            ui.OpenNextLevelPreview();
            preview = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyNextLevelPreview>();
            Assert.That(preview,Is.Not.Null,"A closed preview must be reopenable.");
            preview.Close();
            for(var frame=0;frame<600 && preview!=null;frame++) yield return null;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator ClaimCardBelowStorageCancelsForFreeThenBuysOneWarehouseMember()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForCaptainBattle();
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            ConfigureOutcome(session,true); Finish(session);
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            var ui = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            var membersBefore = ui.Preparation.Members.Count;
            var assetBefore = ui.Roster.CaptureMembers().Count;
            Assert.That(ui.ClaimCardButton.interactable,Is.True);
            Assert.That(((RectTransform)ui.ClaimCardButton.transform).anchoredPosition.y,Is.LessThan(-100));
            ui.ClaimCardButton.onClick.Invoke();
            var overlay = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyClaimCardOverlay>();
            Assert.That(overlay.GetComponent<Canvas>().isRootCanvas,Is.True);
            var offer = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.BeginOffer().ToArray();
            foreach(var choice in offer)
            {
                var introduction = overlay.transform.Find(choice.ToString()+"/Introduction");
                Assert.That(introduction.Find("Emblem"),Is.Not.Null);
                Assert.That(introduction.Find("Name").GetComponent<TMP_Text>().text,Is.EqualTo(LegacyUnitLooks.Name(ClaimCardShop.RoleOf(choice))));
                Assert.That(introduction.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>().Any(label=>label.text=="HP"),Is.True);
            }
            Assert.That(overlay.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>().Any(label=>label.text.Contains("Adds one unit")),Is.False);
            overlay.Close();
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance,Is.EqualTo(10));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.CardsTaken,Is.Zero);
            ui.OpenClaimCards();
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.BeginOffer(),Is.EqualTo(offer));
            Assert.That(offer,Is.EqualTo(new[] { ClaimCard.AddAttack, ClaimCard.AddTank, ClaimCard.AddHeal }));
            var unitCard = ClaimCard.AddTank;
            overlay = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyClaimCardOverlay>();
            overlay.transform.Find(unitCard.ToString()).GetComponent<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance,Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.CardsTaken,Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(ui.Preparation.Members.Count,Is.EqualTo(membersBefore+1));
            Assert.That(ui.Roster.CaptureMembers().Count,Is.EqualTo(assetBefore));
            Assert.That(ui.Preparation.Members.Last().Unit.Role,Is.EqualTo(ClaimCardShop.RoleOf(unitCard)));
            Assert.That(ui.ClaimCardButton.interactable,Is.False);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator WarehouseHasTwelveCellsLiveCountAndReturnsUnits()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            yield return null;
            var ui = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            var root = ui.Grid.transform.parent;
            Assert.That(root.Cast<Transform>().Count(child => child.name.StartsWith("Warehouse Cell ")), Is.EqualTo(12));
            Assert.That(ui.StartWaveButton.interactable, Is.False);
            ui.StartWave(); yield return null;
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene));
            var pointer = new PointerEventData(EventSystem.current) { position = new Vector2(-10000, -10000), dragging = true };
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 6; slot++)
            {
                pointer.pointerCurrentRaycast = new RaycastResult { gameObject = ui.Grid.transform.Find("Cell " + slot).gameObject };
                Assert.That(ui.TryPlaceMember(ui.Preparation.Members[slot].MemberId, pointer), Is.True);
            }
            var count = ui.Grid.transform.Find("Squad Count").GetComponent<TMP_Text>();
            Assert.That(count.text, Is.EqualTo("6/6"));
            pointer.pointerCurrentRaycast = new RaycastResult { gameObject = ui.Grid.transform.Find("Cell 8").gameObject };
            Assert.That(ui.TryPlaceMember(ui.Preparation.Members[6].MemberId, pointer), Is.False);
            Assert.That(count.text, Is.EqualTo("6/6"));
            ui.Grid.HandleBeginDrag(0, pointer);
            pointer.pointerCurrentRaycast = default;
            ui.Grid.HandleEndDrag(pointer);
            Assert.That(count.text, Is.EqualTo("5/6"));
            Assert.That(ui.Preparation.IsDeployed(ui.Preparation.Members[0].MemberId), Is.False);
            Assert.That(root.Find("Warehouse Cell 0").GetComponent<CanvasGroup>().alpha, Is.EqualTo(1));
        }

            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive, Is.False);
        }
    }

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\PlayMode\LegacyHudPlayModeTests.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class LegacyHudPlayModeTests
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        private LegacyBattleHud hud;
        [UnitySetUp]
        public IEnumerator Load()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/SquadBattlePrototype.unity");
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>(); session.enabled = false;
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("logEvents", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, false);
            session.StartBattle(); hud = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyBattleHud>();
            yield return null;
        }
        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Close()
        {
            var empty = SceneManager.CreateScene("HUD Test Cleanup"); SceneManager.SetActiveScene(empty);
            if (session != null) yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(session.gameObject.scene);
        }
        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator PauseButtonFreezesBattleAndPreservesSpeedAndEditorPause()
        {
            var speedRect = (RectTransform)hud.SpeedSlider.transform;
            var originalPosition = speedRect.anchoredPosition;
            var originalScale = speedRect.localScale;
            Assert.That(hud.PauseButton.transform.parent, Is.EqualTo(speedRect));
            hud.SetSpeedFromSlider(2);
            session.AdvancePlaybackFrame(0.02f);
            hud.PauseButton.onClick.Invoke();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            Assert.That(hud.PauseButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>().text, Is.EqualTo("Resume"));
            var elapsed = session.Simulation.ElapsedTime;
            session.AdvancePlaybackFrame(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(elapsed));
            hud.OpenSquadEditor();
            hud.TogglePause();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            Assert.That(hud.FormationEditor.ApplyAndResume(), Is.True);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            session.SetUnitInspectionActive(true);
            hud.PauseButton.onClick.Invoke();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.False);
            Assert.That(session.IsUnitInspectionActive, Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.PlaybackSpeed, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(hud.PauseButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>().text, Is.EqualTo("Pause"));
            Assert.That(speedRect.anchoredPosition, Is.EqualTo(originalPosition));
            Assert.That(speedRect.localScale, Is.EqualTo(originalScale));
            session.StartBattle();
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.IsPaused, Is.False);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(hud.PauseButton.interactable, Is.True);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator HudMatchesLegacyLabelsAndSpeedSliderRunsFixedTicksWithoutGlobalTimeScale()
        {
            Assert.That(hud.LevelLabel.text, Is.EqualTo("LEVEL 1"));
            Assert.That(hud.LivesLabel.text, Is.EqualTo(": 3/3"));
            Assert.That(hud.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>(true).Any(label => label.text.Contains("$")), Is.False);
            Assert.That(session.GetComponent<BattleCombatControls>().ShowDebugPanel, Is.False);
            var globalScale = Time.timeScale;
            hud.SpeedSlider.value = 2;
            Assert.That(session.PlaybackSpeed, Is.EqualTo(3));
            session.AdvancePlaybackFrame(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(0.048).Within(0.00001));
            hud.SpeedSlider.value = 1;
            session.AdvancePlaybackFrame(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(0.080).Within(0.00001));
            hud.SpeedSlider.value = 0;
            session.AdvancePlaybackFrame(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(0.096).Within(0.00001));
            session.Simulation.SetPaused(true); session.AdvancePlaybackFrame(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.ElapsedTime, Is.EqualTo(0.096).Within(0.00001));
            Assert.That(Time.timeScale, Is.EqualTo(globalScale));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => session.SetPlaybackSpeed(4));
            yield return null;
        }
        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator LegacyDragDraftIsAppliedAsASlotCommandWithoutResettingMembers()
        {
            session.Advance(0.02f);
            var simulation = session.Simulation;
            var before = simulation.Units.Select(u => (u.MemberId, u.Hp, u.AttackCooldown, u.RouteProgress)).ToArray();
            var healer = simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(4); var tank = simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(1);
            hud.EditButton.onClick.Invoke();
            Assert.That(simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            var editor = hud.FormationEditor; var grid = editor.Grid;
            Canvas.ForceUpdateCanvases();
            var start = grid.transform.Find("Cell 4"); var end = grid.transform.Find("Cell 1");
            var pointer = new PointerEventData(EventSystem.current)
            { position = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(null, start.position), dragging = true };
            grid.HandleBeginDrag(4, pointer);
            pointer.position = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(null, end.position);
            grid.HandleDrag(pointer); grid.HandleEndDrag(pointer);
            Assert.That(grid.CopySourceSlots()[1], Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(4), Is.SameAs(healer), "Dragging changes only the draft.");
            Assert.That(editor.ApplyAndResume(), Is.True);
            Assert.That(simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(1), Is.SameAs(healer));
            Assert.That(simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(4), Is.SameAs(tank));
            Assert.That(simulation.Units.Select(u => (u.MemberId, u.Hp, u.AttackCooldown, u.RouteProgress)), Is.EqualTo(before));
            Assert.That(simulation.IsPaused, Is.False);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(hud.FormationEditor == null, Is.True);
        }
        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator RemovingHudWhileEditingDoesNotMutateOrResumeTheSimulation()
        {
            session.Advance(0.02f); hud.OpenSquadEditor();
            var simulation = session.Simulation;
            var before = simulation.Units.Select(u => (u.MemberId, u.Hp, u.Position, u.SlotId)).ToArray();
            hud.enabled = false;
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            Assert.That(simulation.Units.Select(u => (u.MemberId, u.Hp, u.Position, u.SlotId)), Is.EqualTo(before));
            Assert.That(hud.GetComponent<Canvas>().enabled, Is.False);
            hud.enabled = true;
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(simulation.IsPaused, Is.True);
            Assert.That(hud.GetComponent<Canvas>().enabled, Is.True);
            Assert.That(hud.LivesLabel.text, Is.EqualTo(": 3/3"));
        }
        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator LivesTrackResolvedFailuresOnceAndHudRebindsAfterRestart()
        {
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
            {
                typeof(BattleTower).GetField("damage", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(tower, 1000f);
                typeof(BattleTower).GetField("range", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(tower, 1000f);
                typeof(BattleTower).GetField("attackInterval", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(tower, 0.02f);
            }
            for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++)
            {
                session.StartBattle();
                for (var tick = 0; tick < 3000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; tick++) session.Advance(0.02f);
                Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Defeat));
                Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2 - attempt));
                session.Advance(0.02f);
                Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(2 - attempt));
            }
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(hud.LivesLabel.text, Is.EqualTo(": 0/3"));
            session.StartBattle();
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(hud.GetComponentsInChildren<TMP_Text>(true).Any(label => label.text.Contains("$")), Is.False);
            Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.Zero);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\PlayMode\MacroBattlePlayModeTests.cs ===
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class MacroBattlePlayModeTests
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        [UnitySetUp]
        public IEnumerator Load()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/GridBattleMacroPrototype.unity");
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false;
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("logEvents", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, false);
            session.StartBattle();
        }
        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Close()
        {
            var empty = SceneManager.CreateScene("Macro Test Cleanup");
            SceneManager.SetActiveScene(empty);
            yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(session.gameObject.scene);
        }
        private void Finish()
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < 5000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator NineIndependentViewsSpawnAndCombatCompletesWithoutCloningStaticMap()
        {
            var staticIds = Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleCell>(FindObjectsSortMode.InstanceID).Select(cell => cell.GetInstanceID()).ToArray();
            var meshIds = session.Grid.transform.parent.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>().Select(mesh => mesh.GetInstanceID()).ToArray();
            session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.SpawnedCount, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units.Select(unit => unit.FormationSlot).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(9));
            var presentation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattlePresentation>();
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.EqualTo(9));
            foreach (var unit in session.Simulation.Units)
            {
                Assert.That(presentation.TryGetView(unit.Id, out var view), Is.True);
                view.Present(1);
                Assert.That(view.transform.position, Is.EqualTo(unit.Position));
            }
            var damaged = 0;
            session.Simulation.UnitDamaged += _ => damaged++;
            Finish();
            Assert.That(damaged, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.KilledCount, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.KilledCount + session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleCell>(FindObjectsSortMode.InstanceID).Select(cell => cell.GetInstanceID()), Is.EqualTo(staticIds));
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(session.Grid.transform.parent.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>().Select(mesh => mesh.GetInstanceID()), Is.EqualTo(meshIds));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator RemovingPresentationKeepsMacroBattleOutcomeAndOccupancy()
        {
            Finish();
            var expected = (session.Simulation.Outcome, session.Simulation.KilledCount, session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount);
            Object.Destroy(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattlePresentation>().gameObject);
            Object.Destroy(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleDebugGizmos>());
            yield return null;
            session.StartBattle();
            Finish();
            Assert.That((session.Simulation.Outcome, session.Simulation.KilledCount, session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount), Is.EqualTo(expected));
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\PlayMode\MinimalPresentationTests.cs ===
using System.Collections;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class MinimalPresentationTests
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        private BattlePresentation presentation;
        [UnitySetUp]
        public IEnumerator Load()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/GridBattlePrototype.unity");
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false;
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("logEvents", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, false);
            presentation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattlePresentation>();
            session.StartBattle();
        }
        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Unload()
        {
            var empty = SceneManager.CreateScene("Minimal Presentation Cleanup");
            SceneManager.SetActiveScene(empty);
            yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(session.gameObject.scene);
        }
        private (BattleOutcome outcome, int killed, int goals) Finish()
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < 10000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
            return (session.Simulation.Outcome, session.Simulation.KilledCount, session.Simulation.ReachedGoalCount);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TowerRangesMatchSimulationAndCanBeHiddenWithoutChangingCombat()
        {
            yield return null;
            var ranges = Object.FindObjectsByType<TowerRangeView>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
            Assert.That(ranges.Length, Is.EqualTo(session.Simulation.Towers.Count));
            foreach (var range in ranges)
            {
                var tower = session.Simulation.Towers.Single(t => t.Id == range.TowerId);
                Assert.That(range.DisplayBounds, Is.EqualTo(SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(tower.Position,
                    tower.Definition.Area, tower.Definition.Range, tower.Definition.AttackWidth)));
                Assert.That(range.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().enabled, Is.True);
                Assert.That(range.GetComponentInChildren<LineRenderer>().positionCount, Is.EqualTo(4));
                Assert.That(range.GetComponentInChildren<Collider>(), Is.Null);
            }
            var expected = Finish();
            session.StartBattle();
            presentation.ShowTowerRanges = false;
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<TowerRangeView>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)
                .All(range => !range.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().enabled && !range.GetComponentInChildren<LineRenderer>().enabled), Is.True);
            Assert.That(Finish(), Is.EqualTo(expected));
            presentation.ShowTowerRanges = true;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator SquareViewsReadStateAndInterpolateWithoutChangingSimulation()
        {
            session.Advance(0.02f);
            var unit = session.Simulation.Units[0];
            Assert.That(presentation.TryGetView(unit.Id, out var view), Is.True);
            var firstPosition = unit.Position;
            var hp = unit.Hp;
            session.Advance(0.02f);
            var currentPosition = unit.Position;
            view.Present(0.5f);
            Assert.That(Vector3.Distance(view.transform.position, Vector3.Lerp(firstPosition, currentPosition, 0.5f)), Is.LessThan(0.0001f));
            Assert.That(unit.Position, Is.EqualTo(currentPosition));
            Assert.That(unit.Hp, Is.EqualTo(hp));
            Assert.That(view.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().enabled, Is.True);
            Assert.That(view.GetComponentInChildren<Collider>(), Is.Null);
            view.transform.position = Vector3.one * 1000;
            view.Present(1);
            Assert.That(view.transform.position, Is.EqualTo(unit.Position));
            yield return null;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator DisablingAndRemovingPresentationPreservesBattleResult()
        {
            var expected = Finish();
            presentation.enabled = false;
            session.StartBattle();
            Assert.That(Finish(), Is.EqualTo(expected));
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.Zero);
            presentation.enabled = true;
            session.StartBattle();
            session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Object.Destroy(presentation.gameObject);
            Object.Destroy(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleDebugGizmos>());
            foreach (var renderer in Object.FindObjectsByType<MeshRenderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (renderer != null) Object.Destroy(renderer.gameObject);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<MeshRenderer>(FindObjectsSortMode.None), Is.Empty);
            session.StartBattle();
            Assert.That(Finish(), Is.EqualTo(expected));
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator ViewsMatchAliveUnitsAfterDeathsGoalsLateBindingAndRestart()
        {
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("unitCount", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, 32);
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("spawnInterval", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, 0.15f);
            typeof(BattleSession).GetField("goalLives", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic).SetValue(session, 100);
            session.StartBattle();
            presentation.enabled = false;
            for (var i = 0; i < 100; i++) session.Advance(0.02f);
            presentation.enabled = true;
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.EqualTo(session.Simulation.AliveCount));
            var deaths = 0;
            var goals = 0;
            session.Simulation.UnitDied += _ => deaths++;
            session.Simulation.UnitReachedGoal += _ => goals++;
            for (var i = 0; i < 10000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++)
            {
                session.Advance(0.02f);
                Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.EqualTo(session.Simulation.AliveCount));
            }
            Assert.That(deaths, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(goals, Is.GreaterThan(0));
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.Zero);
            session.StartBattle();
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.Zero);
            session.Advance(0.02f);
            Assert.That(presentation.ViewCount, Is.EqualTo(1));
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<UnitView>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator GameCameraSeesStaticMeshesAndUnitsWithoutDebugGizmos()
        {
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleDebugGizmos>().enabled = false;
            for (var i = 0; i < 50; i++) session.Advance(0.02f);
            foreach (var view in Object.FindObjectsByType<UnitView>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)) view.Present(1);
            var camera = Camera.main;
            var frustum = GeometryUtility.CalculateFrustumPlanes(camera);
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.All(cell => GeometryUtility.TestPlanesAABB(frustum, cell.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().bounds)), Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Towers.All(tower => GeometryUtility.TestPlanesAABB(frustum, tower.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().bounds)), Is.True);
            Assert.That(Object.FindObjectsByType<UnitView>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).Any(view =>
                GeometryUtility.TestPlanesAABB(frustum, view.GetComponentInChildren<MeshRenderer>().bounds)), Is.True);
            yield return null;
        }
    }
}
